Through a critical review of scholarly classifications--auxiliaries (Smeets, 2008), modal prefixes (Longkon, 2011), and preverbal particles/complex verb stems (Zúñiga, 2006)--we demonstrate the limitations of existing frameworks. A diachronic corpus analysis spanning four centuries (1606-present) reveals that these elements exhibit three distinct profiles: stable V1 compounds (kim, shinge), volatile V1 rates reflecting orthographic shift (pepi, wuno), and a true particle (kalli). The discovery of V2 attestations for kim and kupa confirms their status as full lexical verbs. We propose a prosodic-orthographic hypothesis: apparent "variable binding" results from the fossilization of prosodic pauses transcribed by early missionaries as spaces, a convention later reanalysed by speakers as syntactic boundaries. The evidence supports Zúñiga's radical concatenation as the correct grammatical model, with implications for the study of languages with no pre-contact written tradition.

</details>


### [167] [A-SR: Self-Evolving Agentic LLMs for Symbolic Regression via Hierarchical Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04872)
*Wenxiao Zhao,Dong Liu,Kaiyi Xu,Feng Liu,Zhen Zhao,Fei Ben,Shu Wang,Wenhao Li,Yingnian Wu,Fenghua Ling,Haobo Li,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A-SR是一个自进化的智能体框架，用于符号回归（从数据中发现闭式方程）。它将传统LLM引导方法中"表达式编辑"的控制单元转变为"角色条件证据视图"，通过协调协议路由、在线评估器-奖励角色策略和状态路由过程记忆来协调公式发现，并在两个时间尺度上自我进化（运行内自适应和跨运行蒸馏），在Llama3.1-8B和Qwen3-4B上分别将Acc@0.01提升至48.30%和38.29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的符号回归方法存在根本性缺陷：它们依赖统一提议循环，将异构的搜索失败（如表达式不合法、不准确、过拟合等）压缩成单一标量分数和单一提示词，导致不同性质的失败信号无法被差异化利用，制约了搜索效率与最终发现质量。

Method: A-SR框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 协调协议路由，将不同类型的搜索证据分发给不同角色代理；(2) 在线评估器-奖励角色策略，评估器反馈同时刻画可靠性与生产力，并更新角色级别的效用；(3) 状态路由过程记忆，将精英基序（elite motifs）、失败轨迹和有效性诊断分别路由至不同代理。框架在两个时间尺度上自进化：运行内通过适应搜索过程（不更新LLM参数），跨运行则将轨迹蒸馏为开源LLM中的角色条件提议先验（A-SR-LoRA）。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench的四个LSR-Synth科学领域上，A-SR将Acc@0.01从基线的25.79%提升到48.30%（Llama3.1-8B）；A-SR-LoRA将Qwen3-4B的对应结果从24.58%提升到38.29%。在四个真实世界科学发现任务上，A-SR在8个报告的归一化均方误差指标中的7个上取得了最佳分布内或分布外表现。

Conclusion: A-SR通过将搜索失败从"统一标量"重构为"角色条件证据视图"，显著提升了符号回归的发现能力和鲁棒性。其自进化机制（运行内自适应与跨运行蒸馏）为不依赖大规模参数更新的持续搜索改进提供了可扩展范式，且在小模型（4B-8B）上即可超越基线，展示了高效性与实用性。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to discover closed-form equations from data, but existing LLM-guided methods often rely on a unified proposal loop that compresses heterogeneous search failures into a scalar score and a single prompt. We propose A-SR, a self-evolving agentic framework that shifts the control unit from expression edits to role-conditioned evidence views. A-SR coordinates formula discovery through routing among coordination protocols, an online evaluator-reward role policy, and state-routed process memory. During search, evaluator feedback characterizes reliability and productivity, updates role-level utilities, and routes elite motifs, failure traces, and validity diagnostics to different agents. The framework self-evolves at two timescales: within a run, it adapts the search process without updating LLM parameters; across runs, recorded trajectories can be distilled into open-source LLMs as role-conditioned proposal priors. Averaged over the four LSR-Synth scientific domains in LLM-SRBench, A-SR improves Acc@0.01 over baselines from 25.79% to 48.30% with Llama3.1-8B, while A-SR-LoRA improves the corresponding Qwen3-4B result from 24.58% to 38.29%. On four real-world scientific discovery tasks, A-SR obtains the best in-distribution or out-of-distribution normalized mean squared error on 7 of 8 reported metrics.

</details>


### [168] [Evaluation Pitfalls and Sparsity Limitations in LLM-based Confidence Estimates for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04899)
*Elena Merdjanovska,Omar Zaidan,Andreas Rücklé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM置信度估计（如口头化/verbalization）会产生极其稀疏的输出（如Qwen3-32B在SST-2上仅输出8个唯一置信度值，超过一半为95%）。这种稀疏性严重影响了AUARC评估指标（插值方式会翻转模型排名），作者主张标准化stepwise插值，并提出verbalization logprobs方法（利用token概率加权数字）来解决稀疏性，在无额外推理成本下AUARC提升2.3个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM分类任务中置信度估计至关重要，但现有的口头化（verbalization）方法输出置信度值极其稀疏，这不仅限制了实际应用价值，还会歪曲评估结果——AUARC指标中不同插值方式会导致模型排名完全颠倒。作者旨在解决稀疏性问题并建立公平的评估标准。

Method: 本文提出了两项核心内容：(1) 倡导在评估AUARC时标准化使用stepwise插值以确保公平比较；(2) 提出verbalization logprobs方法，即用token概率加权口头化的数字，以此缓解输出稀疏性问题。

Result: 实验覆盖4个数据集和2个LLM，发现一致性采样（consistency sampling）在stepwise与linear插值下从最佳变为最差，凸显评估标准的紧迫性。在标准的stepwise插值下，verbalization logprobs比vanilla verbalization提升2.3个AUARC点，且不增加额外推理成本。

Conclusion: LLM置信度输出的稀疏性是一个被忽视的严重问题，既影响实际可用性又扭曲评估结果。通过标准化stepwise插值并采用verbalization logprobs，可以在不增加计算开销的情况下显著提升置信度估计质量，为LLM置信度评估提供了更公平和更实用的方案。

Abstract: Confidence estimation is essential when LLMs are used for classification, indicating when predictions can be trusted. However, common approaches such as verbalization produce extremely sparse outputs. For instance, Qwen3-32B verbalizes only eight unique confidence values on SST-2, with over half being exactly 95%, a pattern we observe consistently across four datasets and two LLMs. Besides limiting practical utility, we show that this sparsity critically affects evaluation: the choice of interpolation in area under the accuracy-rejection curve (AUARC) dramatically alters rankings, with consistency sampling dropping from best to worst under stepwise versus linear interpolation. We advocate for standardizing stepwise interpolation for a fairer comparison. Under such a fair evaluation, we find that weighting verbalized digits by token probabilities, a method we term verbalization logprobs, addresses sparsity and achieves the best AUARC (+2.3 points over vanilla verbalization) without incurring additional inference cost.

</details>


### [169] [Strengthening Target-Language Features: SAE-Based Steering for Multilingual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04904)
*Hongsheng Wang,Phlipp Koehn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种推理时的多语言模型引导方法，利用预训练的稀疏自编码器识别并增强目标语言相关特征，无需额外训练即可提升多语言性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在不同语言上表现差异显著，现有的适配方法通常需要参数更新和大量多语言训练数据，成本高昂且不灵活。

Method: 利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE），通过多语言平行句比较不同语言的SAE激活模式，选出与目标语言相关的少量层特定特征，将这些特征解码为引导信号注入模型的隐藏状态中，全程无需额外训练。

Result: 在Gemma-3-12B-it上进行实验，XCOPA准确率平均提升10.9个百分点，XNLI提升5.3个百分点，MGSM提升1.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了一种无需参数更新和大量训练数据的推理时多语言引导策略是有效的，为多语言模型性能提升提供了轻量级的新思路。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models exhibit substantial performance differences across languages, while existing adaptation methods often require parameter updates and considerable multilingual training data. We propose an inference-time multilingual steering method that uses pretrained sparse autoencoders to identify and strengthen target-language-related features. Using multilingual parallel sentences, we compare SAE activations across languages and select a small number of layer-specific features associated with each target language. These features are decoded into steering signals and injected into the model's hidden states without additional training. Experiments with Gemma-3-12B-it show average accuracy improvements of 10.9 percentage points on XCOPA, 5.3 points on XNLI, and 1.9 points on MGSM.

</details>


### [170] [Does Out-of-Sight Equal Out-of-Mind in CoT Monitorability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04928)
*Pedro Ferreira,Wilker Aziz,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了潜在思维链（Latent CoT）推理模式下大语言模型的可监控性问题，通过基于提示的干预实验发现：监控能力更多取决于任务属性和模型内部访问级别，而非推理模式本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式思维链（CoT）通过可读的推理轨迹支持模型监控，但潜在CoT用少量连续状态替代显式token，降低了推理成本却移除了可读轨迹。虽然可以通过探测激活或将其言语化来进行替代监控，但这些替代方式保留了多少可监控性尚不清楚，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 采用基于提示的干预设置，将"对提示线索的依赖"（hint-reliance）作为监控目标，作为模型利用偏置输入线索行为的代理。在数学推理和问答任务上，对不同推理模式（显式CoT、弱监督和强监督潜在CoT）下的监控方法进行比较。

Result: 研究发现，在该实验设置下，可监控性更多地取决于任务属性（如正确答案是否约束了支撑推理）以及对模型内部状态的访问级别，而非推理模式本身。

Conclusion: 潜在CoT推理并不固有地降低可监控性；监控能力由任务和访问层面的因素主导，这对设计既高效又可监控的推理模型具有重要启示。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning offers a window into the decision-making of large language models (LLMs), which can be monitored for target behaviors by reading the reasoning trace, motivating work on CoT monitorability. Latent CoT approaches, however, replace the explicit tokens with a small number of continuous states, lowering inference costs but removing the readable trace this monitoring relies on. Monitoring then requires alternative access to the model, such as probing its activations or verbalizing the latent states back into text, but how much monitorability these alternatives preserve is unclear. We study this question with a hint-based intervention setup, a proxy for behaviors where models exploit biasing input cues, e.g., an inadvertently leaked answer or a belief stated by the user, without acknowledging them. Taking hint-reliance as the monitorability target, we compare monitors across reasoning modes, from explicit CoT to weakly- and strongly-supervised latent CoT, on math reasoning and question answering. We find that, in this setup, monitorability depends more on properties of the task (such as whether the correct answer constrains the supporting reasoning) and the level of access to model internals than on the reasoning mode.

</details>


### [171] [State2State: Environment-Derived Mid-Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04934)
*Xuanyu Lei,Yiqi Zhu,Chenliang Li,Kaiming Liu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Ya-Qin Zhang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出State2State，一种无需外部任务指定的环境学习范式，通过将探索到的环境状态转化为训练目标（让智能体到达指定目标状态），以规则匹配验证成功，为LLM智能体提供可扩展、可验证的训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体训练依赖外部指定任务和人工设计的验证信号，无论是监督微调还是在线强化学习，都受限于外部指定任务和人工验证器的瓶颈，限制了智能体训练的规模化和多样性。作者希望摆脱对外部任务的依赖，探索纯粹通过环境交互来获取能力的训练范式。

Method: State2State是一种环境派生的中训练（mid-training）方法。核心思想是：智能体先在环境中探索，将探索到的环境状态转换成训练目标（挑战智能体到达指定目标状态）；通过规则匹配来验证智能体是否成功到达目标状态，从而提供无需专家监督和人工任务设计的可扩展、可验证的训练目标。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld两个基准上的实验表明：State2State作为独立的环境学习阶段，在大多数设置下能提升智能体性能；作为下游强化学习的初始化，能进一步提升最终性能和训练效率，并且展现出跨环境泛化的有力证据。

Conclusion: State2State验证了无需外部任务和监督信号、仅通过环境交互即可进行有效智能体训练的可行性。这种环境学习范式不仅能独立提升智能体性能，还能作为下游RL的良好初始化，并具备跨环境泛化能力。

Abstract: Training LLM agents commonly relies on supervised fine-tuning from expert trajectories or online reinforcement learning over human-specified tasks with handcrafted verifiers. Though effective, both remain bottlenecked by externally specified tasks and supervision signals, limiting the scalability and diversity of agent training. We study an environment learning paradigm in which agents acquire interaction and manipulation capabilities solely through environment interaction, without externally specified tasks. We propose State2State, an environment-derived mid-training method that converts explored environment states into training objectives, challenging agents to reach a specified target state. By deriving tasks from environment exploration and verifying success through rule-based state matching, State2State provides scalable and verifiable training objectives without expert supervision or manual task design. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that State2State improves agent performance as a standalone environment-learning stage in most settings. As initialization for downstream RL, it further improves final performance and learning efficiency, with promising evidence of cross-environment generalization.

</details>


### [172] [Reading Between the Frames: Interpreting Implicit and Non-literal Meaning in Social Media Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04939)
*Yang Wang,Yanan Ma,Yiqi Liu,Zi Yan Chang,Chi-Li Chen,Chia-Yi Hsiao,Tyler Loakman,Aline Villavicencio,Chenghao Xiao,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DrivelHub+基准，用于评估视频-语言模型是否能推理社交媒体视频中隐含的、非线性的、修辞层叠的语用意义（如幽默、反讽、讽刺），而非仅进行表面识别或描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体视频往往通过多模态线索与文化语境的交互传达超出表面内容的隐含义（如幽默、反讽、讽刺），这对视频-语言模型构成挑战。现有视频理解基准多聚焦于识别或描述，缺乏对隐含语用推理能力的评测，需要新的基准来衡量模型从"看到什么"到"意味着什么"的推理鸿沟。

Method: 构建包含1,000个社交媒体视频的基准DrivelHub+，每个视频配有由人工撰写的隐含叙述解释标注。从两个角度评测视频-语言模型：(1) 解释任务——模型需要用自然语言解释视频的语用理解；(2) 表示任务——采用"推理即检索"（reasoning-as-retrieval）范式，测试模型表征在视频-文本和文本-视频两个方向上将视频与其对应的隐含叙述对齐的能力。

Result: 该基准提供了诊断性设置，用于测量多模态感知与语用理解之间的差距，揭示当前模型在多大程度上能超越表面描述、推断隐含意义。

Conclusion: DrivelHub+明确提出了一个核心问题：当前的视频-语言模型能否从描述"所展示的内容"推进到推断"所意味的内容"，为弥合多模态感知与语用理解之间的鸿沟提供了评测框架。

Abstract: Social media videos often communicate meanings that go beyond their visible actions, captions, or speech. A mundane clip may become humorous, ironic, or satire only through the interaction of multimodal cues and cultural context, making such content a difficult test case for video-language models. In this paper, we introduce \textit{DrivelHub+}, a benchmark for evaluating whether models can infer the implicit, non-linear, and rhetorically layered meanings of social media videos that appear nonsensical on the surface but convey deliberate pragmatic meanings. DrivelHub+ consists of 1,000 videos collected from social media, each annotated with a human-written implicit narrative explanation. Unlike conventional video understanding tasks focused on recognition or description, we present a benchmark that targets contextual multimodal reasoning. We evaluate current video-language models from two perspectives: explanation, where models must explain the pragmatic comprehension of a video in natural language; and representation, where we adapt reasoning-as-retrieval to test whether model representations align videos with their corresponding implicit narratives in both video-to-text and text-to-video retrieval. Our benchmark provides a diagnostic setting for measuring the gap between multimodal perception and pragmatic comprehension, asking whether current models can move beyond describing what is shown to inferring what is meant.

</details>


### [173] [Protoreasoning in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04980)
*Eduardo Valle,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"原型推理"（protoreasoning）——一种极简的思维链形式，使仅约100万参数的小型transformer能进行逐步推理，从而在可负担的计算规模下研究推理的通用性。通过Dyck语言任务，证明原型推理能显著缩小分布外泛化差距，且消融实验确认是推理轨迹的内容而非额外token本身带来了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型展现出惊人的逐步推理能力，但我们尚不清楚其通用性——即LLM何时能学到真正通用的算法而非"启发式工具包"。这类问题难以在计算密集、数据不透明的前沿模型上研究，因此需要更小、更可控的实验平台。

Method: 在远低于自然语言能力阈值的模型规模（约100万参数）下，定义基于Dyck语言（正确嵌套括号的句子）的推理友好任务，并在小型transformer上采用一种简单的思维链形式——原型推理轨迹来训练和评估。

Result: 原型推理轨迹显著缩小了分布外泛化差距；消融实验（去除轨迹内容而仅保留额外token）证实性能提升源于推理轨迹的实际内容而非额外token数量的表面效应。

Conclusion: 即使在极小规模模型上，思维链式的中间推理步骤也能带来实质性的泛化收益，这为在可控环境下深入研究LLM推理的通用性机制提供了可行的实验范式，并为探索推理何时真正成为"通用算法"奠定了小型化研究基础。

Abstract: We show that tiny transformers can profitably employ a simple form of Chain of Thought, which we call protoreasoning, allowing us to study step-by-step reasoning on ~1M-parameter models and opening up opportunities for much more detailed experimentation and analysis than is feasible for larger models. Current Large Language Models exhibit impressive step-by-step reasoning, but we have yet to understand its generality, i.e., when and how LLMs learn genuinely general algorithms rather than "bags of heuristics." Such questions are hard to settle on compute-intensive frontier models trained on opaque data. To work at model scales far below the threshold for natural-language competence, we define reasoning-friendly tasks on Dyck languages (sentences of correctly nested brackets). We find that protoreasoning traces substantially close the out-of-distribution generalization gap, and ablations confirm that the trace's content, not merely its extra tokens, drives the gain.

</details>


### [174] [DelusionEval: Measuring Delusion-Linked Behaviors in AI Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05004)
*Jared Moore,Andrea Mock,Yifan Mai,Jacy Reese Anthis,Ryan Louie,William Agnew,Ashish Mehta,Kevin Klyman,Percy Liang,Nick Haber,Eric Lin,Desmond C. Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DelusionEval评估协议，利用589段真实对话历史（来自18位曾经历幻觉和心理伤害的用户，共12,591条消息）测试LLM是否表现出助长用户幻觉的行为。研究发现模型大小、发布日期或测试时推理能力与幻觉相关行为并无可靠相关性，但延长上下文会显著增加幻觉相关行为的发生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康专业人士对LLM交互可能造成心理伤害（如"妄想螺旋"）表示担忧，但缺乏基于真实用户心理伤害案例的评估方法。随着LLM聊天机器人被广泛使用，迫切需要构建扎根于真实世界心理伤害事件的安全评估体系。

Method: 开发DelusionEval评估协议，用589个来自18名经历过幻觉和心理伤害用户的对话历史（12,591条消息）对模型进行提示测试，评估其表现出的与助长用户幻觉相关的行为倾向，并系统分析模型大小、发布时间、测试时推理及上下文长度等因素的影响。

Result: 模型表现出幻觉相关行为的倾向与模型大小、发布日期或测试时推理能力无可靠相关性；延长上下文会显著提高幻觉相关行为率（如用户表达自杀意念时未能劝阻自伤的比例从30.0%升至41.1%，当追加350条消息时）；所有模型家族（GPT、Claude等）均表现出可观的幻觉相关行为率，且家族内更新、更大或更高推理能力的模型并非在所有行为类别中一律更优。

Conclusion: 研究结果对LLM潜在心理影响提出了严峻关切，强调上下文在LLM安全评估中的关键作用，并呼吁对真实世界人机交互进行更严谨的研究。

Abstract: Mental health professionals have raised concerns about risks of psychological harm from interaction with large language models (LLMs), including "delusional spirals" in which concerning human and LLM behaviors reinforce each other over time. With growing public use of LLM-powered chatbots, there is an urgent need to build evaluations grounded in real-world episodes of psychological harm experienced by users. We developed DelusionEval, an evaluation protocol that tests a model's tendencies to exhibit behaviors linked to promoting user delusions. We prompt each model with 589 unique conversation histories from 18 participants, comprising 12,591 messages from users who experienced delusions and psychological harm. We find that the tendency of an evaluated LLM to exhibit delusion-linked behavior does not reliably correlate with model size, release date, or the presence of test-time reasoning. However, extending the context of prior messages substantially increases rates of delusion-linked behaviors, providing evidence for the importance of context in LLM safety evaluation. For example, the rate of failing to discourage self-harm when the user expresses suicidal ideation increases from 30.0% to 41.1% when an additional 350 messages are prepended to the conversation history. All model families (e.g., GPT, Claude) exhibit substantial rates of delusion-linked behaviors. Within families, later, larger, or higher-reasoning models are not uniformly better across all behavior categories. Our results raise concerns regarding the potential psychological impact of LLMs and the need for more rigorous studies of real-world human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [175] [OneDayAgent: Towards a Long-Horizon Harness for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05013)
*Jingsheng Zheng,Xinyuan Fang,Jintian Zhang,Zhengke Gui,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OneDayAgent 是一个面向开放域长期任务的长时程智能体调度框架，将开放式请求转化为受管理的执行流程：将任务分解为有界子任务、在上下文压力下维护执行记忆、验证并修复最终交付物。在 AgentIF-OneDay 的 104 个任务上以 GLM-5.2 为后端达到 0.821 的综合得分，刷新了当前最优成绩；同一框架无需调参即可在来自三个模型家族的五种后端 LLM 上运行，证明其跨后端具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 智能体在面对跨越工作、学习和生活的开放式日常请求时，面临长时程（long-horizon）、跨环境（cross-environment）和多模态（multimodal）的挑战，需要在大量步骤中保持目标和约束，同时导航异构的工具和附件。已有工作仅分别解决了目标漂移、状态丢失、上下文溢出等单一失效模式，但对于单一调度框架能否联合管理这些失效模式并在不同后端上保持有效，研究尚不充分。

Method: OneDayAgent 作为一个长时程调度框架（long-horizon harness），将开放式请求转化为受管理的执行流程，核心组件包括：(1) 将任务分解为有界子任务（bounded subtasks）；(2) 在上下文压力下维护执行记忆（execution memory）；(3) 对最终交付物进行验证与修复（verification and repair）。该框架独立于底层 LLM，可直接适配不同后端模型。

Result: 在 AgentIF-OneDay 基准的 104 个任务上，OneDayAgent 以 GLM-5.2 为后端取得了 0.821 的综合得分，达到新的最优水平（state of the art）。此外，同一调度框架无需任何调参即可运行在来自三个模型家族的五个后端 LLM 上，说明框架具有跨后端的泛化能力，且不同模型在同一工作流下会呈现不同的执行风格。

Conclusion: OneDayAgent 证明了单一调度框架能够联合管理目标漂移、状态丢失和上下文溢出等失效模式，并在多个后端 LLM 上保持一致的有效性，无需针对特定模型进行调优。然而，不同模型在同一工作流下产生的执行风格差异表明，框架层面仍需考虑模型特性的适配。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to open-ended everyday requests that span work, study, and life. These tasks are long-horizon, cross-environment, and multimodal, forcing the agent to preserve goals and constraints across many steps while navigating heterogeneous tools and attachments. While prior work has addressed individual failure modes such as goals drift, states loss, and context overflow, whether a single harness can manage them jointly and remain effective across backends has received less study. We present OneDayAgent, a long-horizon harness for autonomous agents. OneDayAgent turns an open-ended request into a managed execution process that decomposes tasks into bounded subtasks, maintains execution memory under context pressure, and verifies and repairs the final deliverable. We evaluate OneDayAgent on AgentIF-OneDay across 104 tasks. With the GLM-5.2 backend, OneDayAgent sets a new state of the art with an overall score of 0.821. The same harness runs across five backend LLMs from three model families, indicating the harness generalizes across backends without tuning, even as different models induce distinct execution styles under the same workflow.

</details>


### [176] [Language Models Generalize to Human-like Word Order Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05028)
*Amanda Popadich,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验发现，语言模型在训练语料中完全移除多修饰语名词短语后，仍能从贫乏输入中泛化出与人类一致的域同态修饰语排序偏好，且这种偏好不能由词点互信息（PMI）解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言习得中的核心问题在于：语言偏见能否仅从一般学习机制作用于欠定性输入中涌现？人工智能语言学习（ALL）研究已证明人类学习者能从证据中可靠泛化，包括偏好域同态名词短语修饰语排序。作者希望探究语言模型是否在类似条件下展现同样的偏见。

Method: 构建受控学习环境：训练语料中删除所有包含多个修饰语的名词短语（消除修饰语排序的直接证据），然后在三种不同规模的模型上评估其对多修饰语句子的表现。同时使用点互信息（PMI）测量名词-修饰语关联强度，以探究偏好的来源。

Result: 三种规模的模型都一致地偏好域同态修饰语排序，尽管训练期间从未见过此类结构。偏好强度因修饰语类型而异。PMI虽能反映已知的修饰语排序模式，但无法解释模型的排序偏好。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够从贫乏输入中恢复类似人类语言学的泛化能力，该研究为探究此类偏见背后的机制提供了受控框架。

Abstract: A central question in language acquisition is whether linguistic biases can emerge from general learning mechanisms operating over underdetermined input. Artificial Language Learning (ALL) studies have shown that human learners reliably generalize beyond the evidence provided, including by preferring scope-homomorphic noun phrase modifier orders. In this work, we investigate whether language models exhibit the same bias under similar conditions. We create a controlled learning environment in which models are trained on a corpus where all noun phrases containing multiple modifiers have been removed, eliminating direct evidence about modifier ordering, and are then evaluated on multiple modifier sentences. Across three model sizes, we find that they consistently prefer scope-homomorphic orders despite never observing them during training. These preferences vary in strength by modifier type. To investigate the source of these preferences, we examine noun-modifier association strength using pointwise mutual information (PMI). While PMI reflects known modifier-ordering patterns, it does not explain the models' ordering preferences. These findings demonstrate that LMs can recover human-like linguistic generalizations from impoverished input and provide a controlled framework for investigating the mechanisms underlying such biases.

</details>


### [177] [Provable Limits and Certified Deferral for Verbalized Uncertainty in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05064)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究小型开放权重语言模型在私有、离线、成本敏感场景下，基于言语化置信度实现"风险受控的延迟决策"（即模型何时应交由人类处理）的可行性与局限性。通过对三个模型家族（0.5B到14B参数）的11个指令微调模型在ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA上的25,168个本地预测进行评估，结合三个理论结果与实证分析，发现温度缩放无法校准一定程度概率的系统，仅少数模型-任务组合能在20%或10%风险预算下获得认证自主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开放权重语言模型越来越多地部署在私有、离线、成本敏感的环境中，此时关键问题不仅是模型回答什么，还包括何时应将对人类决策的延迟交由人工处理。作者希望探究言语化置信度能否支持"风险受控的延迟/转交"机制，以确保小型模型在满足预设风险预算的前提下安全使用。

Method: 本文结合理论分析与实证研究。理论上，作者推导了三个结果：(1) 严格单调的校准保持了风险覆盖率前沿和错误检测AUROC；(2) 温度缩放无法校准那些置信度始终高于0.5而准确率低于0.5的模型；(3) Clopper-Pearson程序可在独立同分布部署假设下，将200题校准集转化为有限样本风险证书。实证上，作者对三个模型家族共11个指令微调模型（0.5B-14B参数）在ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA上进行25,168个本地预测，测试温度缩放与Platt缩放的校准效果，并修复了TruthfulQA多项选择题形式中的答案排序伪影。

Result: 22个模型-任务组合中，8个在温度缩放不可行性下限的一个百分点以内（即命中理论预测边界）。Platt缩放可将ECE降至低至0.02，但在20%风险预算下仅有3个模型-任务组合获得认证自主权，10%预算下则一个都没有。

Conclusion: 校准赋予置信度语义，但认证的延迟/转交机制（certified deferral）决定了小型模型何时安全可用。即使校准效果很好（ECE极低），多数模型-任务组合仍无法满足实际业务中的风险预算要求，说明从"校准良好"到"获得风险认证"之间存在显著差距。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models increasingly run in private, offline, and cost-sensitive settings, where the key deployment question is not only what a model answers but when it should defer to a human. We study whether verbalized confidence can support risk-controlled deferral, evaluating eleven instruction-tuned models from three families, 0.5B to 14B parameters, on ARC-Challenge and TruthfulQA with 25,168 local predictions. Three theoretical results delimit what calibration can provide: strictly monotone calibration preserves the risk-coverage frontier and error-detection AUROC; temperature scaling cannot calibrate models whose confidence stays above one half while accuracy falls below it; and a Clopper-Pearson procedure converts a 200-question calibration set into a finite-sample risk certificate under an i.i.d. deployment assumption. Empirically, eight of 22 model-task pairs hit the temperature-scaling infeasibility floor within one percentage point of the predicted bound. Platt scaling reduces ECE to as low as 0.02, yet certified autonomy at a 20% risk budget is granted to only three model-task pairs and to none at 10%. We also identify and repair an answer-ordering artifact in the multiple-choice form of TruthfulQA. Calibration gives confidence semantics; certified deferral determines when small models are safe to use.

</details>


### [178] [German parties shifted towards intuition-based rhetoric after the far right's parliamentary breakthrough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05075)
*Peer Saleth,Segun T. Aroyehun,Fabio Carrella,Christoph M. Abels,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2015至2025年间4.5百万条推文和59,170篇议会演讲，利用分布词典表示方法测量德国政治精英的证据导向与直觉导向修辞，发现直觉导向语言日益突出，右翼行动者始终具有最低的"证据减直觉"（EMI）分数，且极右翼AfD进入议会后，整个议会的EMI分数出现显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误信息的传播被视为对民主协商的威胁，但政治精英对真理的修辞承诺如何随着民粹主义行动者的崛起而变化仍未被充分理解。作者旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建了6.5万篇德语政治精英文本数据集（2015-2025），使用经过验证的分布词典表示法分别测量证据导向与直觉导向修辞，并计算两者之差（EMI分数），比较议会与社交媒体两个场域、不同政治派别之间的差异。

Result: 两个场域中直觉导向语言均变得更加突出；右倾行动者始终表现出最低的EMI分数；2017年极右翼AfD进入议会与整个议会的EMI分数急剧下降同时发生，而Twitter上呈现出更渐进的下降趋势。

Conclusion: 研究发现记录了极右翼可见性与多元政党制欧洲民主国家精英话语中真理观转变之间的关联，表明极右翼势力的崛起可能与精英话语中证据导向修辞的衰退相关。

Abstract: The spread of misinformation is widely perceived as a threat to democratic deliberation, yet how political elites' rhetorical commitments to truth shift alongside the rise of populist actors remains poorly understood. Analysing 4.5 million tweets and 59,170 parliamentary speeches by German political elites between 2015 and 2025, we measure evidence-based and intuition-based rhetoric using a validated distributed dictionary representation. Across both arenas, intuition-based language has become more prominent, and right-leaning actors consistently exhibit the lowest Evidence Minus Intuition (EMI) scores. The parliamentary entry of the extreme-right Alternative for Germany (AfD) in 2017 coincides with sharp downward shifts in EMI across the broader chamber, while a more gradual decline is observed on Twitter. These findings document an association between far-right visibility and a changing approach to truth in elite discourse in a multiparty European democracy.

</details>


### [179] [Same Formulas, Different Semantics: Do Language Models Follow Modal Logic Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05097)
*Réemi Andrieu,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一组配对模态逻辑问题（相同前提与结论但不同的框架/域条件），通过自动推理验证相反标签，并平衡核心以避免语义条件泄露答案。测试发现五个近期模型中四个在直接提示下表现低于仅基于条件猜测的基线，但启用推理模式后，DeepSeek V4 Flash的准确率从4.4%跃升至88.1%。结果表明：模型遵循规定的模态语义高度依赖于推理模式和模型身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态推理（关于必然性与可能性的推理）依赖于世界间可达性假设及各世界中对象存在性的假设。同一推理在不同模态系统下可能成立与否截然不同，因此评估语言模型在此类问题上的表现，必须检验其判断是否遵循给定的语义而非熟悉的逻辑。现有评测往往无法区分模型是在做语义推理还是依赖经验捷径，作者希望构造能在不同语义条件下产生相反答案的配对问题来精确测试。

Method: 作者构造配对模态问题，两个问题具有相同的前提和结论，但框架条件（世界间可达关系）或域条件（各世界中的对象存在）不同，通过自动推理验证这对问题在不同语义下应得到相反的标签。构建平衡核心集，使单纯的语义条件无法揭示答案（防止模型通过猜测条件类别投机）。在这些问题上对五个近期模型进行直接提示和推理模式两种方式评估。

Result: 在平衡核心测试集上，五个近期模型中有四个在直接提示下表现低于仅基于条件猜测的基线。然而，启用推理模式后（提示不变），DeepSeek V4 Flash的准确率从4.4%提升至88.1%。当省略框架条件时，模型之间往往达成一致，但各自拟合不同的常见逻辑系统。

Conclusion: 语言模型遵循约定的模态语义的能力强烈取决于推理模式（是否启用推理链）以及模型身份。在直接提示下模型往往倾向熟悉的逻辑而非给定的语义；推理模式能显著改善这一状况。作者公开了公式、oracle产物、反模型及模型回复以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Reasoning about necessity and possibility depends on assumptions about accessibility between worlds and about which objects exist at each one. The same inference may therefore hold under one modal system and fail under another. Evaluating language models on such problems requires testing whether their judgments follow the stated semantics rather than a familiar logic. We construct paired modal problems with identical premises and conjecture but different frame or domain conditions; automated reasoning verifies opposite labels. A balanced core prevents the semantic condition alone from revealing the answer. On this core, four of five recent models perform below the condition-only baseline under direct prompting. Yet enabling reasoning mode raises DeepSeek V4 Flash from 4.4% to 88.1% on unchanged prompts. Following stipulated modal semantics thus depends strongly on inference mode as well as model identity. When frame conditions are omitted, models often agree but fit different familiar logics best. We release the formulas, oracle artifacts, countermodels, and responses.

</details>


### [180] [Chained Recursive Language Models for Multi-Iteration Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05124)
*Purbesh Mitra,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出链式递归语言模型（Chained RLM），一种推理时架构，通过将同一模型反复调用为一系列全新的推理根（fresh reasoning roots），每个根只接收原始问题、上下文、紧凑的纯文本摘要、黑板（blackboard）和前任根留下的任务工件（artifacts），而不是完整的对话历史，从而将长上下文推理分解为部分任务逐步完成，支持中间产物的检查、纠正和扩展，缓解单次推理轨迹需同时探索、存储、验证和生成带来的错误传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，单一推理轨迹需要同时承担上下文探索、中间状态存储、证据验证和答案生成等多重任务，尤其在提取、计数、排序和多跳推理等任务中，早期错误会传播至最终答案。现有方法缺乏将推理过程分解为可检查、可纠正的分阶段计算的机制。

Method: 提出Chained RLM推理时架构：同一底层模型被反复调用，每次作为全新的推理根（fresh root），不继承完整对话历史；每个根接收原始问题、上下文、前任根编写的紧凑纯文本摘要、纯文本黑板以及任务相关的持久化工件，通过系统模型、交接机制（handoff）、工件工作区（artifact workspace）和评估协议实现分阶段的上下文管理。

Result: 论文描述并评估了该系统的架构与协议，研究在何种条件下"全新上下文工件延续"（fresh-context artifact continuation）相比直接LLM回答甚至递归工具调用（recursive tool-calling）能带来可测量的准确率提升。

Conclusion: Chained RLM通过将长上下文推理分解为多个可检查和可纠正的分阶段推理，实现了中间状态的外部化存储与验证，避免了单次推理轨迹中的错误累积与传播，为长上下文复杂推理提供了一种新的推理时架构范式。

Abstract: Long context reasoning in large language models (LLMs) is usually constrained by the fact that a single inference trajectory has to simultaneously explore the context, store intermediate state, verify evidence, and produce the final answer. This becomes particularly difficult in tasks that require extraction, counting, ordering, or multi-hop reasoning, where an early mistake can propagate until the final response. In this work, we propose Chained Recursive Language Models (Chained RLM), an inference-time architecture, in which the same underlying model is called repeatedly as a sequence of fresh reasoning roots. Each root receives the original problem and context, but does not inherit the full conversational history. Instead, it receives a compact plain-text summary, a plain-text blackboard, and some durable task-specific artifacts written by predecessor roots. The motivation is to manage the context by chopping into partial tasks rather than one large inference response; in each staged computation, intermediate artifacts can be inspected, corrected, and extended by a later fresh inference by the same model. We describe the system model, handoff mechanism, artifact workspace, and evaluation protocol for this system. We study when fresh-context artifact continuation gives a measurable gain in accuracy over direct LLM answering even with recursive tool-calling.

</details>


### [181] [Spoken Function Calling: A New Perspective on Spoken Language Understanding for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05126)
*Yuezhang Peng,Yuxin Liu,Changfeng Gao,Zhifu Gao,Xiangang Li,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Spoken Function Calling (SFC)，一种基于结构化规则定义的口语语义理解新范式，将传统闭集SLU扩展为开放域函数调用语义理解，并通过SFC-Bench数据集构建、LLM/LALM评估及后训练增强，实验证明SFC显著优于传统SLU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLU在闭集任务经领域内监督微调后能有效提取用户语义，但因其规则定义模糊，在利用上下文学习（in-context learning）处理开放域任务时面临重大挑战，亟需一种新的语义理解范式。

Method: 1）提出SFC语义理解视角，以结构化规则定义优化语义理解；2）基于传统SLU数据集策划并扩展口语函数集合；3）构建多智能体系统合成SFC-Bench数据集；4）评估LLMs和LALMs在SFC上的表现；5）通过后训练（post-training）增强LALMs的SFC能力。

Result: 实验表明SFC在语义提取准确率上优于传统SLU，显著提升了LLMs和LALMs的语义提取能力。

Conclusion: SFC作为一种超越传统闭集SLU的新范式，通过结构化的函数定义实现了更优的开放域语义理解，为任务型对话系统的人机交互提供了更强大的语义理解基础。

Abstract: Spoken Language Understanding (SLU) is the core component of task-oriented dialogue systems and a pivotal link in achieving seamless human-agent interaction. While traditional SLU can effectively extract user semantics for closed-set tasks after in-domain supervised fine-tuning, it faces significant challenges in leveraging in-context learning for open-domain tasks due to its ambiguous rule definitions. This work proposes Spoken Function Calling (SFC), a novel semantic understanding perspective that optimizes semantic understanding with structured rule definitions, to evolve beyond traditional closed-set SLU. Specifically, we curate and extend a suite of spoken functions based on traditional SLU datasets, construct a multi-agent system to synthesize the SFC-Bench dataset, evaluate the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Audio Language Models (LALMs), and enhance the SFC capabilities of LALMs through post-training. Experiments demonstrate that SFC outperforms traditional SLU, substantially enhancing the semantic extraction accuracy for LLMs and LALMs.

</details>


### [182] [Toward Skill-Native LLMs: Skill Entropy for Benchmarking and Training Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05139)
*Yinghui He,Ling Yang,Jiarui Liu,Yongjin Yang,Lechen Zhang,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Mengdi Wang,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨技能长程推理问题（cross-skill long-horizon tasks），引入Skill Entropy作为衡量技能切换难度的指标，并构建了基于558个技能、覆盖9个可验证领域的Skill^2-Bench基准。同时提出Skill-Entropy RL训练框架，通过在RL中同时预测答案和技能序列，结合技能熵奖励来提升模型的跨技能推理能力，在Qwen3系列模型上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常评估单一技能，缺乏衡量模型在长推理链中切换不同技能能力的机制，需要从评估和训练两方面弥补这一空白。

Method: 1) 提出Skill Entropy指标量化技能切换难度；2) 构建Skill^2-Bench基准，包含558个技能、9个领域、按任务级技能熵分为三个难度等级；3) 提出Skill-Entropy RL框架，模型在每一步同时预测答案和所用技能，奖励结合步级正确性和技能熵奖励（衡量预测技能序列与黄金技能序列的对齐程度）。

Result: 在8个前沿模型和4个开源模型上评估发现技能切换差距（精度随熵增加而下降）。Skill-Entropy RL使Qwen3-4B-Instruct的Skill^2-Bench得分从34.4%提升至68.4%，Qwen3-1.7B从14.6%提升至40.1%，优于竞争基线。该管线还可应用于OpenR1-Math等现成训练数据。

Conclusion: 技能熵是一个可复用的训练信号，Skill-Entropy RL框架能有效提升跨技能长程推理能力，且该管线具有通用性。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning in recent LLMs demands that the model switch between distinct skills inside a reasoning chain, such as first doing a math derivation, then using the result to plan a schedule. We call such problems cross-skill long-horizon tasks: multi-step tasks whose steps require different reasoning skills and depend on earlier outputs. Existing benchmarks often evaluate individual skills, lacking a principled way to measure how well a model switches between skills. We address this gap from both the evaluation and training sides. We introduce Skill Entropy, a measure of the difficulty of switching from one skill to another. We then propose Skill^2-Bench, a benchmark of cross-skill long-horizon tasks built over 558 skills across 9 verifiable and open-ended domains. Each task is assigned a task-level skill-entropy score and grouped into three difficulty levels. Evaluating 8 frontier and 4 open-source models on Skill^2-Bench reveals a skill-switching gap: accuracy decreases on higher-entropy tasks. We then turn skill entropy from a benchmark scale into a training signal. We propose Skill-Entropy RL, an RL framework where the model predicts not only the answer at each step but also the skill used to produce it. The reward combines step-level correctness with a skill-entropy reward that measures the alignment between the model-predicted skill sequence and the gold skill sequence. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct and Qwen3-1.7B, Skill-Entropy RL improves the Skill^2-Bench score from 34.4% to 68.4% and from 14.6% to 40.1%, respectively, outperforming competitive baselines. The same pipeline can be applied to off-the-shelf training data such as OpenR1-Math, indicating that skill entropy is a reusable training signal. Code available at: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Skill-Entropy-RL

</details>


### [183] [Reasoning Core: Designing Broad Procedural Data for Completion-Supervised Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05148)
*Damien Sileo,Valentin Lacombe,Dimitri Kachler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Core，一个包含50个程序化推理生成器（覆盖数学、逻辑、规划、状态跟踪、形式语言、结构化数据、游戏、因果和代码）的库，配备语义评分器、难度控制和任务评估器。在匹配的完成监督协议下，与三个现有程序化数据集合对比，Reasoning Core在3B模型比较中于DROP、LogiQA和ARC-Challenge上取得最高平均分。任务级分析表明语义有效性不等于训练效用，紧凑目标和校准难度是关键设计因素。通过结合模型辅助审查、人类裁定和回归测试的审计，揭示了生成、渲染、目标与评分之间的细微不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成器能以规模生产有用的可验证推理问题，但作为完成监督微调的数据受到的关注不足。现有程序化数据集的训练效用缺乏系统评估，且程序化生成本身不保证正确性，需要审计来识别生成、渲染、目标和评分之间的不匹配。

Method: 构建Reasoning Core，包含50个跨越9个领域的生成器，具备语义评分器、难度控制和任务评估器。在四种基础模型设置和多种训练时长下，采用匹配的完成监督协议，将Reasoning Core与Procedural Warmup、Reasoning Gym和SynLogic三个替代集合进行对比。结合模型辅助审查、人类裁定和回归测试进行审计。

Result: 在主要3B比较中，Reasoning Core在DROP、LogiQA和ARC-Challenge上取得最高平均分，超过无程序化数据的基线和全部三个替代程序化集合。任务级分析显示语义有效性本身不保证训练效用，紧凑目标和校准难度是重要设计因素。审计揭示了程序化集合中存在的生成、渲染、目标和评分之间的细微不匹配。

Conclusion: Reasoning Core作为高质量的程序化推理数据集合优于现有方案，但程序化生成本身并不能确保正确性，需要严格审计。该库、生成的数据集和审计材料均公开可用，为未来程序化推理数据研究提供资源。

Abstract: Procedural generators produce useful verifiable reasoning problems at scale, but have received less attention as data for completion-supervised fine-tuning. We introduce Reasoning Core, a collection of 50 generators spanning mathematics, logic, planning, state tracking, formal languages, structured data, games, causality, and code, with semantic scorers, difficulty controls, and task evaluators. Under a matched completion-supervised protocol, we compare Reasoning Core with Procedural Warmup, Reasoning Gym, and SynLogic across four base-model settings and multiple training durations. In the primary 3B comparison, Reasoning Core achieves the highest mean scores on DROP, LogiQA, and ARC-Challenge, exceeding both the baseline without procedural data and all three alternative procedural collections. Task-level analyses show that semantic validity alone does not ensure training utility, highlighting compact targets and calibrated difficulty as important design factors. We ran audits combining model-assisted review, human adjudication, and regression testing. Applied throughout Reasoning Core development and to the other collections, they reveal subtle mismatches among generation, rendering, targets, and scoring, a reminder that procedural generation alone does not guarantee correctness. The library, generated datasets, and audit material are publicly available.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Time^2: A framework for the neural dynamics of visual perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04218)
*Laurent Caplette,Frédéric Gosselin*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉感知具有两个时间维度——处理时间（processing time）和刺激时间（stimulus time），并认为二者常被混淆。作者提出了一种基于反向相关的新方法"Time^2"，可同时测量这两个时间维度，从而更全面地刻画视觉感知中的多种神经现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉感知看似即时，但实际上涉及两个不同的时间过程：一是大脑处理视觉信息所需的时间（处理时间），二是视觉信息持续接收于视网膜上的时间（刺激时间）。现有文献常将二者混为一谈，且实验设计通常只单独考虑其中一个维度，导致难以获得完整的视觉感知图景并约束视觉模型。

Method: 提出了一种基于反向相关（reverse correlation）的新方法，命名为"Time^2"。该方法能够同时测量处理时间和刺激时间两个时间维度，对视觉刺激在时间维度上的变化与感知响应进行联合分析。

Result: 作者展示了Time^2方法能够精确刻画多种神经现象，包括节律性感知（rhythmic perception）、预测性加工（predictive processing）以及从粗到细的采样（coarse-to-fine sampling）。

Conclusion: 为了更完整地描绘视觉感知并进一步约束视觉模型，必须同时考虑和测量处理时间与刺激时间这两个时间维度。Time^2方法为实现这一目标提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Whenever we look at an object, we seem to perceive it immediately. However, this is not the case for two reasons. First, it takes hundreds of milliseconds for the brain to process visual information reaching the retina. Second, we have to look at an object for a certain amount of time to perceive it (and we typically look at it for hundreds of milliseconds) -- during that time, visual information is continuously received on our retinas. These facts together imply that visual information is both processed and received through time. These two temporal facets of perception, which we term processing time and stimulus time, are often conflated in the literature. Moreover, processing time and stimulus time are usually not considered together in experiments. Here, we argue that, to obtain a more complete portrait of visual perception and constrain further models of vision, it is essential to consider and measure both temporal facets simultaneously. We present a new method designed to do so that is based on reverse correlation: Time^2. We show that this method allows us to precisely characterize many neural phenomena, including rhythmic perception, predictive processing and coarse-to-fine sampling.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Influence of passenger head-position uncertainty on infection risk predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04024)
*Philipp Bahavar,Florian Webner,Andrei Shishkin,Daniel Schmeling*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文采用基于CFD的正向风险预测模型，研究自然头部运动对空气传播病原体感染风险的影响。通过准蒙特卡洛模拟将乘客呼吸区域的位置和方向参数化表征，发现前后和侧向头部位置对感染风险有显著影响，而基于矩估计的统计方法（如一阶二阶矩法）无法充分捕捉头部运动各分量与感染风险之间的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在公共交通等环境中，空气传播病原体的感染风险评估通常针对固定座位位置进行，但乘客自然的头部运动会改变呼吸区域的位置和方向，从而可能显著影响局部感染风险的预测结果。为了在设计和政策决策中提高稳定性，有必要量化这种头部运动对局部座位特异性感染风险分布的影响。

Method: 采用计算流体力学（CFD）框架下的正向风险预测模型，用概率分布描述每个乘客呼吸区域的位置和方向参数，并通过准蒙特卡洛（Quasi-Monte-Carlo）模拟获得感染风险的概率分布。同时，对比了一阶二阶矩（FOSM）和高阶矩方法在估计风险统计矩和敏感性方面的表现。

Result: 研究发现前后向和侧向头部位置对感染风险有显著影响，应在座位特异性局部感染风险预测中予以考虑。与准蒙特卡洛采样方法相比，基于矩估计的一阶二阶矩和高阶方法无法充分捕捉头部运动各分量之间的相互依赖关系及它们与感染风险之间的非线性关联。

Conclusion: 自然头部运动对局部感染风险评估具有不可忽视的影响，应将其纳入预测模型以提高决策的稳健性；传统的矩估计统计方法在处理此类高维依赖关系时存在局限性，建议采用基于采样的方法进行感染风险评估。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of natural head movement on the infection risk posed by airborne pathogens using a CFD-based forward risk prediction model. Quasi-Monte-Carlo simulations are used to obtain the resulting infection risk distributions by representing head movement via probability distributions of parameters describing the position and orientation of each passenger's breathing zone. A significant impact of fore/aft and lateral head position on infection risk was found and should be accounted for to increase robustness when predictions of local, seat-specific infection risks are used to guide design and policy decisions. Unlike the sampling-based Monte Carlo approach, estimates of the statistical moments of the risk distributions and sensitivities, calculated using first-order second-moment and higher-order methods, were found to inadequately capture the dependencies between head movement components and infection risk.

</details>


### [186] [The Cost of Binarizing Survival Outcomes in Clinical Prognostic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04046)
*Shashank Yadav,David M. Routman,Andrew Y. K. Foong*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文指出临床机器学习研究中常将生存数据二值化的做法会丢失预后信号，并提出将Cox偏似然替代二值评分函数，形成"生存感知贝叶斯网络"（Survival-Aware Bayesian Network），通过多个癌症数据集验证该方法能恢复被二值化遗漏的预后特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多临床机器学习研究在训练模型前将生存（time-to-event）数据二值化处理，这种做法排除了删失患者、将时间信息压缩为单一阈值，并可能影响预后相关特征的筛选。作者旨在量化这种二值化带来的代价，并证明生存分析方法应作为默认选择。

Method: 以两个近期发表的研究为案例——一个头颈癌队列的贝叶斯网络（BN）特征选择研究，以及一个同样对生存终点做二值化的外科队列研究。作者将二值评分函数替换为Cox偏对数似然用于"特征-结局"边，提出"生存感知贝叶斯网络"（Survival-Aware Bayesian network），并通过消融实验区分改进来源于时间-事件评分公式而非保留更多患者。

Result: 改进在五个头颈癌"终点-队列"组合中均有体现，并扩展到三种其他癌症类型（乳腺癌、结直肠癌和肾癌）。消融实验确认改进由时间-事件评分公式驱动，而非患者保留数目增加。二值化会遗漏由生存分析保留的预后特征。

Conclusion: 建议具有生存结局的临床研究应默认使用时间-事件（time-to-event）方法，因为二值化会丢弃生存分析所保留的预后信号。

Abstract: Survival analysis is an established framework for analyzing time-to-event data, yet many clinical machine learning studies still binarize the outcome before model training. This practice excludes censored patients, collapses temporal information into a single threshold, and can affect which features are selected as prognostically relevant. We examine the cost of this binarization in the context of Bayesian network (BN) feature selection, using two recent publications as case studies: one that applies BN-based feature selection to a head-and-neck cancer cohort and a second surgical cohort study that, while not BN-based, likewise binarizes its survival endpoint. We replace the binary scoring function with the Cox partial log-likelihood for feature-to-outcome edges, a modification we call the Survival-Aware Bayesian network, and recover prognostic features that binarization misses. Our ablation experiment confirms that the improvement is driven by the time-to-event scoring formulation rather than by retaining more patients. The results generalize across five endpoint-cohort combinations in head-and-neck cancer and extend to three further cancer types (breast, colorectal, and kidney). We propose that clinical studies with survival outcomes should use time-to-event methods by default, as binarization discards the prognostic signal retained by survival analysis.

</details>


### [187] [AutoProteinEngine: A Large Language Model Driven Agent Framework for Multimodal AutoML in Protein Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04440)
*Yungeng Liu,Zan Chen,Yu Guang Wang,Yiqing Shen*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 提出AutoProteinEngine (AutoPE)，一个利用大语言模型（LLMs）实现多模态自动化机器学习（AutoML）的智能体框架，使无深度学习背景的生物学家能够通过自然语言交互进行蛋白质工程，在两项真实任务中显著优于传统零样本和手动微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蛋白质工程方法效率低且资源密集，虽然深度学习模型有前景，但训练和实施深度学习模型对缺乏计算专业知识的生物学家来说非常困难，存在技术门槛障碍。

Method: AutoPE是一个基于LLM的智能体框架，将LLM与AutoML相结合，支持：1) 蛋白质序列和图两种模态的模型选择；2) 自动超参数优化；3) 从蛋白质数据库自动检索数据。用户通过自然语言与框架交互即可完成蛋白质工程任务。

Result: 在两个真实世界的蛋白质工程任务中，AutoPE相比传统零样本和手动微调方法表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: AutoPE通过弥合深度学习与生物学家领域专业知识之间的鸿沟，使研究人员无需深厚编程知识即可利用深度学习进行蛋白质工程，降低了入门门槛。

Abstract: Protein engineering is important for biomedical applications, but conventional approaches are often inefficient and resource-intensive. While deep learning (DL) models have shown promise, their training or implementation into protein engineering remains challenging for biologists without specialized computational expertise. To address this gap, we propose AutoProteinEngine (AutoPE), an agent framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) for multimodal automated machine learning (AutoML) for protein engineering. AutoPE innovatively allows biologists without DL backgrounds to interact with DL models using natural language, lowering the entry barrier for protein engineering tasks. Our AutoPE uniquely integrates LLMs with AutoML to handle model selection for both protein sequence and graph modalities, automatic hyperparameter optimization, and automated data retrieval from protein databases. We evaluated AutoPE through two real-world protein engineering tasks, demonstrating substantial performance improvements compared to traditional zero-shot and manual fine-tuning approaches. By bridging the gap between DL and biologists' domain expertise, AutoPE empowers researchers to leverage DL without extensive programming knowledge. Our code is available at https://github.com/tsynbio/AutoPE.

</details>


### [188] [TourSynbio-Search: A Large Language Model Driven Agent Framework for Unified Search Method for Protein Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06024)
*Yungeng Liu,Zan Chen,Yu Guang Wang,Yiqing Shen*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: TourSynbio-Search是一个基于TourSynbio-7B蛋白质多模态大语言模型的生物信息学搜索智能体框架，采用PaperSearch和ProteinSearch双模块架构，支持通过自然语言查询跨UniProt、PDB、ArXiv和BioRxiv等平台进行文献和蛋白质数据检索，降低了研究人员的使用门槛并提升了蛋白质工程领域的信息检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质相关数据库和科学文献呈指数级增长，现有检索方法效率低下且需要专业生物信息学知识，迫切需要一种统一、可访问的搜索方法来弥合复杂生物数据库与研究人员之间的获取鸿沟。

Method: 提出TourSynbio-Search框架，其核心是TourSynbio-7B蛋白质多模态大语言模型驱动的智能体系统。该框架采用双模块架构：PaperSearch模块负责科学文献检索，ProteinSearch模块负责蛋白质数据检索。智能体能解析自然语言查询、优化搜索参数，并跨UniProt、PDB、ArXiv和BioRxiv等多个主要平台执行搜索操作。

Result: 通过文献检索和蛋白质结构可视化两个详细案例研究，证明了TourSynbio-Search在简化生物信息检索流程和提升研究生产力方面的有效性，能够减少技术壁垒让没有深厚生物信息学背景的研究者高效访问和分析复杂生物数据。

Conclusion: TourSynbio-Search是连接复杂生物数据库与研究人员之间可访问性差距的重要进展，具有加速蛋白质工程应用发展的潜力，相关代码已在GitHub上开源。

Abstract: The exponential growth in protein-related databases and scientific literature, combined with increasing demands for efficient biological information retrieval, has created an urgent need for unified and accessible search methods in protein engineering research. We present TourSynbio-Search, a novel bioinformatics search agent framework powered by the TourSynbio-7B protein multimodal large language model (LLM), designed to address the growing challenges of information retrieval across rapidly expanding protein databases and corresponding online research literature. The agent's dual-module architecture consists of PaperSearch and ProteinSearch components, enabling comprehensive exploration of both scientific literature and protein data across multiple biological databases. At its core, TourSynbio-Search employs an intelligent agent system that interprets natural language queries, optimizes search parameters, and executes search operations across major platforms including UniProt, PDB, ArXiv, and BioRxiv. The agent's ability to process intuitive natural language queries reduces technical barriers, allowing researchers to efficiently access and analyze complex biological data without requiring extensive bioinformatics expertise. Through detailed case studies in literature retrieval and protein structure visualization, we demonstrate TourSynbio-Search's effectiveness in streamlining biological information retrieval and enhancing research productivity. This framework represents an advancement in bridging the accessibility gap between complex biological databases and researchers, potentially accelerating progress in protein engineering applications. Our codes are available at: https://github.com/tsynbio/Toursynbio-Search

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [A Distributed Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm For Unit Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04159)
*Ali Rajabi,Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布式量子近似优化算法（DQAOA）的三块交替方向乘子法（ADMM）框架，用于机组组合（UC）问题。该框架将松弛的连续变量与二元机组承诺变量分别求解，其中二元承诺问题被转化为QUBO形式，可通过暴力枚举、单片QAOA或分布式QAOA求解。在五机组（15个二元变量）实例上验证了三种求解模式的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机组组合（UC）问题是电力系统中的重要组合优化问题，包含连续调度变量和二元承诺变量。传统的量子优化方法（如QAOA）受限于量子处理单元（QPU）的容量，难以处理规模较大的二元问题。因此需要一种能够突破单QPU容量限制的方法，同时保持求解结果的一致性。

Method: 构建一个三块ADMM分解框架：一个连续二次规划块求解松弛的承诺与调度变量，一个QUBO块处理二元承诺问题，通过DQAOA接口支持暴力枚举、单片QAOA和分布式QAOA三种求解模式。在分布式模式下，逻辑承诺量子比特被分配到多个容量受限的QPU上，避免整个二元问题必须适配单一设备的要求。其余ADMM更新保持不變。

Result: 在含15个二元变量的五机组UC实例上进行评估。三种求解模式均将ADMM原始残差降至设定容差以下，并获得相同的承诺调度、经济调度结果与运行成本。结果表明三种求解模式具有解一致性，且分布式QAOA展现了多QPU容量扩展能力。

Conclusion: DQAOA-enabled三块ADMM框架能够有效解决UC问题，分布式QAOA模式在保持与传统方法结果一致的同时，突破了单QPU容量限制，为更大规模QC问题的量子求解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (DQAOA)-enabled three-block alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework for unit commitment (UC). The relaxed commitment and dispatch variables are solved in a continuous quadratic programming block, while the binary commitment block is formulated as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem. The DQAOA interface allows this QUBO to be solved using brute-force enumeration, monolithic QAOA, or distributed QAOA, while the remaining ADMM updates are kept unchanged. In the distributed mode, the logical commitment qubits are allocated across multiple capacity-constrained quantum processing units (QPU), avoiding the requirement that the complete binary problem fits on a single device. The framework is evaluated on a five-unit UC instance containing 15 binary variables. All three solver modes reduce the ADMM primal residual below a certain tolerance and recover the same commitment schedule, dispatch, and operating cost. The results demonstrate solution consistency across the three solver modes and the multi-QPU capacity accommodation provided by the distributed QAOA method.

</details>


### [190] [Zero-Instrumentation Dependency Discovery for Guided Microservice Migration Using eBPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04413)
*Eshan Trivedi,Chandrahasa Pranava*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用eBPF内核级网络追踪，在无需应用插桩的情况下自动发现微服务间的运行时依赖关系，生成流量感知的迁移计划并按投资回报率排序。在20服务共享运行时测试环境中，系统恢复了全部20个服务的身份并发现32条依赖边，模拟结果表明ROI排序的迁移顺序相比字母顺序将迁移窗口内的跨VM流量暴露减少了27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务跨虚拟机迁移时，若缺乏对运行时通信模式的了解，可能产生跨VM热点和难以从静态分析预判的延迟尖峰。因此需要一种无需应用插桩、能自动发现运行时服务间依赖关系的方法。

Method: 使用eBPF内核级网络追踪自动发现运行时的服务间依赖关系，无应用插桩；通过两阶段PID-to-port关联算法识别共享运行时中无法区分的服务进程身份；基于依赖图采用谱图聚类结合Kernighan-Lin精细化算法将服务划分为VM内聚分组；按投资回报率对迁移计划排序。

Result: 在20服务测试环境中，系统从3分钟内捕获的13,615个网络事件中识别出全部20个服务及32条依赖边；模拟中ROI排序相比字母顺序减少27%的累积跨VM流量暴露；吞吐量仅下降4.4%，但p50延迟上升383%，p99延迟上升1,050%。

Conclusion: eBPF-based依赖发现与ROI排序迁移方案在测试环境中有效，但采集开销在高负载下延迟显著（p50上升383%，p99上升1050%），建议在非高峰或专用采样节点运行采集。结果仅来自单个自建20服务测试床，不声称适用于生产依赖图。

Abstract: Migrating microservices across virtual machines (VMs) without knowledge of their runtime communication patterns risks creating cross-VM hotspots and latency spikes that are difficult to predict from static analysis alone. We use extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) kernel-level network tracing to automatically discover inter-service dependencies at runtime, with no application instrumentation, and use the resulting dependency graph to produce a traffic-aware migration plan ranked by return on investment (ROI). A two-pass process-identifier (PID) to port correlation algorithm recovers the identity of all 20 services in a shared-runtime testbed where processes are otherwise indistinguishable, matching the known ground-truth topology. The system discovers 32 dependency edges from 13,615 network events captured in three minutes, and applies spectral graph clustering with Kernighan-Lin refinement to partition services into VM-coherent groups. In simulation over the discovered graph, our ROI-ranked migration order reduces cumulative cross-VM traffic exposure during the migration window by 27% relative to alphabetical ordering, a deterministic proxy for arbitrary dependency-blind ordering. Collection overhead is mixed: in a controlled A/B test at near-saturation load on a host with two virtual CPUs (vCPUs), throughput fell by only 4.4%, but median (p50) latency rose by 383% and 99th-percentile (p99) latency by 1,050%. We therefore recommend running captures off-peak or on dedicated sampling nodes rather than under production saturation. All results are from a single 20-service testbed that we authored; we make no claim about behavior on production dependency graphs.

</details>


### [191] [CommBench: Can LLMs Write Correct and Efficient GPU Communication Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04450)
*Shuang Ma,Yuyi Li,Yihan Zhang,Hezhi Xie,Danyang Chen,Shuyang Ji,Ziming Mao,Cheng Ji,Ansha Prashanth,Wenting Yang,Yiran Wang,Chihan Cui,Pei Yu Lin,Ion Stoica,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CommBench是一个面向GPU通信编程的综合基准测试，包含100多个专家精心设计的任务，涵盖点对点通信、集合操作、专家并行通信、计算-通信融合和通信工具函数等五大类。论文引入抗作弊的评估框架和多维统一指标，在一系列顶级模型上评估后发现最强的GPT-5.5也仅在30.7%的任务上正确实现并获得有竞争力的性能，揭示了当前LLM与专家编写的GPU通信代码之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的训练和推理高度依赖高性能GPU通信，但编写高效的GPU通信原语需要对GPU架构、网络硬件和分布式通信模式有深入理解，这对代码生成模型极具挑战性。目前缺乏针对这一关键领域的系统化基准测试，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 论文构建了CommBench基准，包含五类通信任务（点对点通信、集合操作、专家并行通信、计算-通信融合、通信工具函数），参考实现由GPU通信专家编写或从生产代码库中提炼。同时提出了抗作弊的自动评估框架（在多GPU系统上编译、执行和验证生成的代码）以及统一的度量指标，联合衡量功能正确性和通信性能。评估在节点内NVLink和节点间RDMA两种平台上进行。

Result: 评估了领先的闭源和开源代码生成模型后发现，即便是最强模型GPT-5.5，也仅在30.7%的任务上正确实现并达到有竞争力的通信性能，揭示了LLM与专家级GPU通信代码之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: CommBench确立为一个极具挑战性的基准测试，该领域仍是LLM代码生成能力的薄弱环节，论文工作为推进AI辅助的系统级编程研究提供了重要的评估工具和基准数据集。

Abstract: Training and serving large language models (LLMs) rely heavily on high-performance GPU communication, yet implementing efficient GPU communication primitives requires deep expertise in GPU architectures, networking hardware, and distributed communication patterns, making them particularly challenging for code generation models. We present CommBench, a comprehensive benchmark for GPU communication programming, consisting of over 100 expert-curated tasks spanning point-to-point communication, collective operations, expert-parallel communication, compute--communication fusion, and communication utility functions, with reference implementations either written by GPU communication experts or distilled from production codebases. We further introduce a cheat-resistant evaluation framework that automatically compiles, executes, and validates generated code on multi-GPU systems, and a unified metric that jointly measures functional correctness and communication performance. Evaluating leading frontier and open-source code generation models on both intra-node NVLink and inter-node RDMA platforms reveals that even the strongest model, GPT-5.5, correctly implements and achieves competitive performance on only 30.7\% of the benchmark tasks. Our results expose a substantial gap between current LLMs and expert-written GPU communication code, establishing CommBench as a challenging benchmark for advancing AI-assisted systems programming.

</details>


### [192] [AFD-Ledger: Deployment Provisioning for Attention--FFN Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04502)
*Chengyu Qiu,Xiao Fu,Fengcun Li,Yulei Qian,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Yingdi Shan,Yongwei Wu,Mingxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AFD-Ledger，一个离线解析式供给系统，用于在相同的硬件预算和SLO约束下，公平对比AFD（注意力-FFN分离）架构与协同部署架构的吞吐量表现，通过解析执行模型和评估受限的硬件搜索来降低供给成本，并揭示了AFD并非在所有场景下都优于协同部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFD系统虽然提升了分离式执行的效率，但未回答一个关键部署问题：在相同的模型、工作负载、TPOT SLO、硬件预算和运行时能力下，AFD是否比最优协同部署提供更高的吞吐量？由于需要联合优化硬件分配和部署组织，穷举式供给成本过高，需要一种高效的离线分析供给方法来公平比较两种架构。

Method: AFD-Ledger采用两阶段方法：(1) 建立解析执行模型来预测AFD和协同部署两种架构的吞吐量；(2) 使用评估受限的硬件搜索（evaluation-bounded hardware search）来高效探索部署空间，避免穷举式的硬件配置评估，从而在可接受的成本内找到全局最优部署。

Result: 在穷举供给可行的部署空间中，AFD-Ledger将完整部署评估减少了68.8%--83.5%，同时仍能恢复全局最优部署。在三个物理LongCat 2.0部署上，它保持了正确的架构决策，AFD与协同部署吞吐量预测与实测偏差在6.6%--9.6%以内。研究还发现：同构AFD仅在少数场景下提升固定预算吞吐量；异构AFD需要部署级的硬件互补性而非启发式设备选择；角色特定的硬件改进主要在跨越部署能力-价格边界、促成更好部署组织时才有意义。

Conclusion: AFD并非普适优于协同部署，其优势取决于具体的部署设置。AFD-Ledger提供了一种高效且准确的离线供给方法，使得在复杂部署空间中系统地评估和比较两种架构成为可能，为AFD的实际部署决策提供了实用工具和设计指导。

Abstract: Attention--Feed-Forward Network (FFN) Disaggregation (AFD) is emerging as a promising architecture for serving Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models. While existing AFD systems improve the efficiency of disaggregated execution, they leave a deployment question unanswered: under the same model, workload, time-per-output-token (TPOT) service-level objective (SLO), hardware budget, hardware catalog, and runtime capabilities, does AFD provide higher throughput than the best collocated deployment? Answering this question requires jointly optimizing hardware assignment and deployment organization for both architectures, making exhaustive provisioning prohibitively expensive. We present AFD-Ledger, an offline analytical provisioning system that independently provisions AFD and collocated deployments using an analytical execution model and an evaluation-bounded hardware search. Across deployment spaces where exhaustive provisioning is feasible, AFD-Ledger reduces complete deployment evaluations by 68.8%--83.5% while still recovering the globally optimal deployment. On three physical LongCat 2.0 deployments, it preserves the correct architecture decision while predicting AFD-to-collocated throughput within 6.6%--9.6% of measurement. Using this validated framework, we show that homogeneous AFD improves fixed-budget throughput in only a minority of the studied settings, heterogeneous AFD requires deployment-level hardware complementarity rather than heuristic device selection, and role-specific hardware improvements matter primarily when they enable better deployment organizations by crossing deployment capability--price boundaries.

</details>


### [193] [AsymSpec: Efficient Cloud-Edge Speculative Decoding over Asymmetric Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04974)
*Guotao Yang,Hao Chen,Rui Guo,Xinyu Li,Liang Zheng,Sheng Chen,Yitao Hu,Keqiu Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AsymSpec是一种面向云-边缘协同推测解码的调度与通信协议，通过非对称验证协议和确认前缀流水线，解决了上行受限和无效依赖工作问题，相比最强基线实现2.82-28.03倍的输出吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边推测解码将轻量草稿模型放在边缘网关、高质量目标模型放在云端，但每次推测块都引入通信开销。在上行带宽受限时，候选消息会排队而验证器空闲；停等方法使边缘计算利用率低下，而乐观的同请求预取在拒绝或意外奖励token使依赖草稿失效时浪费计算。

Method: 提出两个对应机制：(1) 非对称验证协议——公共路径的接受上传保持紧凑，将更丰富的仅拒绝修正信息放到下行；通过残差分布的总变差(TV)证书判断小型目标top-K响应是否足够，不足时逐步升级到基于提议的精确恢复，最终回退到完整分布。(2) 确认前缀流水线——只将独立、有效的请求暴露给边缘调度器，让云端对到达块重新批处理，在有其他确认前缀请求就绪时隐藏验证等待，避免使用同请求预取。

Result: 在三个草稿-目标模型对、两个工作负载和三个非对称网络配置文件下，端到端评估显示AsymSpec相比最强基线实现2.82-28.03倍的输出token吞吐提升。

Conclusion: AsymSpec有效解决了上行门控验证和无效依赖工作两大问题，通过非对称通信协议与确认前缀流水线的组合设计，在受限上行网络条件下显著提升了云边协同推测解码的吞吐性能。

Abstract: Cloud-edge speculative decoding places a lightweight draft model at an edge gateway and a higher-quality target model in the cloud, but inserts communication into every speculative block. Under a constrained uplink, candidate messages may queue while the verifier is idle. Stop-and-wait scheduling leaves edge compute underutilized; optimistic same-request runahead can waste work when a rejection or an unexpected bonus token invalidates dependent drafts. We present AsymSpec, which addresses uplink-gated verification and invalid dependent work with two corresponding mechanisms. Its asymmetric verification protocol keeps the common-path acceptance upload compact and moves richer, rejection-only correction information to the downlink. A total-variation (TV) certificate for the residual distribution determines whether a small target top-K response suffices; if not, the protocol progressively escalates through proposal-based exact recovery before falling back to the full distribution. Its confirmed-prefix pipeline exposes only independent, valid requests to the edge scheduler and lets the cloud re-batch arrived blocks, hiding verification waits when another confirmed-prefix request is ready without using same-request runahead. Across three draft-target pairs, two workloads, and three asymmetric network profiles, our end-to-end evaluation shows that AsymSpec delivers 2.82-28.03$\times$ the output-token throughput of the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [194] [RAC: Reference-Aware Activation Compression for Communication-Efficient Split LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04991)
*Guotao Yang,Mingxi Zhao,Haopeng Li,Zhengchao Wang,Sheng Chen,Yitao Hu,Keqiu Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"系统"（即RAC，参考感知编解码器）的方法，通过利用token历史引用和校准残差量化来压缩LLM分裂推理中本地-云端-本地路径的边界隐藏状态传输，从而在不显著牺牲任务性能的情况下大幅降低TTFT和TPOT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体反复处理长且隐私敏感的内容，纯云部署将用户数据暴露于可信端点之外，而纯本地部署往往需要昂贵硬件。分裂推理（本地执行头部/尾部/工具层，云端执行中间层）提供了折中方案，但本地-云端-本地路径每次调用都会传输边界隐藏状态，造成严重的通信瓶颈。

Method: 提出RAC（参考感知编解码器），包含四个核心组件：（1）为prefill上行链路检索精确token历史片段；（2）对同轮prefill下行链路重用重建的上行状态；（3）用轻量级因果预测器生成边界特定的解码参考；（4）应用分组仿射对齐和带可选prefill离群值的校准残差量化。此外，发送端线格式重建用于同步后续引用，离线校准同时考虑质量和打包表示成本。

Result: 在三种模型和九组模型-链路组合上，Raw-to-RAC的平均TTFT和TPOT比值分别为1.24-2.72倍和1.01-2.79倍，即性能提升显著；12项非困惑度任务分数变化范围为-0.40到+2.50个点，表明任务质量基本保持甚至略有提升。

Conclusion: RAC通过参考感知的编解码策略有效缓解了分裂推理中的通信瓶颈问题，在不损害模型任务性能的前提下实现了显著的推理速度提升，为隐私敏感的LLM部署提供了一种高效且实用的中间路线。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents repeatedly process long, privacy-sensitive contexts, while cloud-only deployment exposes user data beyond the trusted endpoint and fully local deployment often requires costly hardware. Split inference offers a middle ground by executing the model head, tail, and tools locally and the middle layers in the cloud, but its local-cloud-local path transfers boundary hidden states at every invocation and creates a critical communication bottleneck. We present \system, a reference-aware codec that retrieves exact-token historical spans for prefill uplinks, reuses the reconstructed uplink state for same-round prefill downlinks, and generates boundary-specific decode references with lightweight causal predictors. RAC applies grouped affine alignment and calibrated residual quantization with optional prefill outliers, while sender-side wire-format reconstruction synchronizes subsequent references and offline calibration accounts for quality and packed representation costs. Across three models and nine evaluated model-link pairs, Raw-to-RAC mean time to first token (TTFT) and time per output token (TPOT) ratios are 1.24-2.72$\times$ and 1.01-2.79$\times$, while the 12 non-perplexity task-score changes range from $-0.40$ to $+2.50$ points.

</details>


### [195] [SparseDitto: Customizing GPU Kernels for Different Sparsity Patterns with LLM-Based Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05033)
*Shiyang Li,Guangyan Sun,Jinwei Tang,Yanzhi Wang,Mingyi Hong,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SparseDitto是一个基于LLM的系统，能够针对每个矩阵、算子（SpMV、SpMM、SpGEMM）和目标GPU自动构建定制化的GPU内核，通过轻量级排名模型和架构感知规划器选择最优执行策略，在多个GPU上相比cuSPARSE获得2.68x-2.79x的几何平均加速比，最高加速达146.61x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵内核的GPU性能高度依赖于输入的稀疏模式和执行策略。对于同一个SpMM，cuSPARSE在CSR和Blocked-ELL格式之间存在高达350倍的性能差距，且没有任何单一实现能在所有稀疏模式和算子中一致占优。这促使需要一个能针对每个工作负载和目标GPU自适应调整表示形式、执行策略和硬件映射的系统。

Method: SparseDitto采用统一设计框架支持SpMV、SpMM和SpGEMM。具体方法包括：(1) 轻量级加性模型利用输入矩阵的结构特征对各策略进行排名；(2) 架构感知规划器据此提出多个候选设计；(3) 编码和验证智能体使用目标GPU的测量结果进行实现和优化。

Result: 在多样化的矩阵和三种稀疏算子测试中，SparseDitto在NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU上相对cuSPARSE实现2.68x几何平均加速比（最大146.61x），在NVIDIA H200 GPU上实现2.79x（最大78.5x）。生成的SpMM内核还能将全批次GCN训练加速高达3.39x。

Conclusion: 通过LLM驱动的自适应内核生成框架，SparseDitto证明了针对每个具体工作负载定制GPU内核的有效性，能够显著超越传统的通用稀疏矩阵库，并展示了该方法在多算子和多GPU平台上的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Sparse matrix kernels are fundamental to scientific computing, graph analytics, and machine learning. Their GPU performance depends strongly on the input sparsity pattern and execution strategy. For the same SpMM on the same matrix, cuSPARSE exhibits a 350x performance gap between CSR and Blocked-ELL. Our study of multiple data formats, specialized systems, and sparse compilers shows that no single implementation consistently dominates across sparsity patterns and operators. This motivates a system that can adapt its representation, execution strategy, and hardware mapping to each workload and target GPU.
  We present SparseDitto, an LLM-based system that constructs a GPU kernel for each matrix, operator, and target GPU. SparseDitto supports SpMV, SpMM, and SpGEMM within a unified design framework. A lightweight additive model ranks established strategies using structural features of the input matrix. An architecture-aware planner then proposes several candidate designs. Coding and verification agents implement and refine them using measurements from the target GPU. Across three sparse operators and a diverse set of matrices, SparseDitto achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 2.68x over cuSPARSE on an NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU, with a maximum of 146.61x. On an NVIDIA H200 GPU, it achieves 2.79x, with a maximum of 78.5x. Its generated SpMM kernels also accelerate full-batch GCN training by up to 3.39x.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C²MOE框架，通过一致性和互补性引导的混合专家模型，解决多模态情感识别中模态缺失问题，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态情感识别方法依赖完整的多模态输入，但实际场景中常因传输错误或用户行为导致模态缺失，严重影响性能。现有方法通过跨模态一致性学习提升鲁棒性，但忽略了模态间的互补性，导致重建结果存在偏差。

Method: C²MOE是一个一致性和互补性引导的混合专家框架：(1) 通过交互感知专家将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性两个部分；(2) 一致性通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获，互补性通过最大化模态间条件熵保留；(3) 引入双分支预测机制：一致性分支通过最小化不确定性将填充特征与联合分布对齐，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态特有线索；(4) 采用可学习的重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性分数，实现自适应融合。

Result: 在多个多模态情感识别基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C²MOE在各种模态缺失设置下均持续超越最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: C²MOE通过信息论框架统一了表示学习与缺失模态填补，同时兼顾模态一致性和互补性，有效解决了传统方法由于忽略模态互补信息而导致的重建偏差问题，为不完整多模态情感识别提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [197] [An entropic explanation of insistence on sameness in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04616)
*Przemysław Śliwiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论的框架，将自闭症中对同一性的坚持（insistence on sameness）解释为个体试图减少惊喜和不确定性的普遍行为模式，并给出自闭症的重新定义——认知功能局限于对环境有形属性的辨别、记忆和预测。通过熵度量D_H(R,M)的约束最小化类比，论证坚持同一性是限制刺激以匹配已知记忆的结果，并提出了量化工具体系、学习治疗指导方针及图灵测试式验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试图为自闭症中对同一性的坚持这一核心症状提供一个形式化、可量化的理论解释，将行为观察（坚持同一性）与信息论中的熵最小化原则建立类比联系，从而为该症状的理解、治疗设计提供理论框架和验证途径。

Method: 提出信息论框架，定义熵度量D_H(R,M)=H(R|M)+H(M|R)，其中条件熵分别解释为惊喜和不确定性。通过逻辑推理论证：要最小化该度量，个体要么学习R（将知识存入M），要么将R限制为已知的M。分析一组描述自闭症个体认知局限的假设，并推导坚持同一性属于后者的结论。同时给出该框架的三个应用方向：概念量化、治疗优化算法化（可编程为机器人照护者）、以及图灵测试式验证方法。

Result: 推断出坚持同一性是限制刺激以匹配已有记忆（即约束R到M）的体现；证明了框架可以量化惊喜、不确定性、感官过载/剥夺、焦虑、舒适区、失望、迷失、迂腐、僵硬、遵守或异常精确等十余个概念；可生成学习治疗和日常照护指南并将其定义为优化算法，实现为机器人照护者程序；提出了无需自闭症患者参与实验的图灵测试式验证方案。

Conclusion: 该框架若经正向验证，将为改善自闭症个体基本日常生活活动自立能力的治疗提供正式的理论基础与设计指导方针，标志着从行为描述到形式化理论构建的转向。

Abstract: An information theory-based framework is proposed in attempt to explain insistence on sameness in autism as an instance of a general behavior pattern in which an individual tries to reduce surprise and uncertainty. It offers a new definition of autism as an impairment in which cognitive functions are restricted to discrimination, memorization and prediction of tangible properties of the environment. An analogy between insistence on sameness and constrained minimization of the entropy metric is observed and examined for a set of assumptions that describe cognitive limitations of a person with autism. The metric is given by the formula $D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)$, where $R$ represents sequences of random stimuli, $M$ is a memory that stores and retrieves them, and where $H(.|.)$ denotes their conditional entropies interpreted as surprise and uncertainty, respectively. It is first inferred that to minimize the metric an individual can learn about $R$ (and store that knowledge in $M$) or can restrict $R$ to the already known $M$. Then, it is concluded that insistence on sameness is a manifestation of the latter. Moreover, it is shown that the proposed framework: (1) Helps to quantify the concepts of surprise, uncertainty, sensory overload and deprivation, anxiety, comfort zone, disappointment, disorientation, pedantry, rigidness, observance or aberrant precision. (2) Leads to a list of guidelines for learning therapies and daily care routines, and allows them to be defined as optimization algorithms and implemented as programs for robotic live-in caregivers. (3) Can be validated with the help of a Turing test-like approach that requires no experiments involving individuals with autism. The framework-if positively validated-will provide formal foundations and design guidelines for therapies aimed at improving self-reliance of individuals with autism in basic activities of daily living.

</details>


### [198] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了次优势（minmax）超度量算子的ℓ₀型稳定性理论。该算子等价于单链聚类诱导的超度量，其经典稳定性理论通常在ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff框架下表述，但这些界不适用于仅改变少量成对距离的稀疏扰动。本文证明稀疏编辑仅通过最小生成树（MST）传播，并给出每个编辑的暴露切割分数、树仅全局包络、Hamming-Lipschitz界及锐度结果，最后在深度嵌入图上验证了结构性分数作为层次表示漏洞诊断的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稳定性理论（如ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff意义上的界）对于稀疏扰动不适用——现实中许多扰动仅改变少数成对距离。作者旨在建立一种适合稀疏情形的稳定性理论，理解超度量值在哪些条件下会改变以及改变多少。

Method: 从理论上分析稀疏编辑如何通过MST传播超度量变化。对于树内编辑，导出每编辑的暴露切割分数；对于树外编辑，分析新暴露的切割。建立树仅全局包络，推导Hamming-Lipschitz界（度量改变的超度量条目数上界）。构造锐度反例证明结果不可改进，并推导多编辑条件近可加性原理。最后在深度嵌入图上进行实验验证。

Result: 证明了稀疏编辑仅通过MST传播：成对超度量值仅在其树路径穿越被编辑边或由被编辑树外边新暴露的切割时才可能改变；在严格切割分离条件下树边界精确达到；存在一族中一次编辑改变Θ(n²)个超度量条目的显式构造；对来自深度嵌入图的MST验证了结构性分数作为层次表示漏洞诊断工具的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个面向稀疏扰动的超度量稳定性理论框架，揭示了树几何结构（具体地，MST）在编辑传播中的核心作用。所得的结构性分数可作为层次表示中脆弱性诊断的实用工具，并为超度量算子的稳定性分析提供了比经典ℓ∞/GH界更精细的刻画。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [199] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信任区域框架来统一理解和解释Adam等自适应矩估计优化机制，并由此推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制（称为Gmake）。当p=4时涉及峰度估计。实验表明，当信任区域约束较弱时四阶矩实现优势最大，而随着约束增强，二阶矩实现（即Adam类）更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩估计优化算法在深度学习中广泛使用，但其行为缺乏统一的解释框架。作者希望通过信任区域框架从理论上统一理解自适应矩估计、学习率调度、动量（视为谱低通滤波）以及算子级谱归一化等多种看似独立的机制。

Method: 作者构建了一个信任区域框架：将每个权重更新步长的大小约束在一个由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）所决定的信任区域内。由此推导出一族学习率机制，基于二阶矩估计和归一化的p阶矩估计。当p=4时，涉及峰度（kurtosis）类估计。整体机制称为Gmake（Generalized moment-adaptive kurtosis estimation），将矩估计归一化、学习率调度、动量谱低通滤波和算子级谱归一化统一在同一个信任区域框架中。

Result: 在GPT2-124M模型上使用FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集进行实验。结果表明：当信任区域约束较弱时，四阶矩（峰度类）实现带来最大收益；随着信任区域约束逐渐增强，二阶矩实现变得更具竞争力，通常能达到略低于对应四阶矩实现的验证损失。

Conclusion: 信任区域框架为自适应优化机制提供了统一的理论视角。四阶矩（峰度）估计在弱约束下更有优势，而二阶矩（Adam类）在强约束下更优，这为在实践中根据信任区域约束强度选择适当的矩阶数提供了指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [200] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了将机器学习框架与传统R-Matrix代码结合，用于自动检测中子透射谱中的共振峰。采用全卷积神经网络对七个透射谱（两个评估数据、五个实验数据）中的每个点进行分类，虽然分类准确率达到93%，但该指标夸大了模型的泛化能力。即使增加了训练数据，模型仍无法可靠地泛化到未见过的同位素。未来工作需更大的训练数据集并融入中子共振的物理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R-Matrix代码在拟合中子透射数据时依赖于先前评估结果，需要大量人工干预且存在偏差。中子透射数据复杂且噪声大，传统峰值识别方法难以有效分析，因此需要开发自动化方法来加速后处理过程并减少对先前评估的依赖。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对中子透射谱中的每个数据点进行分类，判断其属于共振区或非共振区。模型在七个透射谱（两个评估数据和五个实验数据）上进行训练和评估。

Result: 模型在七个透射谱上实现了约93%的分类准确率，但进一步分析表明该指标高估了模型的泛化能力。尽管在PHYSOR 2026先前分析的基础上加入了额外的训练数据，模型仍然无法可靠地泛化到先前未见过的同位素。

Conclusion: 该初步研究表明机器学习方法有潜力加速中子透射数据处理，但目前模型的泛化能力不足。未来需要：(1) 评估更大、更多样化的训练数据集是否能产生可泛化的模型；(2) 将中子共振的已知物理特征融入模型以提升性能。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [201] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lindblad开放系统动力学启发的多时间尺度回声状态网络（水库计算）架构，将循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态构成，使旋转（相位混合）和衰减（记忆遗忘）成为独立可调的设计变量，并提供直接的全局稳定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
