Method: ABC框架包含两个核心组件：(1) Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery——从答案回溯恢复解决查询所需的中间线索；(2) Clue-Anchored Step Scoring——基于恢复的线索评估每个搜索步骤，将稀疏的二值结果监督转化为稠密的步骤级奖励。基于此构建ABC-SFT（按步骤重加权损失）和ABC-GRPO（以步骤级分数作为GRPO奖励）。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5-4B和8.5k训练样本训练的ABSeeker在BrowseComp上达到37.3%，在BrowseComp-ZH上达到39.1%；加入上下文管理后分别提升至55.3%和52.9%，显著超越同规模（4B）智能体，甚至匹配约30B参数的更大模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，基于答案回溯的步骤级信用分配方法对于训练长程搜索智能体是有效的，即使是失败轨迹中的有用步骤也能获得正向奖励，从而提升整体搜索策略的学习效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon search agents must make multiple sequential actions (steps) to search, retrieve, verify, and integrate evidence to reach a final answer. However, existing methods for training these agents typically treat all steps within a trajectory uniformly during both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), failing to distinguish useful actions from erroneous or redundant ones. In this paper, we propose Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment (ABC), a fine-grained credit assignment framework for training long-horizon search agents by converting sparse trajectory-level outcomes into dense step-level supervision that rewards useful actions (even in failed trajectories) while suppressing erroneous or redundant actions. Specifically, given a potentially obscure query and its corresponding ground-truth answer, ABC first performs Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery, which traces back from the answer to recover intermediate clues required to solve the question. It then applies Clue-Anchored Step Scoring to evaluate each search step against these clues, converting sparse binary outcome supervision into dense step-level rewards. Based on these rewards, we develop ABC-SFT, which reweights the loss of each turn, and ABC-GRPO, which uses the step-level scores as rewards in GRPO. Building on this framework, we train ABSeeker based on Qwen3.5-4B with only 8.5k examples. ABSeeker achieves 37.3% on BrowseComp and 39.1% on BrowseComp-ZH. With context management, the scores further improve to 55.3% and 52.9%, respectively, significantly outperforming same-scale (4B) agents and even matching the performance of larger ones (approximately 30B). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of answer-backtracked step-level credit assignment for training long-horizon search agents.

</details>


### [331] [CoPlan: A Trustworthy Co-Intelligence Interface for Care Planning through Role-Based Contestable Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05107)
*Hung Truong Thanh Nguyen,Hélène Fournier,Piper Jackson,Makoto Itoh,Shannon Freeman,Rene Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoPlan，一个面向人机协同护理规划的"共智能且可争议"交互界面。它通过多智能体工作流（专业AI智能体生成候选干预方案及支持或质疑论点），让人护理规划者可以接受、拒绝、修改或补充论点后再生成最终方案，从而将人类与AI的互补专长相结合，并保持AI建议的可检查、可修订和可论证性。该系统在居家养老护理规划场景中得以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在护理规划中通常以固定输出形式呈现建议，限制了利益相关者检查、质疑和修订计划的能力。当AI建议与临床判断、患者价值观或现实可行性冲突时，缺乏人类介入和调整的机制，这削弱了系统的可信度与临床责任担当。

Method: CoPlan采用多智能体工作流：专业AI智能体生成候选干预方案以及支持性或挑战性论点；人工护理规划者可在最终方案生成前接受、拒绝、修改或添加论点。该系统支持自适应护理团队招募、基于角色的论点审查、最终护理方案生成，并通过调度智能体实现实际随访。

Result: 在居家养老护理规划场景中进行了演示，验证了CoPlan在自适应护理团队招募、角色化论点审查、最终护理方案生成以及通过调度智能体进行实际随访等方面的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: CoPlan贡献了一个可争议的护理规划交互界面，并为可信赖的人机协同护理规划提供了设计框架，该框架在保持AI辅助能力的同时，保留了人的主观能动性和临床问责机制。

Abstract: AI-supported care planning can help clinicians, patients, caregivers, and care teams coordinate complex decisions across clinical, functional, psychosocial, and environmental needs. However, many AI systems present recommendations as fixed outputs, limiting stakeholders' ability to inspect, challenge, and revise plans when they conflict with clinical judgment, patient values, or real-world feasibility. We present CoPlan - a Co-Intelligent and Contestable Interface for Human-AI Care Planning. CoPlan uses a multi-agent workflow in which specialized AI agents generate candidate interventions and supporting or challenging arguments, while human care planners can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before final plan generation. Through this design, CoPlan combines co-intelligence, in which humans and AI agents contribute complementary expertise, with contestability, where recommendations remain open to inspection, revision, and justification. We demonstrate CoPlan in an aging-in-place care planning scenario. The system supports adaptive care team recruitment, role-based argument review, final care plan generation, and practical follow-up through scheduling agents. This work contributes a contestable care planning interface and a design framing for trustworthy human-AI care planning that preserves human agency and clinical accountability.

</details>


### [332] [OctoLong: Mid-Training On Cross-Repository Code Contexts Enhances Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05141)
*Indraneil Paul,Falko Helm,Goran Glavaš,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Context lengths of language models (LMs) have dramatically increased, driven by the demands for in-context learning, self-improvement, and long-horizon agentic workflows. Existing long-context corpora, however, are dominated by books, academic articles, and code repositories, which are finite resources and often scarce in long-distance dependencies. In this work, we introduce OctoLong, a context engineering pipeline that instruments an AST parser, a language server backend, and a package manager to facilitate the recursive retrieval of code references, enabling the curation of dependency-rich code contexts of millions of tokens in length. We then train OctoLong-Instruct, a suite of capable long-context open LMs, derived from base models ranging in size from 600M to 14B parameters, via context-extension mid-training on a ~50B-token mixture containing ~6.2B tokens of OctoLong code contexts, followed by ~10B tokens of instruction tuning. Our training ablations and experimental evaluations against 18 state-of-the-art open-weight long-context LMs show that supplanting just 12% of traditional context-extension corpora with OctoLong data yields substantial gains in long-range retrieval, long-term state tracking, repository-level code understanding, and downstream agentic tasks, while also enhancing API usage in short-context coding scenarios.

</details>


### [333] [Argus: A General-Purpose Agentic Runtime for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05144)
*Boxiu Li,Zimo Wen,Yijia Fan,Junxiang Lei,Sufeng Guo,Jiaao Wu,Ruize Tang,Mukai Li,Yifei Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Wanbo Zhang,Runjing Gu,Yifei Gao,Yuheng Wu,Xuyao Huang,Zelong Zhao,Jiachen Zhang,Shibo Hu,Hangxi Guo,Yilin Chen,Yuzhe Zhang,Fan Yang,Chuan Wen,Xian Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Argus是一个固定权重、自我演化的持久化智能体运行时系统，通过Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四个角色在可持久化项目状态上执行有界任务，实现长期推理中的自我修正和知识积累。在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%的准确率（对比Direct Copilot的59%），并通过验证门控的自我演化实现推理效率和token利用率的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期推理需要智能体运行时能够在该方法受证据支持时持续执行，并在测量结果揭示失败、隐藏约束或目标设定错误时转向。现有系统缺乏持久化的运行时状态和自我演化能力，难以在长时程任务中积累经验和修正策略。

Method: Argus将稳定的用户意图与操作目标、约束和验证标准分离，采用Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四个角色协作执行有界任务。记忆、技能、程序、验证器、路由决策和已拒绝路径均在角色所属的审查和任务原生验证通过后才被接纳。模型权重保持不变，自我演化仅通过持久化运行时状态和控制策略实现，在操作者拥有的升级点之间进行自主执行。

Result: 在七个GPT-5.5基准竞技场上，Argus在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%的准确率（对比Direct Copilot的59%），token消耗为1.41倍。经过验证门控的自我演化后，成熟SWE-Bench波次比初始波次减少21%的solve-input token和15%的活跃工作流时间，记录34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环救助。在AARRI-Bench上达到76.8%，数学数据合成任务上有28.0分的差距，GPU内核和语言模型训练结果具竞争力。RWKV6内核已合并上游，多日数学战役保留被证伪的路径和基于证明的前沿更新，六个论文流程完成254个任务并进行16次阶段回滚。

Conclusion: 固定权重的自我演化框架能够修订、恢复和积累经过验证的方法，同时为未来的监督学习和强化学习生成结构化的轨迹数据，证明了无需更新模型权重即可实现持续的智能体自我改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires an agentic runtime that can persist when evidence supports its current approach and pivot when measurements reveal failure, hidden constraints, or a misspecified objective. We present Argus, a persistent, self-evolving runtime in which Manager, Planner, Engineer, and Reviewer execute bounded missions over durable project state. Argus separates stable user intent from operational objectives, constraints, and verification criteria, and admits memories, skills, procedures, verifiers, routing decisions, and rejected routes only after role-owned review and, when available, task-native verification. Model weights remain fixed; self-evolution occurs through persistent runtime state and control policy, with autonomous execution between operator-owned escalation points. Across seven GPT-5.5 benchmark arenas, Argus achieves about 78% on SWE-Bench Pro versus 59% for Direct Copilot while using 1.41 times the aggregate tokens. After verification-gated self-evolution, mature SWE-Bench waves use 21% fewer solve-input tokens and 15% less active workflow time per task than startup waves, while recording 34 verifier recoveries and 22 strict review-loop rescues. Argus also reaches 76.8% on AARRI-Bench and a 28.0-point gap on mathematical data synthesis, with competitive GPU-kernel and language-model-training results. Beyond benchmarks, an optimized RWKV6 kernel was merged upstream; a multi-day mathematics campaign retained falsified routes and proof-backed frontier updates; and six paper pipelines completed 254 missions with 16 stage rollbacks. These results show that a fixed-weight, self-evolving harness can revise, recover, and accumulate verified approaches while producing structured trajectories for future supervised and reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [334] [Similarity All The Way Up: Multilingual Generalization in LLMs Relies on Language-Level Similarity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22699)
*Supantho Rakshit,Adele Goldberg,Henry Conklin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大语言模型（LLMs）的多语言泛化能力，从认知科学中的"相似性空间表征驱动泛化"角度出发，发现LLMs的潜在表征能大致恢复印欧语系语言树的层次结构，且表征相似度结构与XNLI基准性能正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在多语言（尤其是训练数据稀少的非英语语言）上的泛化能力难以量化和理解。现有的研究工作缺乏对"模型为何在某些语言上表现更好"的机制性解释。作者借鉴认知科学中关于泛化与相似性空间表征关系的长期研究，试图从表征空间的角度理解LLMs的多语言泛化差异。

Method: 作者从认知科学视角出发，假设成功的泛化源于在相似性空间中具有恰当的表征。他们检验LLMs的潜在表征是否捕捉了不同语言之间的层次化相似性结构（即印欧语系家族树），并计算表征中语言相似性结构与其实际谱系关系的对应程度，进而将该对应程度与模型在XNLI多语言自然语言推理基准上的表现进行相关性分析。

Result: 结果表明：LLMs的潜在表征在很大程度上恢复了印欧语系语言树的层次结构，即同一亚语系的语言在表征空间中彼此靠近。此外，模型反映语言相似性结构的程度与其在XNLI上的性能呈正相关——即越能正确表征语言间相似关系的模型，其跨语言泛化能力越好。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典认知科学中关于"相似性驱动泛化"的理论扩展到大语言模型的规模上，揭示了LLMs的多语言泛化能力与其在表征空间中正确反映语言层次相似性结构密切相关，为理解模型多语言泛化机制和指导模型改进提供了新的视角。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) grow more capable across diverse tasks, their (in)ability to generalize remains difficult to quantify and poorly understood beyond limited domains. In particular, LLMs are known to struggle generalizing multilingually, to languages outside of English, and that are poorly attested in their training data. To understand why this may be, and what enables some models to perform better than others, we turn to a long history of work across the cognitive sciences, arguing that successful generalization derives from appropriate representations in similarity space. We look at how well LLMs' representations capture the hierarchical similarity structure between distinct languages. Strikingly, we show LLMs' latent representations largely recover the hierarchical structure of the Indo-European language family tree -- grouping languages that are members of the same subfamily closely together in representation space. Furthermore, we show that the degree to which models reflect the similarity structure of languages correlates with their performance on XNLI, a multilingual natural language inference benchmark. This extends classic work on similarity-driven generalization at scale, showing how models that represent similar languages similarly generalize better from one language to another.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [335] [Securing Contrastive mmWave-based Human Activity Recognition against Adversarial Label Flipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04029)
*Amit Singha,Ziqian Bi,Tao Li,Yimin Chen,Yanchao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于毫米波（mmWave）的无线人体活动识别（HAR）系统在监督对比学习框架下对标签翻转投毒攻击的脆弱性，提出了三种标签投毒攻击方法及相应的防御对策，并在原型系统上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线HAR技术因其非侵入性在医疗、虚拟现实和监控等领域具有广阔应用前景，mmWave技术进一步提升了其性能。然而，现有研究缺乏对mmWave HAR系统在监督对比学习范式下安全性的系统性探讨，尤其是针对标签投毒攻击的脆弱性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 本文提出了三种针对监督对比学习中mmWave HAR系统的标签翻转投毒攻击方法，并设计了对应的防御对策。通过原型系统实验对攻击效果和防御有效性进行了验证。

Result: 实验结果验证了三种标签投毒攻击的有效性以及所提防御对策的防护能力。该攻击和防御方法可推广至其他无线HAR系统。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了mmWave HAR系统在监督对比学习下的安全漏洞，并通过提出攻击与防御方案促进无线HAR系统在设计和部署中的安全性考量，具有重要的推广意义。

Abstract: Wireless Human Activity Recognition (HAR), leveraging their non-intrusive nature, has the potential to revolutionize various sectors, including healthcare, virtual reality, and surveillance. The advent of millimeter wave (mmWave) technology has significantly enhanced the capabilities of wireless HAR systems. This paper presents the first systematic study on the vulnerabilities of mmWave-based HAR to label flipping poisoning attacks in the context of supervised contrastive learning. We identify three label poisoning attacks on the contrastive mmWave-based HAR and propose corresponding countermeasures. The efficacy of the attacks and also our countermeasures are experimentally validated on a prototype system. The attacks and countermeasures can be easily extended to other wireless HAR systems, thereby promoting security considerations in system design and deployment.

</details>


### [336] [SoK: How Frontier AI Reshapes System-Level Security Risk Dynamics in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04033)
*Chandra Thapa,Mohan Baruwal Chhetri,Marthie Grobler,Shahroz Tariq,Tooba Aamir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个五维风险动力学框架，系统化分析前沿人工智能（FAI）如何重塑关键基础设施（CI）的网络安全态势，并识别学术研究与实际运营之间的结构性错配，提出面向部署的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAI（大语言模型、多模态基础模型、智能体系统）正被广泛集成到关键基础设施中，挑战了传统安全假设（如行为有界、网络分段、组件透明、人速决策）。现有AI安全文献按攻击类型、生命周期阶段或资产类别组织风险，但无法捕捉系统层面的动力学——即风险如何产生、传播和被控制——而这正是FAI重塑CI安全结果的关键。

Method: 采用系统化知识（SoK）方法，提出一个五维风险动力学框架，覆盖FAI全生命周期如何重构CI安全策略：(i) 能力涌现（FAI带来的新型攻防能力）、(ii) 渗透路径（通过数据、模型和AI供应链）、(iii) 跨系统传播（互联基础设施与依赖关系）、(iv) 有效技术/人控权威的退化、(v) 制度性应急能力承受运营压力。

Result: 框架不再枚举具体威胁，而是识别出共同决定系统级风险的反复出现的机制。同时发现学术AI安全研究与CI运营约束之间存在结构性错配，并据此推导出以系统级保障标准为基础的、面向部署的研究议程。

Conclusion: 该工作将研究视角从以模型为中心的鲁棒性转向互联CI环境中生命周期结构化、系统级保障，为FAI集成到关键基础设施的安全治理提供了新的分析框架和方法论指导。

Abstract: Frontier artificial intelligence (FAI), encompassing large-scale, general-purpose AI systems, including large language models, multimodal foundation models, and agentic systems, is increasingly integrated into critical infrastructure (CI). This challenges long-standing security assumptions of bounded behavior, segmented networks, component transparency, and human-paced decision-making. Existing AI-security literature typically organizes risks by attack type, lifecycle stage, or asset class, but fails to capture the system-level dynamics, such as how risk emerges, spreads, and is controlled, through which FAI reshapes CI security outcomes. This Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) introduces a five-dimensional risk-dynamics framework that characterizes how FAI reconfigures CI security across the lifecycle: (i) Capability Emergence through new FAI-enabled attack and defense capabilities, (ii) Infiltration Pathways through data, models and AI supply chains, (iii) Cross-System Propagation across interconnected infrastructures and dependencies, (iv) degradation of effective technical and human Control Authority, and (v) strain on institutional Response Capacity under operational pressure. Rather than enumerating threats, the framework identifies recurring mechanisms that jointly determine system-level risk. We further identify a structural mismatch between academic AI-security research and CI operational constraints, and derive a deployment-oriented research agenda grounded in system-level assurance criteria. Collectively, this work shifts attention from model-centric robustness to lifecycle-structured, system-level assurance in interconnected CI environments.

</details>


### [337] [A Multimodal Automatic Redteaming Evaluation based on Atomic Jailbreak Strategy Decoupling and Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04034)
*Shiji Zhao,Yuxuan Zhou,Chen Xiong,Dongxian Wu,Yang Bai,Xun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HACA（Hierarchical Atomic Combination Attack）的多模态自动化红队越狱攻击方法。该方法将文本-图像越狱策略空间分解为结构、语义和句法三个层次，构建覆盖文本和图像双模态的六维原子策略空间，通过跨模态联合规划器组合不同原子策略，并使用统一生成执行器生成越狱指令，在五个主流多模态大语言模型上实现了平均95.48%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态越狱攻击面临两个根本性挑战：一是缺乏原子级的多模态策略空间，二是缺乏超越人工经验的简洁高效的可执行框架。已有的多模态越狱攻击虽证明了可行性，但未能系统性地解决这些问题。

Method: 方法包括三个核心组件：（1）将文本-图像越狱策略空间分解为结构、语义、句法三个层次，构建涵盖文本和图像模态的多模态越狱策略集；（2）基于六维策略空间，使用跨模态联合规划器（cross-modal joint planner）选择和组合不同的原子越狱策略；（3）在实施层面，探索使用统一生成执行器（unified generate executor）基于选定的多模态策略直接生成越狱指令。

Result: 在五个主流多模态大语言模型上进行的系列实验表明，该方法作为自动化红队工具，实现了平均95.48%的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 通过系统化分解策略空间并引入自动化规划与生成框架，HACA方法有效解决了多模态越狱攻击中策略覆盖不全和实施框架缺乏的问题，在多模态大语言模型上展现出很高的攻击有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive progress in image-text comprehension and generation, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that can trigger harmful outputs and pose serious safety concerns. Existing multimodal jailbreak attacks have shown the feasibility of such attacks, but they still face two fundamental challenges: the lack of a atomic multi-modal strategy space, the absence of a concise and efficient executable framework beyond human-craft experience. To address these challenges, we first decompose the text-image jailbreak strategy space into three levels: structural, semantic, and syntactic, constructing a jailbreak strategy set encompassing both text and image modalities to systematically achieve combined coverage of different attack types. Then we propose a multimodal automated red team jailbreak method named Hierarchical Atomic Combination Attack (HACA). Specifically, based on a six-dimensional strategy space, a cross-modal joint planner is used to select and combine the different atomic jailbreak strategy for subsequent jailbreak command generation. Finally, at the implementation level, we explore to apply a unified generate executor to directly generate jailbreak instructions based on the selected multi-modal strategies. A series of experiments show that our automated red team method can achieve an attack success rate of average 95.48\% against five mainstream MLLMs.

</details>


### [338] [AMD SEV-SNP: A Confidential Computing Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04039)
*Amean Asad,Patrick McClurg,Patrick Woodhead*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是AMD SEV-SNP（安全加密虚拟化与安全嵌套分页）的技术入门指南，系统介绍了这一硬件机密计算实现如何在视虚拟机监控程序（hypervisor）为敌手的威胁模型下，通过硬件级加密、内存完整性校验和远程证明机制，为虚拟机提供可信执行环境（TEE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算环境中，虚拟机监控程序（hypervisor）被假定为不可信或潜在敌手，传统虚拟化无法保护虚拟机内存和寄存器状态不被监控程序窃取或篡改。因此需要一种从硬件层面构建的机密计算方案，确保即便hypervisor完全被攻破，客户虚拟机仍具有机密性和完整性保障。

Method: 本文从硬件底层出发系统性地阐述了SEV-SNP的实现机制：（1）利用AMD安全处理器和内存控制器中的加密引擎，使用hypervisor从未掌握的密钥加密客户机内存和寄存器状态；（2）通过反向映射表（RMT）在内存访问点检测任何篡改，提供内存完整性保护，对抗控制页表的敌手；（3）通过VM特权级别（VMPL）、加密VM保存区（VM Save Area）和GHCB协议，实现客户机与不信任的hypervisor之间的协作通信机制；（4）通过将硬件签名的客户机初始状态测量值绑定到AMD证书链，构建远程证明管道，使远程验证者能够独立确认正在运行的代码。

Result: SEV-SNP通过将加密密钥隔离在硬件层，使hypervisor无法获取客户机内存和寄存器的密钥；通过反向映射表提供针对页表控制型攻击的内存完整性；通过VMPL、加密VM保存区和GHCB协议建立不可信hypervisor与受信任客户机间的安全协作；并通过基于AMD证书链的硬件签名测量实现可独立验证的远程证明。

Conclusion: SEV-SNP为虚拟机提供了从硬件层构建的机密计算解决方案，在将hypervisor视为敌手的威胁模型下，实现了内存与寄存器状态的加密保护、内存完整性校验以及可远程验证的代码证明能力，为云计算场景中的可信执行环境提供了完整的技术支撑。

Abstract: This paper is a technical primer on AMD Secure Encrypted Virtualization with Secure Nested Paging (SEV-SNP), a hardware confidential computing implementation that provides Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) for virtual machines. SEV-SNP treats the hypervisor as adversarial. It encrypts guest memory and register state with keys the hypervisor never possesses, detects any tampering with guest memory at the point of access, and lets a guest prove to a remote verifier exactly what code it is running. The paper constructs each of these guarantees from the hardware up. It opens with the threat model that drives the design and the hardware that enforces it, the AMD Secure Processor and the encryption engine in the memory controller. It then develops the mechanisms that make a confidential guest practical. The Reverse Map Table provides memory integrity against an adversary who controls the page tables. The privilege and communication machinery (VM Privilege Levels, the encrypted VM Save Area, and the GHCB protocol) lets the guest cooperate with a hypervisor it does not trust. The attestation pipeline binds a hardware-signed measurement of the guest's initial state to AMD's certificate chain, so a remote verifier can confirm independently what is running.

</details>


### [339] [Beyond the QBER Threshold: A Temporal QBER Based Machine Learning Framework for Multi Attack Detection in BB84 QKD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04047)
*Isha,Deepak Singh,Devesh Kumar,S. K Pal,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时间序列QBER的机器学习框架，用于检测和分类BB84 QKD系统中的窃听攻击。相比传统固定的11% QBER阈值监测（仅25.82%准确率、0.8477的假阴性率），该框架提取63个物理信息驱动的时序特征，通过XGBoost等分类器实现88%准确率和0.0198的假阴性率，显著提升了对隐蔽攻击的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BB84 QKD系统依赖固定的11% QBER阈值来检测窃听，但隐蔽性攻击可以保持在阈值以下而不被察觉，同时仍危及信道安全。由于平均会话级QBER信息不足以捕捉微妙攻击特征，需要更精细的时序分析框架来弥补这一安全漏洞。

Method: 从BB84 QKD系统中提取63个物理信息驱动的时序特征，涵盖突发行为、时序不稳定性、基选择不对称性以及QBER损失交互等方面。在噪声和有损条件下，使用随机森林、XGBoost和支持向量机（径向基函数核）三种分类器，对七种窃听攻击及正常信道场景进行分类评估。同时利用SHAP进行特征可解释性分析，并与固定11% QBER阈值的传统基线方法进行对比。

Result: 在十次独立运行中，XGBoost表现最佳，准确率达88.01%（±0.47%），宏F1分数为0.8803；SVM-RBF性能相当。作为二元攻击检测器，传统11% QBER阈值仅达25.82%准确率且假阴性率为0.8477，而所提框架将假阴性率降至0.0198。SHAP分析表明物理信息驱动的时序和信道特征对识别窃听策略具有高度判别力。

Conclusion: 时序QBER驱动的机器学习方法为BB84 QKD系统提供了准确、可解释且实用的多攻击安全监测框架，显著优于传统的固定阈值QBER监测方法，能够有效检测阈值以下的隐蔽攻击。

Abstract: Conventional BB84 Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) systems rely on a fixed 11% Quantum Bit Error Rate (QBER) threshold to detect eavesdropping. However, stealthy attacks can remain below this threshold while still compromising channel security. This paper proposes a temporal QBER based machine learning framework for detecting and classifying eavesdropping attacks in BB84 QKD systems. Rather than relying on average session level QBER, the framework extracts 63 physics-informed temporal features capturing burst behavior, temporal instability, basis dependent asymmetry, and QBER loss interactions. Random Forest, XGBoost, and Support Vector Machine with a Radial Basis Function kernel (SVM-RBF) classifiers are evaluated on seven eavesdropping attacks and a normal channel scenario under noisy and lossy conditions. Averaged over ten independent runs, XGBoost achieves the best performance with 88.01% (0.47%) accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.8803, while SVM-RBF performs comparably, confirming the robustness of the proposed features. Evaluated as a binary attack-versus-normal detector for comparison with conventional monitoring, a fixed 11% QBER threshold achieves only 25.82% accuracy with a False Negative Rate (FNR) of 0.8477, whereas the proposed framework reduces the FNR to 0.0198, substantially improving detection of stealthy attacks that evade threshold-based monitoring. SHapley Additive exPlanations based (SHAP) explainability shows that physics-informed temporal and channel derived features are highly discriminative for identifying eavesdropping strategies. These results demonstrate that temporal QBER driven machine learning provides an accurate, explainable, and practical framework for multi attack security monitoring in BB84 QKD systems.

</details>


### [340] [When Modalities Fail to Tango: Conformal Backdoor Detection in Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04052)
*Yiming Chen,Kemou Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CASCADE，一种基于共形预测（Conformal Prediction）的两阶段粗到精后门检测框架，用于多模态对比学习（MCL）中的图像-文本对后门攻击检测。该方法通过跨模态一致性粗筛和高置信度中毒对的参考集构建，利用文本空间相似度的非一致性分数进行细粒度识别，在CC3M数据集上达到100% TPR下平均FPR 5.79%和平均AUROC 0.9867。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIPScore的检测方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 良性对与中毒对的CLIPScore分布存在大量重叠，降低了该指标的可靠性；(2) 固定阈值检测无法为重叠区域内的模糊样本提供统计保证。因此需要一种能够提供可证明置信边界的新型检测方法。

Method: 提出CASCADE两阶段框架：粗粒度阶段利用跨模态一致性识别高置信度的良性和中毒对；细粒度阶段从高置信度中毒对构建参考集，基于文本空间相似度计算每个未识别样本的实例级非一致性分数（NCSs），衡量其与中毒分布的符合程度，从而精确识别潜在中毒对。整体基于共形预测框架建立可证明的置信边界。

Result: 在CC3M大规模数据集上的大量实验表明，CASCADE在多种攻击下平均FPR为5.79%（100% TPR条件下），平均AUROC为0.9867，且对自适应攻击保持有效。

Conclusion: CASCADE通过引入共形预测框架解决了传统CLIPScore阈值检测方法在重叠分布区域缺乏统计保证的问题，提供了一种具有可证明置信边界的后门检测方案，在检测性能上显著优于现有方法，并对自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks in multimodal contrastive learning (MCL) have garnered growing attention in recent years, as many downstream tasks critically depend on pre-trained MCL models. Existing detection-based defenses predominantly rely on the CLIPScore metric, under the assumption that poisoned pairs exhibit lower semantic similarity between the image and the caption. However, we identify two critical flaws remaining in existing methods: (1) the substantial overlap between CLIPScore distributions of benign and poisoned pairs undermines the reliability of this metric, and (2) fixed-threshold detection cannot provide statistical guarantees for ambiguous samples within overlapping regions. To overcome these limitations, we propose integrating conformal prediction (CP), a statistical framework that quantifies uncertainty through nonconformity scores (NCSs), to establish provable confidence bounds for detecting poisoned image-caption pairs. Building on CP, we introduce CASCADE, a novel two-stage Coarse-to-Fine Conformal Backdoor Detection framework. The coarse-grained stage uses cross-modality consistency to identify high-confidence benign and poisoned pairs. In the fine-grained stage, a reference set is constructed from high-confidence poisoned pairs, and instance-level NCSs based on text-space similarity are computed for each sample in the unidentified subset. These NCSs measure conformity to the poisoning distribution and enable precise identification of latent poisoned pairs within the unidentified subset. Extensive experiments on the large-scale CC3M dataset demonstrate that CASCADE achieves an average FPR of 5.79% at 100% TPR and an average AUROC of 0.9867 across diverse attacks, while remaining effective against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [341] [AgentAntibody: An Adaptive Immune System for Defending LLM Agents against Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04053)
*Shihao Weng,Yang Feng,Xiaofei Xie,Jiongchi Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentAntibody提出了一种受适应性免疫启发的自进化防御系统，通过持久化"抗体库"来学习用户的语义安全边界，在运行时识别威胁并随时间推移强化免疫能力，在三个基准和四个LLM骨干网络上优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示注入防御将每个任务视为独立问题，忽视了用户请求经常缺乏明确性（欠指定）的事实。攻击者可以利用这种模糊性让代理以用户会拒绝的方式完成任务。随着用户期望通过具体案例逐渐明确，防御系统应从每次遭遇中学习并应用到下一次。

Method: 受适应性免疫系统启发，AgentAntibody为LLM代理配备自进化免疫系统：(1) 将用户安全边界的演化理解表示为持久化的抗体库；(2) 运行时，抗体库识别威胁并触发相应的免疫响应；(3) 跨多次遭遇，抗体库进化以强化对未来攻击的免疫力。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和四个骨干LLM上的大量实验表明，AgentAntibody通过经验学习用户安全边界，在防止有害行为的同时保持合法任务完成方面优于现有防御，即使有害行为和合法行为都与所述任务兼容时也依然有效。

Conclusion: 通过经验学习用户语义边界而非孤立地处理每个任务，是抵御利用任务欠指定性的提示注入攻击的有效策略，AgentAntibody验证了这一思路的可行性。

Abstract: Prompt injection remains a critical threat to LLM agents, yet existing defenses treat each task as a self-contained problem, independent of previous encounters. In practice, user requests are often underspecified: they describe the desired outcome without fully specifying acceptable behavior. An injection can exploit this ambiguity, causing the agent to complete the task in a way the user would reject. As the user's expectations become clearer through concrete cases, a defense should learn from each encounter and apply what it learns to the next. Inspired by adaptive immunity, we propose AgentAntibody, which equips LLM agents with a self-evolving immune system against prompt injection. AgentAntibody represents its evolving understanding of the user's security boundary as a persistent library of antibodies. At runtime, the library recognizes threats to this boundary and mounts corresponding immune responses. Across encounters, it evolves to strengthen the agent's immunity to future attacks. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks and four backbone LLMs show that, by learning the user's boundary through experience, AgentAntibody outperforms existing defenses in preventing harmful actions while preserving legitimate task completion, even when the harmful and legitimate actions are both compatible with the stated task.

</details>


### [342] [An Inline Control Architecture for Language Models in Intelligent Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04065)
*Narendra Kumar Dewangan,Mounira Msahli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Guard-V2X，一种用于保护LLM增强型车联网（V2X）服务的内联语义护栏架构，在满足实时性约束的前提下，通过规则过滤、安全分类器、结构化生成、可信检索和决策后裁决等多层防护机制，抵御针对LLM提示的对抗性攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X系统越来越多地集成LLM用于消息摘要、操作员辅助和决策支持等语义任务，但这些组件不在安全关键控制回路内，因此引入了传统V2X安全机制（侧重认证和消息完整性）无法解决的提示级攻击面。鉴于此，需要一种新的安全架构来保护LLM增强型V2X服务免受提示注入等攻击。

Method: Guard-V2X采用内联语义护栏架构，集成五层防御机制：1）基于规则的入口过滤；2）轻量级安全分类器；3）策略约束的结构化生成；4）仅信任来源的检索；5）决策后裁决。该架构在LLM输出被下游执行前强制执行机器可检查的安全边界，并通过四阶段实验流水线评估：入侵漏洞分析、校准与延迟基准测试、护栏验证、对抗压力下的鲁棒性测试。

Result: 在基于V2X对齐模拟数据集（源自RSU通告、操作员消息和标注的V2X消息摘要）上的实验结果表明：无防护和仅提示词基线的多轮对抗试验中仍存在残余漏洞，而Guard-V2X持续降低了入侵接受成功率，在两轮设置中消除了所有观察到的非安全完成，且未超出V2X语义咨询路径的延迟预算。

Conclusion: Guard-V2X证明了在实时约束下对LLM增强型V2X服务实施内联语义防护是可行且有效的，为V2X系统中LLM安全应用提供了一种可机器验证的多层防御方案，填补了传统V2X安全机制在提示级攻击防护方面的空白。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-everything (V2X) systems increasingly incorporate large language models (LLMs) for semantic tasks such as message summarization, operator assistance, and decision support at roadside units and edge nodes. Although these components are not part of safety-critical control loops, they introduce prompt-level attack surfaces that are not addressed by traditional V2X security mechanisms focused on authentication and message integrity. This paper presents Guarded-V2X, an inline semantic guardrail architecture for securing LLM-enabled V2X services under real-time constraints. The proposed system integrates rule-based ingress filtering, a lightweight safety classifier, policy-constrained structured generation, trusted-only retrieval, and post-decision adjudication to enforce machine-checkable safety boundaries prior to downstream execution. Guarded-V2X is evaluated using a four-stage experimental pipeline encompassing intrusion vulnerability analysis, calibration and latency benchmarking, guardrail validation, and robustness under adversarial stress. Experiments are conducted on a V2X-aligned simulated dataset derived from RSU advisories, operator messages, and annotated V2X message summaries. Results show that unguarded and prompt-only baselines retain residual vulnerability under multi-turn adversarial trials, while Guarded-V2X consistently reduces intrusion acceptance success rates and eliminates observed unsafe completions in two-turn settings, without exceeding latency budgets for V2X semantic advisory paths.

</details>


### [343] [FBID: Adaptive Personalized Federated Learning for Robust Out-of-Distribution Attack Detection in IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04073)
*An Khanh Bui,Cong Thanh Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Pham,Hoang Thai Dinh,Diep N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FBID是一种新颖的自适应个性化联邦学习框架，通过服务器端的上下文多臂老虎机动态控制每个客户端的本地训练强度，并引入基于信任的混合机制在全局与本地模型之间插值，以解决PFL在异构IoT入侵检测中因过度个性化导致的OOD攻击检测性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法主要依赖客户端侧自调整，在高度非IID数据分布下容易导致过度个性化，使得模型对分布外（OOD）攻击的检测能力显著下降。这一核心缺陷限制了PFL在异构IoT入侵检测场景中的实际应用效果。

Method: FBID框架包含两个关键机制：1）服务器端部署上下文多臂老虎机（Contextual Multi-Armed Bandit），根据每个客户端观察到的行为和更新质量动态调节其本地训练强度；2）信任感知混合机制，为每个客户端计算全局模型与本地模型之间的个性化插值系数，在保留全局攻击检测知识的同时允许有益的本地专业化。

Result: 在CICIoT2023数据集上、异构客户端分布及OOD压力测试设置下，FBID相比最强稳定基线，将个体客户端的OOD检测率（DR）相对提升最高达7.66%，F1分数相对提升最高达5.08%，同时对未见攻击类别的鲁棒性也有显著改善。

Conclusion: 通过服务器端主动控制个性化程度而非依赖客户端自调整，可以有效缓解PFL中的过度个性化问题，在保持全局攻击检测泛化能力的同时实现局部适应性提升，为异构IoT环境下的入侵检测提供了更稳健的PFL解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) has emerged as a promising solution for intrusion detection in heterogeneous IoT environments, as it can improve local adaptation under highly Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (non-IID) data distributions. However, existing PFL methods often rely on client-side self-adjustment, which may lead to over-personalization and substantial degradation in out-of-distribution (OOD) attack detection. In this paper, we propose Federated Bandit Intrusion Detection (FBID), a novel adaptive PFL framework to address this limitation through server-side personalization control. In particular, FBID employs a contextual multi-armed bandit at the server to dynamically regulate each client's local training intensity according to its observed behavior and update quality. Moreover, FBID introduces a trust-based blending mechanism to derive client-specific interpolation coefficients between the global and local models, thereby preserving global attack-detection knowledge while still allowing beneficial local specialization. Through extensive experiments on the CICIoT2023 dataset under heterogeneous client distributions and OOD stress-test settings, we show that FBID improves individual client OOD Detection Rate (DR) by up to 7.66% and F1-Score (F1) by up to 5.08% (relative) over the strongest stable baseline, while also improving robustness to previously unseen attack classes.

</details>


### [344] [NEBULA: A Language - Independent Specification for Opaque Rotating Refresh Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04115)
*Matteo Teodori*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NEBULA——一个精确的、语言无关的RFC 9700刷新令牌模型规范，配有十种语言的参考实现和以数据而非散文形式发布的机器可读一致性测试套件（38个场景），通过比较并交换（compare-and-set）轮换契约解决并发刷新下重用检测的绕过漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RFC 9700作为OAuth 2.0安全的最佳当前实践，规定了公开客户端刷新令牌必须轮换并带有重用检测，但该BCP仅规定策略而非机制——没有指定线格式、存储模式、验证步骤顺序、并发契约或边缘情况语义（如丢失响应的重试或密钥轮换）。实现可能在决定安全结果的关键边界情形上产生分歧。

Method: NEBULA是一个精确的语言无关规范，定义了刷新令牌模型：128位公开选择器加256位密钥验证器，均为CSPRNG输出，不携带声明或签名，令牌有效性由服务器端状态决定而非密码学验证。其一致性方法论将行为套件以数据（一个机器可读文件包含38个场景）而非散文的形式发布，每个实现通过薄层语言运行器执行，从根本上排除转述漂移。规范的核心包括一个compare-and-set轮换契约，关闭并发刷新下重用检测的可复现绕过。

Result: 提供了十个符合规范的参考实现（TypeScript、Python、Go、Rust、Java、PHP、C#、Ruby、Elixir、Dart），分析了包括后量子态势在内的安全属性，并报告了跨语言一致性作为多实现安全规范的方法论成果。所有规范、实现和一致性工件均以Apache License 2.0开源发布。

Conclusion: NEBULA展示了通过精确规范加机器可读一致性测试的方法，可以消除多语言实现间在安全关键边界情形上的分歧，将RFC 9700的策略要求落地为可验证的机制级保证。

Abstract: Refresh tokens are among the most sensitive credentials in modern authentication systems: long-lived, bearer-style, and sufficient to mint access tokens for days or weeks. RFC 9700, the current Best Current Practice for OAuth 2.0 security, mandates that refresh tokens issued to public clients be rotated on every use with replay (reuse) detection, or be sender-constrained. But the BCP specifies policy, not mechanism: it prescribes no wire format, no storage schema, no ordering of verification steps, no concurrency contract, and no semantics for edge cases such as lost-response retries or key rotation. Implementations may therefore diverge in precisely the corner cases that determine security outcomes.
  We present NEBULA, a precise, language-independent specification of the RFC 9700 refresh-token model, together with ten conformant reference implementations (TypeScript, Python, Go, Rust, Java, PHP, C#, Ruby, Elixir, Dart). NEBULA tokens are opaque -- a 128-bit public selector and a 256-bit secret verifier, both CSPRNG output, carrying no claims and no signature -- so token validity is a property of server-side state rather than of cryptographic verification. Its conformance methodology publishes the behavioural suite as data rather than as prose: 38 scenarios in one machine-readable file that every implementation executes through a thin per-language runner, so that drift by transcription is structurally excluded. We describe the specification -- including a compare-and-set rotation contract that closes a reproducible bypass of reuse detection under concurrent refresh -- analyse its security properties including its post-quantum posture, and report on cross-language conformance as a method for multi-implementation security specifications. The specification, implementations, and conformance artefacts are open source under the Apache License 2.0.

</details>


### [345] [Behavioral Information Leakage in Darknet Traffic: A Multi-Channel Analysis Across Anonymity Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04143)
*Javeriah Saleem,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个行为信息泄漏框架，将流级流量分解为控制、结构和节奏三类描述符，用于分析Tor、I2P、FreeNet、ZeroNet四种匿名网络中的服务可区分性。结果显示Tor具有最高的服务可分离性（Macro-F1为0.7165，累积归一化泄漏为3.9461），而FreeNet泄漏最低（0.8744）。分组大小组织、方向交换不平衡、分组节奏和静默-突发行为是主要泄漏机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗网流量分类研究过度强调预测精度，缺乏对加密服务可区分性背后的行为机制的理解。作者希望超越单纯的分类性能，系统性地揭示是什么行为特征使得不同匿名网络中的加密服务可以被区分。

Method: 提出行为信息泄漏框架，将流级流量分解为控制、结构和节奏三个描述符组；结合归一化互信息分析、基于随机森林的预测验证、结构-节奏交互分析，以及交叉网络服务变异性评估；在泄漏安全的重复分层交叉验证下进行实验；并引入服务变异性指数（Service Variability Index）和泄漏变异性指数（Leakage Variability Index）两个新指标。

Result: 行为泄漏在不同匿名网络间差异显著：Tor可分离性最高（Macro-F1 0.7165，累积泄漏3.9461），FreeNet最低（0.8744）。主要泄漏机制包括分组大小组织、方向交换不平衡、分组节奏和静默-突发行为。组合的结构-节奏表示在网内性能最强，但留一网络评估显示跨匿名架构的迁移性有限。视频服务具有一致的网络特定可分离性，而聊天和电子邮件在匿名网络对之间表现出更大变异性。

Conclusion: 匿名网络的行为泄漏并非均匀分布，不同网络架构导致不同程度的服务可区分性风险。结构-节奏特征的组合最能揭示服务区分信息，但该信息难以跨匿名架构迁移，说明匿名网络间的行为指纹具有架构特异性。所提出的框架和指数为理解匿名流量隐私泄漏机制提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Existing darknet traffic classification studies largely emphasize predictive accuracy while offering limited insight into the behavioral mechanisms that make encrypted services distinguishable. This paper proposes a behavioral information leakage framework that decomposes flow-level traffic into control, structural, and rhythmic descriptor groups across Tor, I2P, FreeNet, and ZeroNet. The framework combines normalized mutual information analysis with Random Forest-based predictive validation, structural-rhythmic interaction analysis, and cross-network service-variability evaluation under leakage-safe repeated stratified cross-validation. Results show that behavioral leakage varies considerably across anonymity networks. Tor achieves the highest service separability, with a Macro-F1 of 0.7165 and cumulative normalized leakage of 3.9461, whereas FreeNet exhibits the lowest combined leakage of 0.8744. Packet-size organization, directional exchange imbalance, packet tempo, and silence-burst behavior emerge as the main leakage mechanisms. The combined structural-rhythmic representation consistently provides the strongest within-network performance, while leave-one-network-out evaluation reveals limited transferability across anonymity architectures. The proposed Service Variability Index and Leakage Variability Index further show that video exhibits consistent network-specific separability, whereas chat and email demonstrate greater variability across anonymity-network pairs.

</details>


### [346] [LLM-Assisted Detection and Repair of Hardware Security Vulnerabilities in Verilog Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04907)
*Ethen Santana,Gabriel Gyaase,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLM）从Verilog硬件设计中识别常见弱点枚举（CWE）安全漏洞的方法，实验证明了其在自动化硬件安全分析中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件设计与软件一样存在安全漏洞风险，但与软件不同，硬件缺陷在制造后永久嵌入硅片中，难以修补。因此，需要在硬件设计阶段尽早发现这些安全弱点，以提高硬件安全性。

Method: 提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法，直接从Verilog格式的硬件设计中识别潜在的硬件CWE安全弱点。方法在单模块Verilog设计数据集上进行迭代评估，以检验其在检测硬件安全弱点方面的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够有效利用LLM辅助检测硬件设计中的安全漏洞，证明了LLM在增强传统硬件安全分析方面的潜力，能够提供自动化、可扩展的漏洞识别能力。

Conclusion: LLM可以作为传统硬件安全分析的有力补充，为硬件设计过程中的安全漏洞检测提供自动化且可扩展的辅助手段，有助于在硬件制造前发现并修复安全隐患。

Abstract: Hardware designs, like software, are susceptible to bugs that can introduce security vulnerabilities and create opportunities for malicious exploitation. Unlike software vulnerabilities, however, hardware flaws become permanently embedded in silicon after fabrication, making them difficult or impossible to patch. Many of these weaknesses are categorized under the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) framework and include improper access control, exposure of sensitive information, and unintended privilege escalation. To improve the detection of such vulnerabilities, we propose a methodology that leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) to identify potential hardware CWEs directly from hardware designs in Verilog. The proposed approach is evaluated iteratively on a dataset of single-module Verilog designs to assess its effectiveness in detecting hardware security weaknesses. Our results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to augment traditional hardware security analysis by providing automated, scalable assistance for identifying security vulnerabilities during the hardware design process.

</details>


### [347] [Open-World Darknet Traffic Recognition Under Leave-One-Service-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04167)
*Javeriah Saleem,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于留一服务评估和不确定性感知分类的开世界暗网流量分类框架，发现从闭世界转移到开世界设置时模型性能显著下降（如XGBoost Macro-F1从88.8%降至46.1%），并揭示了已知与未知服务间强烈的行为相似性导致的语义吸收问题，表明行为重叠是开世界暗网流量分类可靠性的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗网流量识别研究大多采用闭世界评估，假设所有服务类别在训练和测试阶段均已知，这不符合真实场景。作者旨在通过开世界评估框架更准确地反映实际部署中的模型鲁棒性，填补闭世界评估对部署性能高估的研究空白。

Method: 采用留一服务评估（leave-one-service-out）方法模拟开世界场景，结合不确定性感知分类机制，使用随机森林（Random Forest）和XGBoost两种模型进行流量分类，并通过语义吸收分析揭示已知服务如何吸收未知服务流量。

Result: 从闭世界到开世界转换中性能显著下降：XGBoost Macro-F1从88.8%降至46.1%（I2P环境），Random Forest从87.4%降至45.7%。不确定性拒绝仅轻微改善鲁棒性；FreeNet视频流量被88.1%分配为浏览流量，I2P点对点流量被83.4%吸收为FTP相关行为，表明已知与未知服务间存在强烈行为相似性。

Conclusion: 闭世界评估会显著高估暗网流量分类模型的部署鲁棒性；即便引入不确定性拒绝机制，已知与未知服务的行为重叠（行为相似性）仍是开世界暗网流量分类的主要挑战，需要针对性解决方案提高可靠性与实用价值。

Abstract: Darknet traffic recognition is critical for cyber threat intelligence, as anonymity networks are often used to conceal malicious activity. However, most existing studies rely on closed-world evaluation, assuming all service categories are known during training and testing, which is unrealistic in real-world environments. This paper presents an open-world darknet traffic classification framework using leave-one-service-out evaluation and uncertainty-aware classification with Random Forest and XGBoost models. Experimental results demonstrate significant performance degradation when transitioning from closed-world to open-world settings, demonstrating that closed-world evaluation substantially overestimates deployment robustness. For example, XGBoost Macro-F1 decreases from 88.8% to 46.1% in the I2P environment, while Random Forest performance drops from 87.4% to 45.7%. Although uncertainty-based rejection slightly improves robustness, strong behavioral similarity between known and unknown services leads to frequent misclassification. Semantic absorption analysis further shows that FreeNet video traffic is classified as browsing traffic with an 88.1% assignment rate, while I2P peer-to-peer traffic is absorbed into FTP-related behavior with an 83.4% assignment rate. The findings demonstrate that behavioral overlap remains a major challenge for reliable open-world darknet traffic classification.

</details>


### [348] [Kerckhoffs-Compliant Watermarking for Physical Design IP Protection: From Placement to Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05055)
*Andrew B. Kahng,Yiting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PDMarks是一个符合Kerckhoffs原则的物理设计（PD）IP水印框架，仅依赖32字节密钥通过HMAC-SHA256在布局、时钟树综合和布线等多个PD阶段确定性嵌入所有权证据，安全性不依赖隐藏的构造细节。集成到OpenROAD-flow-scripts，在NanGate45和ASAP7设计上实现了联合巧合概率低于10^{-32}，PPA开销可比或更小，且能抵御错误密钥和攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理设计IP（包括单元布局、时钟分配和布线决策）是VLSI实现中的宝贵资产，随着PD工具普及，未经授权复用已布局布线数据库的问题日益突出。现有PD水印方法要么只保护单一PD阶段，要么依赖隐藏的构造细节，在面对白盒攻击者时存在漏洞。

Method: PDMarks采用Kerckhoffs原则设计，安全性仅依赖于秘密密钥。使用HMAC-SHA256从32字节密钥确定性派生所有水印实例和目标值，在布局、时钟树综合（CTS）和布线等多个PD阶段嵌入所有权证据，并集成到OpenROAD-flow-scripts中实现一致的水印嵌入和验证。

Result: 在NanGate45和ASAP7设计上的实验表明，PDMarks相比先前方法提供了更强的所有权证据，PPA开销可比或更小。所有评估设计的近似联合全阶段巧合概率均低于10^{-32}。错误密钥无法重现完整的所有权证明，且弱化水印需要对受保护实现进行广泛扰动。

Conclusion: PDMarks提供了一种多阶段、密钥安全且符合Kerckhoffs原则的PD IP水印方案，在保证低PPA开销的同时实现了统计上极低的巧合概率（低于10^{-32}），有效抵御白盒攻击者，并已在OpenROAD流程中实现实用集成。

Abstract: Physical design (PD) intellectual property (IP) is a valuable artifact of modern VLSI implementation. It includes optimized cell placement, clock distribution, and routing decisions produced by carefully tuned PD flows. As access to PD tools expands, unauthorized reuse of placed-and-routed databases becomes an increasing concern. Existing PD watermarking methods either protect only one PD stage or rely on hidden construction details, leaving them vulnerable to a white-box adversary. In this work, we develop PDMarks, a Kerckhoffs-compliant watermarking framework whose security depends only on a secret key. PDMarks embeds ownership evidence across multiple stages of the PD flow, including placement, clock tree synthesis (CTS), and routing. All watermark instances and target values are deterministically derived from a 32-byte secret key using HMAC-SHA256, enabling consistent embedding and verification. PDMarks has been integrated into OpenROAD-flow-scripts. Experiments on NanGate45 and ASAP7 designs show that PDMarks outperforms prior physical design watermarking methods by providing much stronger ownership evidence with comparable or smaller PPA overhead. The approximate joint all-stage coincidence probability is below 10^{-32} for every evaluated design. Wrong-key and attack evaluations further show that incorrect keys do not reproduce the complete ownership proof and that weakening the watermark requires broad perturbation of the protected implementation.

</details>


### [349] [Behavioral Skill Reconstruction: Reconstructing Hidden Functionality from LLM Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04192)
*Peichun Hua,Haoxuan Xu,Mengyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出行为技能重建（BSR）问题，即攻击者仅通过合法交互就能克隆隐藏技能的功能。作者介绍SkillClone黑盒攻击方法，通过形成接口假设、发送结构化探测、合成可执行副本并迭代修复，在30个技能上实现精确或部分恢复，表明文件保密不等于功能保密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要针对提示注入攻击导致的文件泄露，但防止文件泄露并不阻止用户恢复该文件实现的功能。一个根本问题悬而未决：用户能否在文件保持隐藏的情况下，仅通过正常使用重建技能的功能？

Method: SkillClone包含四个步骤：(1) 从技能的公开宣传信息形成接口假设；(2) 发送结构化的良性探测请求；(3) 合成一个可执行的副本；(4) 通过与受害者技能的差异验证进行迭代修复。整个攻击仅使用合法交互，无需直接访问隐藏文件。

Result: 在涵盖规则、表格、流程和算法四类共30个技能上，SkillClone对多个目标的保留输入实现了精确或部分恢复。迭代重新查询弥补了单轮重建遗漏的差距。现有防泄露防御对此覆盖有限，不详细的技能描述也提供有限保护。

Conclusion: 文件保密本身并不能确保功能保密。防御措施必须同时限制正常使用过程中累积的信息泄露，而非仅仅关注文件层面的泄露防护。

Abstract: Closed source agent skills may encode proprietary instructions, scripts, constants, and data. Providers may offer their capabilities as services while keeping the underlying packages hidden. Prior work focuses on prompt injection attacks that directly disclose these artifacts, and existing defenses accordingly aim to prevent such leakage. However, preventing file disclosure does not prevent users from recovering the functionality those files implement. This raises a fundamental question: can a user reconstruct a skill's functionality through ordinary use while its files remain hidden?
  We study behavioral skill reconstruction (BSR), in which an attacker uses valid task requests and observed responses to build a functional clone of a hidden skill. We introduce SkillClone, a black-box attack that clones a target skill by forming an interface hypothesis from its public advertisement, issuing structured benign probes, synthesizing an executable replica, and iteratively repairing it through differential validation against the victim skill. Across 30 skills spanning rules, tables, procedures, and algorithms, SkillClone achieves exact or partial recovery on held-out inputs for several targets. Iterative requerying closes gaps missed by single-round reconstruction. Because SkillClone uses only legitimate interactions, disclosure-focused defenses provide limited coverage, and less detailed skill descriptions offer limited protection. These results show that file secrecy alone does not ensure functional secrecy. Defenses must also limit cumulative information leakage from ordinary use.

</details>


### [350] [Hardware Design and Security in the Era of Chiplets and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05063)
*Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Paul V. Gratz,Ramesh Karri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对半导体行业两大前沿（异构2.5D chiplet系统与大语言模型驱动的EDA流程）进行了统一的硬件安全分析，涵盖从架构、逻辑到物理层面的攻击面，并综述了相应的防御技术，包括基于2.5D拆分制造与有源interposer的物理隔离信任根架构，以及针对LLM驱动EDA管线的原生威胁识别与防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体行业正经历双重变革：一是向异构2.5D chiplet系统迁移，二是将LLM集成到EDA流程中。虽然这些范式在良率、模块化、设计效率等方面带来前所未有的优势，但它们也极大地扩展了硬件攻击面。因此需要对这两大前沿进行统一的安全分析，以识别和防御新兴威胁。

Method: 本文采用统一分析的方法论，首先从架构级、逻辑级和物理级三个层面系统梳理针对chiplet系统（包括LLM加速硬件栈）的攻击；其次分析针对LLM驱动EDA管线的各种利用手段；随后分别综述两类系统的防御方案——借助2.5D拆分制造和有源interposer构建物理隔离的信任根（RoT）架构来保护chiplet系统，以及识别LLM驱动EDA管线中的原生威胁并综述前沿防御技术；最后探讨LLM如何反向赋能现代系统（含chiplet）的硬件安全工作。

Result: 本文在攻击面分析方面，系统地归类了chiplet系统在架构、逻辑、物理三个层级的安全威胁，以及LLM驱动EDA管线面临的各类攻击模型；在防御层面，提出了基于2.5D拆分制造和有源interposer的物理隔离信任根方案，并整理了针对LLM-EDA管线原生威胁的防御技术；同时展望了利用LLM系统推动硬件安全工作的研究方向。

Conclusion: 论文指出chiplet系统与LLM驱动EDA的双重融合在带来巨大收益的同时也大幅扩展了攻击面，需要采取物理隔离的信任根架构和针对原生威胁的防御措施来保障安全性，并强调LLM本身也可作为硬件安全分析的有利工具，为现代复杂系统的安全设计提供了统一的分析框架与防御路线。

Abstract: The semiconductor industry is undergoing a dual revolution: the shift toward heterogeneous 2.5D chiplet systems and the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Electronic Design Automation (EDA) flows. While these paradigms offer unprecedented benefits in yield, modularity, design productivity, etc., they radically expand the hardware attack surface. This paper provides a unified analysis of these frontiers, ranging from attacks on chiplet systems (including hardware stacks for LLM acceleration) across architectural, logical, and physical levels, to various exploits against LLM-driven EDA pipelines. To secure chiplet systems, we review a powerful defense approach that leverages 2.5D split manufacturing and active interposers for physically isolated Root of Trust (RoT) architectures. To secure LLM-driven EDA pipelines, we first identify native threats and then review state-of-the-art defense techniques. Finally, we discuss how LLM systems can advance hardware security efforts for modern systems, including chiplets.

</details>


### [351] [PriDyG: Privacy-preserving Dynamic Graph Inference with LLM-GNN Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04255)
*Yuyang Xia,Ruixuan Liu,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PriDyG框架，解决动态图推理中的边级差分隐私问题。通过增量式私有多跳聚合和LLM语义推理，该框架在并行组合原理下使总隐私成本等同于单次静态发布，与更新次数无关，同时避免了几何衰减预算分配带来的指数级噪声增加，在保持效用的情况下将累积隐私成本降低高达三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图上基于关系数据进行图推理会暴露敏感边信息，且动态图中重复的模型更新导致隐私损失累积，使该风险更加严重。现有方法难以在动态场景下兼顾隐私保护与模型效用，因此需要新的框架来解决这一挑战。

Method: PriDyG框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于GNN的结构学习，引入增量式私有多跳聚合，通过缓冲新到达的边并确保每条边仅被处理一次，利用并行组合原理使总隐私预算独立于更新次数和调度；(2) 基于LLM的语义推理，仅使用节点文本生成预测，不引入额外的边级隐私成本，用于补充私有化GNN输出。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的节点分类和链接预测实验中，PriDyG在相同隐私预算下始终优于几何衰减基线，且与朴素逐次更新重训练方法效用相当，同时将累积隐私成本降低高达三个数量级。此外，相比几何衰减预算分配，增量聚合避免了指数增长的噪声，同时保留精确的一跳信号和至少一半的两跳信息传递。

Conclusion: PriDyG通过增量聚合与LLM语义推理的结合，有效解决了动态图推理中的边级差分隐私问题，实现了与静态发布相当的隐私成本以及与朴素重训练相当的模型效用，为动态图上的隐私保护推理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Graph inference over relational data can expose sensitive edge information, and this risk becomes more severe in dynamic graphs, where repeated model updates cause privacy loss to accumulate. We formulate Edge-level Differentially Private Dynamic Graph Inference (EDG) and propose PriDyG, a private inference framework that combines GNN-based structural learning with LLM-based semantic reasoning. PriDyG introduces incremental private multi-hop aggregation, which buffers newly arrived edges and processes each edge exactly once. By parallel composition, the total privacy cost equals that of a single static release, independent of the number or schedule of model updates. Compared with geometrically decaying budget allocation, incremental aggregation avoids exponentially increasing noise while preserving exact one-hop signals and at least half of two-hop information transfers. PriDyG further complements privatized GNN outputs with LLM predictions derived solely from node text, incurring no additional edge-level privacy cost. Experiments on four benchmarks for node classification and link prediction show that PriDyG consistently outperforms geometrically decaying baselines under the same privacy budget and matches the utility of naive per-update retraining while reducing cumulative privacy cost by up to three orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [352] [Adversarial Attacks for Good: A Survey of Proactive Protection across the Visual Content Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04314)
*Jiaming Zhang,Boyang Chen,Zherui Li,Fuyao Zhang,Xinyu Yan,Hong Xi Tae,Wenwen He,Xuan Wang,Siqi Guo,Junhao Dong,Kun Wang,Hanxun Huang,Yige Li,Xingjun Ma,Yang Cao,Lingjuan Lyu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本篇综述提出了"对抗性攻击用于善"（adversarial attacks for good）这一保护范式，即数据所有者、创作者、平台或审计者利用原本用于攻击学习模型的扰动和结构化信号，来破坏未经授权的自动化处理或支持后续追责。论文系统梳理了五个独立发展但共同围绕视觉资产生命周期不同阶段的研究社区：共享时的隐私过滤、防止未授权训练的不可能学习示例、针对恶意编辑/模仿的生成式防护、针对自动化代理的对抗性验证码、以及用于传播后归因的溯源机制。作者从可迁移性、适应性和部署就绪度三个共享维度对五种保护族进行了统一评估，发现多数保护仅在静态或弱自适应对抗条件下得到验证，缺乏超越受控基准的现实证据，最后总结了跨阶段对抗措施和开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉内容一旦进入AI流水线，其所有者几乎丧失了对内容用途的技术控制。法律和法规补救措施虽可用于应对滥用，但许多技术干预必须在内容发布或访问时更早地应用。作者认识到五个研究社区独立地提出了"对抗性攻击用于善"的反转思路，但缺乏系统性梳理和跨社区比较，因此需要一项统一的调查来评估这一保护范式的现状与局限。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法：首先提出"对抗性攻击用于善"作为统摄性概念框架，识别出五个独立发展但互补的研究社区（隐私过滤、不可能学习示例、生成式防护、对抗性验证码、溯源机制）；然后沿视觉资产生命周期的各阶段逐一剖析五类方法的机制；最后引入三个共享评估维度——可迁移性、适应性和部署就绪度——对不同研究社区的工作进行横向比较，并归纳跨阶段的对抗措施与开放问题。

Result: 五种保护族均在不同程度上利用了人类感知、语义解释与机器推理之间的持久性差距。然而，跨生命周期评估显示，大多数保护方法仍主要针对静态或弱自适应对抗者进行验证，超越受控基准的现实证据仍然稀缺。作者据此总结出跨阶段对抗措施以及实现鲁棒、可组合、可部署的所有者侧保护所需的开放问题。

Conclusion: "对抗性攻击用于善"范式在当前视觉流水线向多模态模型和自主化代理演进的趋势下仍具持续相关性，但其实际有效性尚缺乏充分的部署级验证。论文呼吁推动不同研究社区之间的标准统一和方法互补，以实现鲁棒、可组合且可实际部署的所有者侧保护机制。

Abstract: Once visual content enters an AI pipeline, its owner often retains little technical control over how it is used. Legal and regulatory remedies can address misuse, but many technical interventions must be applied earlier, when content is released or accessed. This survey examines the protective paradigm that has grown around this intervention point, which we call \emph{adversarial attacks for good}. Perturbations and structured signals long studied as attacks on learned models are instead applied by data owners, creators, platforms, or auditors to disrupt unauthorized automation or support later accountability. Five research communities have arrived at this inversion largely independently, each addressing a different stage of a visual asset's lifecycle: privacy filters against unwanted recognition at sharing time, unlearnable examples against unauthorized training, generative safeguards against malicious editing or imitation, adversarial CAPTCHAs for access control against automated agents, and provenance mechanisms for post-circulation attribution. Although developed in separate venues with incompatible success criteria, many of these methods exploit persistent gaps between human perception, semantic interpretation, and machine inference, suggesting that the paradigm remains relevant as visual pipelines evolve toward multimodal models and autonomous agents. To make their claims comparable, we evaluate all five families along shared axes of transferability, adaptability, and deployment readiness. Across the lifecycle, we find that most protections are still validated mainly against static or weakly adaptive adversaries, while evidence beyond controlled benchmarks remains scarce. We close by consolidating cross-stage countermeasures and open problems for robust, composable, and deployable owner-side protection.

</details>


### [353] [Trident : How to Break Deep Reinforcement Learning Cyber Defenses (Agentic)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04317)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Ian Bryant,Armita Kazeminajafabadi,Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,SungHeon Jeong,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Trident框架，一个基于智能体LLM的自动化红队测试框架，通过RLVR训练可学习的规划器生成攻击策略，系统性地揭示现有基于DRL的自主网络防御系统在面对自适应威胁时的根本脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL防御系统的评估仅针对静态启发式红队agent，无法反映自适应威胁的真实攻击能力；同时RLVR技术在网络安全领域的应用缺乏合适的基准环境和交互数据集。

Method: Trident包含三个组件：1) 动态基准环境，涵盖CybORG CAGE 4和CyberWheel的隔离沙箱服务器；2) 包含超过13,000条高保真红蓝交互轨迹的数据集，用于RLVR训练；3) "Code-as-Policy"的RLVR智能体架构（Trident Agentic），采用Log Summarizer-Planner-Coder三方设计，将红队agent训练建模为上下文bandit问题，可训练的Planner从压缩执行日志生成完整攻击策略，冻结的Coder将其转化为可执行的Python策略。

Result: 仅使用单个可训练的7B参数Planner，Trident使蓝队防御性能比静态红队基线平均下降522%，并自主发现静态启发式方法完全无法捕捉的涌现行为，如诱饵规避和自适应状态优先级排序。

Conclusion: 现有DRL防御系统在面对自适应红队攻击时存在根本性的脆弱性，Trident框架填补了RLVR在网络安全领域的空白，为评估和提升自主网络防御的鲁棒性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber defense systems based on Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) have attracted significant research attention, yet remain evaluated almost exclusively against static, heuristic red agents, leaving their robustness against adaptive threats critically understudied. Meanwhile, recent advances in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) have improved LLM reasoning, but their integration into cybersecurity remains elusive due to the absence of suitable benchmark environments and interaction datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce Trident, an agentic LLM red teaming framework comprising three components: a dynamic benchmark with isolated sandbox servers spanning CybORG CAGE 4 and CyberWheel, a dataset comprises over 13,000 high-fidelity red-blue interaction trajectories for RLVR, and a ``Code-as-Policy'' RLVR agentic architecture Trident Agentic). The latter reformulates red agent training as a contextual bandit via a tripartite Log Summarizer--Planner--Coder design, where a trainable Planner generates complete attack strategies from compressed execution logs, which a frozen Coder translates into executable Python policies deployed against live DRL defenders. Empirical evaluations reveal a fundamental brittleness in existing defenses: with a single trainable 7B planner, Trident reduces blue agent defensive performance by an average of 522% compared to static red agent baselines while autonomously discovering emergent behaviors such as decoy avoidance and adaptive state prioritization that static heuristics entirely fail to uncover.

</details>


### [354] [Combating Knowledge Corruption in Agent Systems: A Byzantine-Tolerant Secure Collaborative RAG Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04366)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Daqing He,Zijian Zhang,Ye Liu,Jiamou Liu,Zhirui Zeng,Zhan Qin,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Hongwei Yao,Jincheng An,Yong Liu,Yi Li,Qi Sun,Xiulei Liu,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecureCollaRAG是一个拜占庭容错的协作式RAG框架，通过基于动态GNN的可信度评分机制实现多源知识验证，有效防御知识投毒攻击，同时保持领域知识完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）系统虽然部分解决了大语言模型的幻觉问题，但也引入了新的知识投毒攻击漏洞。攻击者通过污染RAG系统提供的文档来操纵LLM输出，现有方法缺乏对这种隐蔽知识攻击的有效防御机制。

Method: 提出SecureCollaRAG框架，采用多源知识验证机制（Multi-source Knowledge Validation Mechanism），通过动态基于图神经网络（GNN）的可信度评分系统，使智能体系统能够安全地验证文档来源，抵御隐蔽的知识投毒攻击。

Result: 通过大量评估和形式化分析，证明了SecureCollaRAG在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据分布条件下仍能保持对攻击者的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SecureCollaRAG能够有效防御RAG系统中的知识投毒攻击，在保障系统鲁棒性的同时保留关键的领域知识完整性。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation systems partially address the hallucination issues in large language models, it also introduces new vulnerabilities to knowledge corruption attacks. Adversaries exploit these vulnerabilities by poisoning documents provided by RAG system to manipulate LLM outputs. To counter this threat, we propose SecureCollaRAG, a Byzantine-tolerant collaborative RAG framework leveraging Multi-source Knowledge Validation Mechanism. Our approach enables agent system to securely verify document provenance through dynamic GNN-based credibility scoring, effectively preventing stealthy knowledge corruption attacks while preserving essential domain knowledge integrity. Through extensive evaluations and formal analysis, we demonstrate that SecureCollaRAG maintains robustness against attackers under non-IID data distributions.

</details>


### [355] [Large Language Models and Social Media Information Integrity: Opportunities, Challenges, and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04375)
*Junjie Xiong,Zhengyuan Jiang,Xiaoran Xu,Chi Zhang,Changjia Zhu,Ning Wang,Mingkui Wei,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Lingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2019至2024年间大语言模型（LLMs）在社交媒体信息完整性中的双重角色进行了系统性综述，筛选了1048篇研究并深入分析了215篇代表性论文，揭示了LLMs既能增强恶意内容检测和防御能力，又存在生成高度逼真欺骗性内容的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体平台面临虚假信息、错误信息、社交机器人及隐私问题等日益严峻的信息完整性挑战，LLMs的兴起既为应对这些挑战提供了新工具，也带来了新的风险。系统性地理解LLMs在这方面的双重影响对于制定有效的治理策略至关重要。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，从多个数据库搜集2019至2024年间相关文献，通过筛选1048项研究并深入分析215篇代表性论文，从技术能力、伦理影响和隐私关切等多个维度对LLMs在信息完整性方面的潜力与挑战进行分类和分析。

Result: 研究发现LLMs在增强恶意内容检测和开发复杂防御机制方面具有显著潜力，但同时也会带来生成高度逼真欺骗性内容的风险。研究揭示了当前方法中的关键缺口，特别是在跨语言检测、实时监控和隐私保护实现方面。

Conclusion: 本文提出未来研究方向，并为利益相关者提供利用LLMs优势同时降低社交媒体信息完整性风险的策略建议，强调在技术发展、伦理治理和政策制定之间的平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools that impact information integrity on social media platforms. This comprehensive review examines the dual role of LLMs in both facilitating and mitigating various information integrity challenges, including misinformation, disinformation, fake news, social bots, and privacy concerns. \textcolor{black}{We conduct a comprehensive review of the literature from 2019 to 2024, screening 1048 studies and performing an in-depth analysis of 215 representative papers. This systematic approach allows us to identify key patterns in how LLMs influence the information security in social media ecosystems.} Through a systematic analysis of papers from multiple databases, our findings reveal that while LLMs can enhance detection capabilities for malicious content and enable sophisticated defense mechanisms, they simultaneously pose risks by enabling the generation of highly convincing, deceptive content. We categorize and analyze the potential and challenges across different dimensions of information integrity, examining technical capabilities, ethical implications, and privacy concerns. The study demonstrates critical gaps in current approaches, particularly in cross-lingual detection, real-time monitoring, and privacy-preserving implementations. We conclude by proposing future research directions and recommendations for stakeholders to leverage LLMs while mitigating risks in social media information integrity.

</details>


### [356] [DeepInvert: Semi-Supervised Embedding Inversion Against Obfuscated Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04477)
*Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Tao Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepInvert，一种半监督嵌入反转攻击方法，能够以远高于先前方法（73.5% vs 26.2% top-1 token恢复率）的精度从混淆化嵌入中恢复原始token，揭示基于混淆的隐私防御所提供的保护远低于此前认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于混淆的防御（如ObfusLM、SentinelLMs、TextObfuscator、DPNR）被广泛用于保护云端语言模型服务中的敏感提示信息，被视为密码学方案的轻量级替代。然而作者认为这些防御提供的保护被高估了，需要系统性地验证其真实安全性。

Method: DeepInvert利用两个关键观察：(1) 未标记的混淆嵌入在扰動后仍保留可利用的语义结构；(2) 结合监督学习和无监督一致性目标，通过混合训练管道交替训练。具体包括：在标记的影子数据上进行监督训练，同时对未标记的目标嵌入施加新颖的无监督一致性约束，并提供防御感知的自适应变体以扩展到编码器架构和自回归架构下的多种混淆机制。

Result: 在九种防御、五个任务和四种模型架构上的实验表明，DeepInvert在大多数防御上优于先前攻击。针对ObfusLM，DeepInvert达到73.5%的top-1 token恢复率，而先前最优方法仅为26.2%。研究还揭示了任务依赖性张力：保持足够效用信号的混淆方案也保留足够用于反转的结构，而抵抗反转的方案则导致效用崩溃；在简单分类任务上，部分基于DP的防御可以同时维持两者。

Conclusion: 混淆类防御的实际保护强度被严重高估，其安全性与效用之间存在根本性张力。作者呼吁研究界重新评估这一防御类别的有效性。

Abstract: Cloud-based language model services routinely process prompts containing sensitive information. Obfuscation-based defenses---including ObfusLM, SentinelLMs, TextObfuscator, and DPNR---mitigate this risk by transforming prompt representations before transmission, offering a lightweight alternative to cryptographic solutions. We show these defenses provide far less protection than previously believed.
  We present DeepInvert, a semi-supervised embedding inversion attack that recovers original tokens from obfuscated representations with higher accuracy than prior methods. The key insight is that unlabeled obfuscated embeddings retain exploitable semantic structure despite perturbation. DeepInvert combines supervised training on labeled shadow data with a novel unsupervised consistency objective over unlabeled target embeddings, alternating between the two via a mixed training pipeline. Defense-aware adaptations further extend the attack to diverse obfuscation mechanisms across encoder-based and autoregressive architectures.
  Experiments on nine defenses, five tasks, and four model architectures show that DeepInvert outperforms prior attacks on most defenses. Against ObfusLM, DeepInvert achieves 73.5\% top-1 token recovery versus 26.2\% for the previous best. Our results reveal a task-dependent tension: obfuscation schemes preserving enough signal for utility also retain sufficient structure for inversion, while schemes resisting inversion collapse utility. On simpler classification tasks, some DP-based defenses can maintain both. We call for a re-evaluation of this defense class.

</details>


### [357] [Checked-In Secret Detection: Strings Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04523)
*Zhengdong Huang,Kevin Li,Jinqiu Yang,Yepang Liu,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出StringGroup上下文提取算法和基于Transformer的Secretron工具，用于检测源代码中的硬编码密钥。通过仅聚焦字符串字面量作为上下文，仅用33.2%的原始上下文保留80%以上语义信息，在SecretBench数据集上达到98.74%的F1分数，并在真实环境中成功从26个应用中发现48个未知密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码中的硬编码密钥是严重的安全漏洞，现有正则表达式方法因密钥缺乏可识别模式而精度和召回率低下；上下文感知方法面临三大挑战：混淆鲁棒性差（过度依赖易混淆的标识符）、跨语言泛化困难（训练数据分布不均）、上下文冗长嘈杂（引入过多无关token并减慢推理）。

Method: 核心洞察是字符串可作为代码语义的关键信息来源，具有优越的上下文密度、混淆鲁棒性和语言无关性。基于此提出StringGroup算法，通过挖掘潜在密钥周围的字符串来提取上下文，仅需对现有模式做简单修改（将分析范围缩小到字符串字面量）即可获得显著收益。在此基础上设计基于Transformer的Secretron检测工具。

Result: 在SecretBench数据集上达到98.74%的F1分数，在混淆和跨语言场景下表现优于基于LLM的最先进基线。仅使用33.2%的原始上下文保留了80%以上的语义信息。在真实部署中成功从26个应用中检测出48个先前未知的密钥。

Conclusion: StringGroup和Secretron证明了基于字符串字面量的上下文提取策略在密钥检测中高效且鲁棒，兼具高精度和强泛化能力，并在实际生产环境中验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: Hardcoded secrets in source code pose critical security vulnerabilities which can be easily exploited by malicious adversaries. Existing regex-based detection approaches suffer from fundamental limitations, as secrets often lack identifiable patterns, resulting in poor precision and recall. Recent studies have explored context-aware detection methods, as surrounding code can reveal the purpose of candidate strings. However, these methods confront three key challenges: (1) obfuscation robustness where models over-rely on easily obfuscated identifiers, (2) cross-language generalization difficulties due to uneven training data distribution, and (3) lengthy and noisy context that introduces excessive irrelevant tokens and slows inference. We observe that strings serve as a critical information source for code semantics, offering superior contextual density, obfuscation robustness, and language independence. Based on this insight, we propose StringGroup, a novel context extraction algorithm that mines strings surrounding potential secrets. By introducing a relatively simple modification to existing patterns that narrows the analysis specifically to string literals, the method achieves significant gains. With only 33.2% of the original context, it preserves over 80% of semantic information and significantly improves the signal-to-noise ratio for secret detection. We further design a context-aware secret detection tool, Secretron, based on StringGroup methods and Transformer model. Evaluation on the SecretBench dataset demonstrates high accuracy with 98.74% F1-score and strong robustness under obfuscation and cross-language scenarios, outperforming state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines. We deploy our tool in real-world environments and successfully detect 48 previously unknown secret keys from 26 applications, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [358] [Breadcrumbing Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04565)
*Xuebin Li,Hanqing Zhao,Siyuan Liang,Kejiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,Dacheng Tao,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM搜索代理存在一种新型安全漏洞：通过受控的搜索中介接口，攻击者可在多次查询中持续注入受控结果，形成跨轨迹的"证据链"以劫持代理决策。作者提出Authority-Chain Hijack (ACH)攻击策略，实现55.9%的攻击成功率（ASR），并通过Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution (TGSE)自动优化策略，将最强单设置下的成功率提升至71.4% ASR / 95.0% MaxN ASR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理安全研究主要关注静态网页内容注入，但现代代理会进行后续查询并交叉验证多个来源，单一被投毒页面容易被稀释或拒绝。作者动机在于揭示搜索与页面观测传输通道本身是脆弱的安全边界——攻击者不仅能投毒单个页面，还能通过受控搜索接口反复引导代理收集证据和形成最终答案，从而突破现有防御。

Method: 在受约束的工具中介威胁模型下，作者提出策略驱动的长时程攻击系统，每轮查询仅附加一个受控结果即可在代理轨迹中协调证据。具体包含两大组件：(1) Authority-Chain Hijack (ACH)——专家精炼的策略，将零散的搜索结果和页面内容操纵转化为跨多个表面上相互印证来源的一致证据链；(2) Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution (TGSE)——从执行轨迹中自动学习改进攻击策略，取代人工重新设计，实现基于轨迹的驱动式策略优化。

Result: ACH在所有基线中取得最高总体ASR，在完整SafeSearch测试集上达到55.9% ASR / 83.3% MaxN ASR；TGSE的最强单设置在最持出评估中达到71.4% ASR / 95.0% MaxN ASR。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了搜索代理的观测通道是比单一页面注入更广泛的安全攻击面，跨轨迹协调攻击能显著提升成功率，且攻击策略可通过轨迹自动进化。这为LLM搜索代理的安全防护提出了新的紧迫需求。

Abstract: LLM-based search agents are widely used for information-seeking tasks, but their reliance on external tool returns introduces a critical security risk: web content retrieved during execution is untrusted, exposing agents to prompt injection and goal hijacking. Prior work on search-agent safety primarily focuses on static web-content injection, but modern agents issue follow-up queries and cross-check competing sources, so a single injected page is often diluted or rejected. We show that the channel delivering search and page observations is a fragile security boundary: beyond exposing the agent to a single poisoned page, a mediated search interface can repeatedly steer how the agent gathers evidence and forms its final answer. Under a constrained tool-intermediary threat model, appending only one controlled result per query can substantially increase attack success when the evidence is coordinated across the agent's trajectory. We study this setting with a strategy-driven long-horizon attack system and introduce Authority-Chain Hijack (ACH), an expert-refined strategy that turns isolated search-result and page-content manipulations into a coherent evidence chain across seemingly corroborating sources. ACH achieves the highest Overall ASR among all baselines, reaching 55.9% / 83.3% ASR / MaxN ASR on the full SafeSearch test split. We further introduce Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution (TGSE), which automatically improves attacker strategies from execution traces, replacing manual redesign with trace-driven refinement; its strongest single setting reaches 71.4% / 95.0% in held-out evaluation.

</details>


### [359] [Adaptive Intrusion Detection System using Transformer-Based Neural Networks and Continual Learning Approach with Adversarial Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04602)
*Azizi Ariffin,Afif Haris,Faiz Zaki,Hazim Hanif,Nor Badrul Anuar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将表格型Transformer编码器与类别均衡经验回放缓冲器耦合的自适应入侵检测系统（IDS）框架。该框架在每个更新阶段回放良性流量以稳定决策边界，并引入了类-实例增量（CII）场景作为更真实的压力测试，同时揭示了回放缓冲器作为攻击面所面临的标签翻转和后门投毒等安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络入侵检测系统在部署后因威胁分布漂移而性能静默衰退。微调会引发灾难性遗忘，从头重训练计算成本过高。现有基于回放的持续学习方法存在两个问题：一是将良性流量不现实地限制在单一早期任务中，二是忽略了回放缓冲器本身可能成为攻击面。

Method: 提出一个耦合表格型Transformer编码器与类别均衡经验回放缓冲器的自适应IDS框架。具体包括：（1）在每个更新阶段回放良性流量以稳定决策边界；（2）引入类-实例增量（CII）场景，使良性流与新攻击共同出现作为更真实的压力测试；（3）对回放缓冲器进行显式标签翻转和隐蔽后门投毒攻击测试。

Result: 在CICIDS2017基准上，框架在传统类增量设置下达到0.9994准确率，在CII下达到0.9989准确率，遗忘极小，大幅优于顺序微调（0.0052）、EWC（0.0324）、LwF（0.0699）和iCaRL（0.8770）基线。但回放缓冲器引入严重漏洞：1%预算的标签翻转使模型准确率崩塌至0.0053；后门攻击在保持0.97整体准确率的同时，对触发流的攻击成功率达到95%，可规避标准监控。

Conclusion: 在每个经验中注入良性流量对防止遗忘至关重要，但回放缓冲器的完整性成为严格的操作要求。适度的回放预算可恢复接近联合训练的性能，但确保缓冲器安全是部署的前提条件。

Abstract: Network intrusion detection systems (IDS) trained on fixed traffic snapshots decay silently after deployment as threat distributions shift. Fine-tuning models on new attacks triggers catastrophic forgetting, while retraining from scratch is computationally infeasible. Replay-based continual learning counters this, but existing methods unrealistically confine benign traffic to a single early task and ignore the replay buffer as a potential attack surface. To address this, we present an adaptive IDS framework coupling a tabular transformer encoder with a class balanced experience replay buffer that replays benign traffic at every update to stabilize decision boundaries. We introduce the class-instance incremental (CII) scenario where benign flows reappear alongside new attacks as a more faithful stress test, and probe the buffer with overt label flipping and stealthy backdoor poisoning attacks. On the CICIDS2017 benchmark, our framework achieved 0.9994 accuracy under the traditional class incremental setup and 0.9989 under CII, with negligible forgetting, drastically outperforming sequential fine-tuning (0.0052), EWC (0.0324), LwF (0.0699), and iCaRL (0.8770) baselines. While injecting benign traffic into every experience proves essential for preventing forgetting, the replay buffer introduces critical vulnerabilities. Label-flipping collapses the model entirely (0.0053 accuracy at a 1% budget), and the backdoor maintains 0.97 overall accuracy while driving the attack success rate on trigger flows to 95%, evading standard monitoring. Ultimately, while a modest replay budget recovers near-joint-training performance, ensuring buffer integrity emerges as a strict operational requirement.

</details>


### [360] [Blockchain Empowered Trustworthy Agent Networks: Foundations, Taxonomy, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04626)
*Liehuang Zhu,Yuhang Li,Tianxing Wang,Zhihao Chen,Ke Li,Hongyi Liu,Yajie Wang,Lei Xu,Peng Jiang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了从经典多智能体系统到开放智能体网络的演进过程，聚焦于基于LLM的自主智能体、智能体互操作协议、智能体互联网基础设施及区块链赋能的可信机制，提出一个五维信任分类体系，并阐明区块链作为共享信任层而非替代品在智能体网络中的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体从孤立的任务执行者演变为可通信、可委派任务、可调用工具、能跨平台参与服务和经济的网络化自主实体，开放智能体网络应运而生。但由于不同利益相关者拥有的异构智能体缺乏共享的身份、授权、审计、声誉和结算基础设施，网络中出现了无法由单一智能体安全机制或封闭多智能体协调技术解决的信任危机，亟需系统性梳理和解决方案。

Method: 本文采用综述与教程相结合的方法：(1) 梳理1980-2026年间从经典多智能体系统到开放智能体网络的演进历程；(2) 分析信任边界的扩展路径——从个体执行到跨智能体、跨平台、跨组织交互；(3) 提出五维信任分类体系，涵盖实体与能力信任、授权与委派信任、信息与溯源信任、协调与群体鲁棒性信任、问责与结算信任；(4) 基于该分类体系，系统映射智能体网络风险、信任需求与区块链机制之间的对应关系。

Result: 本文识别出开放智能体网络中网络层面的信任危机，构建了五维信任分类框架，并系统阐述了区块链在提供共享身份、可验证授权、防篡改溯源、可审计协作、激励对齐和价值结算方面的作用，同时厘清了区块链作为共享信任层而非替代品的功能边界。

Conclusion: 区块链在开放智能体网络中应作为共享信任基础设施层发挥作用，用于提供身份、授权、溯源、审计、激励和结算等信任保障，但不应替代智能体安全、语义验证、隐私保护或鲁棒推理等关键能力。本文的五维信任分类体系为后续研究和实践提供了理论框架。

Abstract: AI agents are evolving from isolated task executors into networked autonomous entities that can communicate, delegate tasks, invoke tools, access external knowledge, and participate in cross-platform service and economic workflows. This evolution gives rise to open agent networks, where heterogeneous agents owned by different stakeholders interact without naturally shared infrastructures for identity, authorization, auditability, reputation, or settlement. This survey and tutorial article reviews the literature over the period 1980--2026 on the evolution from classical multi-agent systems to open agent networks, with a particular focus on LLM-based autonomous agents, agent interoperability protocols, Internet-of-Agents infrastructures, and blockchain-enabled trust mechanisms. We first review this evolution and show how the trust boundary expands from individual execution to cross-agent, cross-platform, and cross-organizational interaction. We then identify a network-level trust crisis that cannot be fully addressed by single-agent safety mechanisms or closed multi-agent coordination techniques, and develop a five-dimensional taxonomy covering entity and capability trust, authorization and delegation trust, information and provenance trust, coordination and group-robustness trust, and accountability and settlement trust. Based on this taxonomy, we examine how blockchain can provide shared identity, verifiable authorization, tamper-evident provenance, auditable collaboration, incentive alignment, and value settlement for trustworthy agent networks. We further synthesize the mapping between agent-network risks, trust requirements, and blockchain-enabled mechanisms, and clarify the role of blockchain as a shared trust layer rather than a replacement for agent security, semantic verification, privacy protection, or robust reasoning.

</details>


### [361] [MOAT: Model-Agnostic Randomized Transformations for preventing Efficiency Degradation Attacks on ViTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04680)
*Anadi Goyal,Nandish Chattopadhyay,Chandan Karfa,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MOAT，一种模型无关的预处理防御管道，通过组合输入变换来保护高效的Vision Transformer（ViT）实现免受针对token剪枝技术的对抗性效率攻击，且无需修改模型架构或token剪枝机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境中，token剪枝被广泛用于降低ViT的计算成本而不影响精度，但攻击者已开发出针对token剪枝技术的定向攻击，破坏了提升ViT效率的努力。需要一种防御方案来保护高效ViT实现免受此类对抗性效率攻击。

Method: 提出MOAT，一种模型无关的预处理防御管道，通过对输入应用一系列组合输入变换来对抗对抗性效率攻击。MOAT直接作用于输入，无需对模型架构或token剪枝机制进行任何修改。

Result: 实验结果表明，在所有评估的ViT模型中，MOAT将对抗攻击下的GFLOPs退化限制在原始未被攻击模型的3.4%以内。

Conclusion: MOAT能有效防御针对token剪枝的对抗性效率攻击，同时保持模型架构不变，是一种轻量级且通用的防御方案。

Abstract: To adopt the Vision Transformers (ViTs) in resource-constrained environment, token pruning is widely used to reduce computational cost without impacting accuracy. However, adversaries have developed targeted attacks against said token pruning techniques to undermine such attempts to make ViTs efficient. In this paper, we propose MOAT, a model-agnostic pre-processing defense pipeline that applies a combination of input transformations to protect efficient ViT implementations against adversarial efficiency attacks. MOAT operates directly on the input without requiring modifications to the model architecture or token pruning mechanism. Experimental results demonstrate that, across all evaluated ViT models, MOAT limits GFLOPs degradation under adversarial attacks to within 3.4% of the original unattacked model.

</details>


### [362] [LoginTrap: Uncovering Task-Agnostic Phishing-Style Indirect Prompt Injection Attacks against LLM-based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04741)
*Longtao Guo,Zelin Zhang,Kaifeng Huang,Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LoginTrap，一种针对LLM驱动的Web智能体的任务无关登录诱导攻击。攻击者仅控制网页上下文和诱导登录流程，通过类模糊测试的方式生成页面特定的间接注入攻击，使登录看似是继续任务的前提条件，从而诱导智能体登录攻击者可控的页面，实现端到端的私有数据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明恶意网页内容可以操纵Web智能体的行为，但尚未充分检验这类内容是否能诱导登录并造成端到端的私有数据泄露。登录作为涉及凭证和敏感信息的身份验证边界，是一个重要的攻击面，需要系统性研究。

Method: LoginTrap采用黑盒攻击模型，攻击者无需知道用户任务或Web智能体内部结构，仅控制网页上下文和诱导登录流程。该方法通过类模糊测试（fuzzing-inspired）过程生成页面特定的间接注入（page-specific indirect injections），使登录操作看起来是继续当前任务的合理前置条件，引导智能体前往攻击者控制的登录页面。

Result: 在多种LLM后端的真实Web智能体执行中，LoginTrap达到86%的平均端到端攻击成功率，并且在不同智能体架构和防御措施下依然有效。

Conclusion: 研究结果将登录诱导识别为系统的身份验证边界风险，并激励未来针对Web智能体的身份验证感知防御研究。

Abstract: LLM-based web agents automate user tasks by observing webpages and executing browser actions on behalf of users. As these agents operate on real web services, login becomes a sensitive authentication boundary because it involves credentials and sensitive information. Existing work shows that malicious webpage content can manipulate web agent actions, but it has not fully examined whether such content can induce login and cause end-to-end private data leakage. We study this attack surface and present LoginTrap, a task-agnostic login-inducing attack against LLM-based web agents. LoginTrap assumes a black box attacker that controls the webpage context and the induced login flow without knowing the user task or web agent internals. Under this threat model, LoginTrap uses webpage context to generate page-specific indirect injections through a fuzzing-inspired process, making login appear as a plausible prerequisite for continuing the task and guiding the agent to a controlled login page. We conduct a comprehensive analysis of LoginTrap across realistic web agent executions.
