The results show that LoginTrap reaches 86\% average end-to-end attack success across LLM backbones and remains effective across agent architectures and defenses. These findings identify login inducement as a systematic authentication boundary risk and motivate further research on authentication-aware defenses for web agents.

</details>


### [363] [Web Cache Overflow: Exploiting Imprecise Keys for Cache Degradation and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04744)
*Matteo Golinelli,Kaan Onarlioglu,Bruno Crispo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Web缓存键定义不精确导致的安全漏洞：攻击者可通过构造映射到不同缓存键但指向同一对象的请求，生成冗余缓存条目，从而降低缓存效率、增加源服务器负载，并引发依赖缓存驱逐的攻击，在五种独立缓存代理上复现了缓存降级现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web缓存通常由网站运营者定义缓存键，但这些键经常定义不精确，允许客户端构造多个映射到不同缓存键但针对同一对象的请求。这种设计缺陷可能被滥用，但目前缺乏对此类攻击的系统分析和量化研究。

Method: 作者在五种独立运行的缓存代理上复现了缓存降级现象，通过实验系统地表征了影响攻击者成本和缓存命中率的各项参数，量化分析了不同参数配置下冗余缓存条目生成对缓存效果的影响。

Result: 实验表明，在易受攻击的部署中，持续生成冗余缓存副本会降低缓存有效性并增加源服务器负载，可能促成依赖缓存驱逐的攻击，最终导致拒绝服务攻击。

Conclusion: 精确的缓存键设计是抵御此类滥用向量的最直接缓解措施，应被视为Web缓存部署的一项安全最佳实践。

Abstract: Web caches support the scalability needs of contemporary web applications by storing frequently accessed objects closer to clients. Web caches are conceptually associative arrays, tracking stored objects using cache keys consisting of HTTP request fields. However, these cache keys are often imprecisely defined by website operators. This allows clients to craft a multitude of requests that target the same object, but map to different cache keys.
  In this work, we show that request elements included unnecessarily in cache keys can be abused to create redundant cache entries. In susceptible deployments, sustained generation of such redundant copies reduces cache effectiveness and increases origin load, facilitating eviction-dependent attacks. Our experiments reproduce cache degradation across five stand-alone caching proxies and characterize how these parameters affect attacker cost and cache hit rate, potentially resulting in denial-of-service attacks. We conclude that precise cache-key design is the most direct mitigation against this abuse vector and should be recognized as a security best practice.

</details>


### [364] ["Allow" to Achieve, Over-Privileged Inadvertently: The Unintended Cost of Task-Completion-Driven Pop-up Decisions in Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04755)
*Dongsheng Chen,Yuxuan Li,Guanhua Chen,Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao,Lei Ma,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了移动GUI代理的"权限素养"（Permission Literacy）能力——即在任务执行中仅授予必要权限的能力。研究者构建了基于任务相关性和隐私风险的四级权限框架，将Android风格权限弹窗注入真实GUI任务，评估了四个前沿多模态大语言模型。研究发现：应用身份信任偏差（App-Trust Bias）和任务上下文覆盖（Task-Prior Override）会显著影响授权决策，提示干预可减少不必要授权但效果不稳定且可能抑制合法授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI代理在执行任务时频繁遭遇系统权限对话框，但其只授予任务必要权限的能力（即权限素养）从未被系统研究过。这涉及隐私安全与任务可用性的平衡，是一个亟待填补的研究空白。

Method: 1) 构建四级权限框架（基于任务相关性和隐私风险），由三位GUI代理安全专家验证评估场景；2) 将Android风格权限弹窗注入真实GUI任务；3) 使用同步标注截图和UI树层级评估四个前沿多模态大语言模型，使请求者、权限、理由和可用操作对代理可见；4) 进行受控干预实验，分别变化任务上下文和代理可见的请求者身份；5) 测试提示干预的效果。

Result: 在相同Calendar任务下，仅将请求者从Calendar改为PiMusic，授权数从26/32降至0/32，揭示出强烈的任务条件性App-Trust Bias。固定弹窗而改变任务上下文会显著改变授权决策，揭示系统性Task-Prior Override。提示干预可减少不必要的授权，但效果在模型中不一致，且可能以抑制合法授权为代价。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了GUI代理在权限授权中存在的系统性偏差，并指出将任务执行与权限授权分离是未来工作的有前景的设计方向。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents routinely encounter system permission dialogs during task execution, yet their ability to grant only permissions that are necessary for the delegated task remains largely unexamined. We present a systematic study of this capability, which we term Permission Literacy. We construct a four-level permission framework based on task relevance and privacy risk and validate the evaluated scenarios with three independent experts in GUI-agent safety. We inject Android-style permission popups into real GUI tasks and evaluate four frontier multimodal large language models using synchronized annotated screenshots and UI-tree hierarchies, making the requester, permission, justification, and available actions accessible to the agent. Beyond the main study, we conduct controlled interventions that separately vary task context and agent-visible requester identity. Under the same Calendar task, changing only the requester from Calendar to PiMusic reduces grants from 26/32 to 0/32, revealing a strong but task-conditioned App-Trust Bias. Holding a popup fixed while changing task context also substantially changes authorization decisions, revealing a systematic Task-Prior Override. Prompt interventions can reduce unnecessary grants, but their effectiveness is inconsistent across models and may come at the cost of suppressing legitimate grants. These results suggest that separating task execution from permission authorization is a promising design direction for future work.

</details>


### [365] [PURPOSE: Poisoning Conflict Resolution in RAG via Proxy-Fact-Grounded Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04756)
*Zijian Wang,Yubo Zhu,Muzhi Dong,Yanjun Lou,Yisheng Li,ZiLiang Zhang,Wei Tong,Yuan Zhang,Jingyu Hua,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PURPOSE，一种针对RAG后检索冲突消解模块的严格黑盒投毒攻击方法。与传统攻击直接断言目标答案不同，PURPOSE将注入重新框定为一种最小化冲突的"更新"，通过提取与查询相关的事实来逼近消解器的参考知识，并以此为基础锚定一个枢轴事件，使注入内容与消解器可能验证的信息保持一致性，同时引导生成器输出目标答案。在三个QA基准、五个生成器和三种冲突消解方法上，PURPOSE在45个设置中35个达到最高攻击成功率，平均ASR超过最强先前攻击9.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对RAG中冲突消解机制的黑盒投毒攻击研究不足，且现有攻击方法均以正面反驳目标答案的方式进行注入——这恰恰是冲突消解器设计用来检测的信号，导致攻击容易被识别和抵御。因此需要探索一种更隐蔽、不依赖事实冲突的攻击范式。

Method: PURPOSE采用两阶段策略：(1) 提取与查询相关的"事实"，逼近冲突消解器可能的参考知识；(2) 在这些事实基础上锚定一个"枢轴事件"，使注入内容与消解器可能验证的信息保持一致（最小化冲突），同时通过枢轴事件引导生成器朝向目标答案。该方法严格遵循黑盒假设，无需访问模型内部。

Result: 在3个QA基准、5个生成器、3种冲突消解方法的组合设置下，PURPOSE在45个设置中的35个达到最高攻击成功率（ASR），平均ASR比最强的先前攻击高出9.7个百分点，验证了非矛盾式注入作为增强投毒攻击的有效模式。

Conclusion: 研究表明非矛盾式注入是一种实用且有效的投毒攻击模式，能显著规避RAG中冲突消解机制的防御，揭示了当前RAG安全机制在对抗非冲突对抗注入时的脆弱性，为后续防御研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), post-retrieval conflict resolution arbitrates among noisy or contradictory retrieved passages. However, the robustness of this safeguard against knowledge poisoning has not been adequately studied. Existing black-box poisoning methods all assert the target answer in frontal contradiction with what the resolver treats as settled, the very signal these methods are built to detect. We propose PURPOSE, a strict black-box poisoning attack that reframes the injection as an update that minimizes conflict, rather than as a counter-claim. PURPOSE extracts query-related facts approximating the resolver's possible reference, then grounds a pivot event in them to keep the injection consistent with what the resolver might verify while steering the generator toward the target answer. Across three QA benchmarks, five generators, and three conflict-resolution methods, PURPOSE attains the highest attack success rate (ASR) in 35 of 45 settings and exceeds the strongest prior attack with +9.7 mean ASR points. These results show that our poisoning method is effective against conflict resolution in RAG and identify non-contradicting injection as a practical mode to enhance poisoning attack.

</details>


### [366] [Hidden Ciphers and Where to Find Them: Static Discovery and Assessment of Cryptographic Assets in Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04857)
*Christian Näther,Eduard Hirsch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种静态分析方法，用于在软件系统中发现和评估加密资产。作者引入了Crypto-Material、Crypto-Artifacts和Crypto-Invocations三类加密资产分类体系，基于此构建可扩展的、与扫描器无关的规则库，并实现了产生CBOM（Cryptographic Bill of Materials）输出的静态扫描器。在合成基准上F1分数为0.75，弱点/漏洞注释准确率为91%；在真实环境中，6分钟内处理57,610个文件，发现370个加密资产（含6个CVE漏洞和52个后量子迁移候选）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统依赖密码学进行数据保护、认证和信任建立，但组织往往缺乏对源代码、配置、依赖和加密文件中部署的密码学的结构化视图。这种可见性缺失使安全治理和后量子迁移规划变得复杂，亟需一种系统化的加密资产发现与评估方法。

Method: 论文提出一种静态发现与评估加密资产的方案：(1) 引入Crypto-Material、Crypto-Artifacts和Crypto-Invocations三类资产的分类体系；(2) 从分类体系中推导出可扩展的、与扫描器无关的规则库；(3) 实现应用这些规则产生CBOM导向输出的静态扫描器。评估在合成基准（含已知ground truth）和十个真实部署服务上进行。

Result: 合成基准上资产发现F1分数为0.75，91%的预期弱点和漏洞被正确标注。真实场景中，扫描器在6分钟内处理57,610个文件，发现370个加密资产，包括6个与CVE关联的漏洞和52个后量子迁移候选。真实环境覆盖率基于手动编制的参考列表评估而非穷举列表。

Conclusion: 分类驱动的静态发现方法能够为治理和后量子迁移规划提供实用的密码学透明度，验证了该方法在可扩展性和实用性方面的价值。

Abstract: Modern software systems rely on cryptography for data protection, authentication, and trust establishment, yet organizations often lack a structured view of the cryptography deployed across source code, configuration, dependencies, and cryptographic files. This lack of visibility complicates security governance and post-quantum migration planning. This paper presents a static approach for discovering and assessing cryptographic assets in software systems. We introduce a classification of Crypto-Material, Crypto-Artifacts, and Crypto-Invocations, derive an extensible scanner-independent rule repository from it, and implement a static scanner that applies these rules to produce CBOM-oriented output. We evaluate the approach on a synthetic benchmark with known ground truth and on a real-world infrastructure of ten deployed services. The scanner achieves an F1 score of 0.75 for asset discovery and correctly annotates 91% of expected weaknesses and vulnerabilities. In the realworld setting, it processes 57 610 files in under six minutes and discovers 370 cryptographic assets, including six CVE-linked vulnerabilities and 52 post-quantum migration candidates. Real-world coverage is assessed against a manually compiled reference list rather than an exhaustive one. These results show that classification-driven static discovery can provide practical cryptographic transparency for governance and post-quantum migration planning.

</details>


### [367] [HoRFFI: High-Openness RF Fingerprint Identification with a Similarity-Enhanced Variational Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04881)
*Shuiguang Zeng,Yuxiang Shen,Yuanyu Zhang,Yulong Shen,Zhiyuan Tan,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HoRFFI框架，解决高开放度射频指纹识别（RFFI）问题——即仅用少量标记基类设备训练特征提取器时，需同时支持新设备注册和未知设备拒绝。HoRFFI采用基于相似度增强的变分信息瓶颈（SVIB）监督机制，通过特征空间增强和聚类提取样本间相似度信息来辅助正则化嵌入空间，降低对训练类多样性的依赖。在LoRa和Wi-Fi数据集上，HoRFFI在新类准确率上分别比最优基线绝对提升0.112和0.288，AUC分别提升0.029和0.060。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际RFFI系统需要在仅训练少量标记基类设备的情况下，同时注册新的授权设备和拒绝未见过设备（即高开放度问题）。现有开放集识别方法通常依赖大量且多样的已知设备类学习特征空间，不适用于实际场景。

Method: 提出HoRFFI框架，核心是相似度增强的变分信息瓶颈（SVIB）监督机制。该机制通过特征空间增强和聚类生成样本间相似度信息，作为补充监督信号来正则化编码器学习的嵌入空间，降低编码器对训练类多样性的依赖，从而学习更具迁移性的嵌入表示。

Result: 在公共LoRa和Wi-Fi数据集上，HoRFFI在新类准确率上分别比最优基线取得0.112和0.288的绝对提升；在AUC指标上分别取得0.029和0.060的绝对提升。

Conclusion: HoRFFI有效解决了高开放度RFFI中可利用训练数据不足的问题，通过SVIB监督机制学习更可迁移的嵌入空间，在有限标记设备条件下实现了可扩展的设备识别和未知设备拒绝。

Abstract: Radio frequency fingerprint identification (RFFI) is a promising technique for wireless device authentication. However, practical RFFI systems must enroll newly authorized devices while rejecting previously unseen ones, even when the feature extractor is trained on only a few labeled base-device classes, giving rise to a high-openness RFFI problem. Existing open-set recognition methods typically rely on feature spaces learned from a large and diverse set of known-device classes, limiting their applicability in practical scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose HoRFFI, a high-openness RFFI framework that supports scalable device identification and unknown-device rejection using only a small number of labeled training devices. HoRFFI employs a similarity-enhanced variational information bottleneck (SVIB)-based supervision mechanism, which reduces the encoder's dependence on training-class diversity and learns a more transferable embedding space. This supervision mechanism uses feature-space augmentation and clustering to derive inter-sample similarity information, which provides supplementary supervision for regularizing the embedding space. Experiments on public LoRa and Wi-Fi datasets show that HoRFFI achieves absolute improvements of \(0.112\) and \(0.288\) in novel-class accuracy, respectively, and corresponding absolute AUC improvements of \(0.029\) and \(0.060\) over the best-performing baselines.

</details>


### [368] [When Does Latent Communication Pay? A Causal Audit of Relayed KV Caches in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04893)
*Jiaming Cheng,Subhransu Das,Rajiv Ramnath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对多智能体LLM系统中中继键值缓存（key-value cache）的"隐思想"传输声称进行了因果审计。通过将中继缓存替换为错配示例、清零和矩匹配随机版本，作者在需要与不需要发送方私有信息两种场景下系统性地检验了"配对效应"是否真实存在。结果显示，在需要私有信息时呈现天花板效应（100%），而在不需要时接近等价（2.8分内）；但缓存大小的效应并不等同于配对效应，某些系统中继通道存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统声称通过中继键值缓存而非文本实现了"隐思想"的传输，这一说法不仅是关于"是否中继"的断言，更是关于"哪个示例的缓存被中继"的断言。现有基准测试的收益差异无法直接证明存在示例特定的隐思想传递，因此需要严格的因果审计来验证这一核心声称。

Method: 采用因果审计方法，将中继的键值缓存替换为三类扰动版本：错配（不匹配示例）缓存、清零缓存、矩匹配随机缓存。在两种制度下测试：接收方是否需要发送方的私有信息。使用预注册的五种子协议，通过Holm校正的TOST（等效性检验）在GSM8K、ARC-Challenge和MedQA上验证等效性，覆盖三个Qwen3规模和一个额外的模型家族。

Result: 在需要接收方私有信息时，正确配对的中继达到100%准确率，而答案无关的中继仅23-25%，三个家族、五个检查点和一个文档问答场景均复现此对比。在不需要私有信息时，预注册协议确立约3分的等价性（基于被审计系统报告的增益锚定）；但大缓存效应不等于配对效应——清零缓存损失14.7分而错配缓存仅损失0.4分。不同系统的中继通道差异显著：LatentMAS达到天花板、KVComm部分传递、C2C无可检测的示例特定转移。

Conclusion: 基准增量本身不足以确立隐思想传输；必须通过错配缓存审计来验证配对效应。论文作者发布了这一审计工具。关键洞见是：缓存中继的总效应（cache effect）并不必须等同于示例配对效应（pairing effect），且需求（need）也不足以保证传输有效性，不同系统的中继通道在实际传递能力上存在本质差异。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems relay key--value caches instead of text and credit their gains to exchanged ``latent thoughts''. That credit is a claim about \emph{which} example's cache is relayed, not merely that one is. We audit it causally in released systems. The cache is replaced with deranged (mismatched-example), zeroed, and moment-matched random counterparts, under two regimes defined by whether the receiver needs the sender's private information. Where it does, the battery reads ceiling: 100\% against 23--25\% for answer-irrelevant relays on the primary backbone, a contrast replicated across three families, five checkpoints, and a prose document-QA surface. Where it does not, a pre-registered five-seed protocol establishes equivalence within 2.8 points, a margin anchored to the audited system's reported gain, under Holm-corrected TOST on GSM8K and ARC-Challenge across three Qwen3 scales and on MedQA at 8B (one cell shows a small detected advantage inside the margin); a second family shows no detected advantage. A large cache effect need not be a pairing effect. In one natural cell, zeroing the relay costs 14.7 points; a mismatched cache, 0.4. Nor is need sufficient: under the same test, delivered channels span ceiling (LatentMAS's native relay), partial (KVComm's layer subset), and no detected example-specific transfer (C2C's released projector). Benchmark deltas do not by themselves establish latent-thought transmission; establishing it takes a mismatched-cache audit, which we release.

</details>


### [369] [Toward Practical Decentralized Proof-of-Location via Physical Witnessing Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04957)
*Tamor Tomson,Eduardo Brito,Amnir Hadachi,Ulrich Norbisrath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于去中心化位置证明（PoL）的理论架构，用低成本硬件实现了从仿真到物理原型的跨越。原型构建了一个"见证区"（witnessing zone），由固定邻近设备测量证明者的存在、通过本地网状网络交换声明，并记录在防篡改账本中。室内受控评估表明系统能生成准确低延迟的证明对象，同时检测模拟重放和恶意测距攻击，为下一代数字信任基础设施提供了可复用的实验基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字服务越来越依赖"某人在特定时间位于特定地点"的声明，但传统自报位置数据容易被伪造且事后难以验证。位置证明（PoL）系统旨在将存在声明转化为独立验证者可检查的证据。本文的动机是将去中心化PoL的理论研究从仿真推进到实际物理原型，以在真实无线电、网络和时序条件下验证其可行性。

Method: 构建一个见证区原型：固定邻近设备作为见证者来测量证明者的存在，通过本地mesh网络交换位置声明，并将其记录在防篡改账本中。从抽象协议到物理实现需要三个关键适配：(1) 见证者发起的测距（witness-initiated ranging）；(2) 跨见证者一致性检查（cross-witness consistency checks）；(3) 针对重放攻击的新鲜度绑定（freshness binding）。

Result: 受控室内评估显示，系统能够生成准确且低延迟的证明对象，并能有效检测模拟重放攻击和恶意测距攻击。同时揭示了剩余的校准、验证者独立性、无线电完整性和扩展性等方面的需求。

Conclusion: 该工作为去中心化位置证据提供了一个可复用的实验基线，证明了物理存在声明可以在真实无线电、网络和时序条件下被独立检查，并指出了下一代数字信任基础设施中仍需解决的关键工程挑战。

Abstract: Digital services increasingly rely on claims that a person, device, or asset was in a specific place at a specific time. Today, those claims often depend on self-reported location data, which is easy to falsify and difficult to verify after the fact. Proof-of-Location (PoL) systems address this gap by turning presence claims into evidence that an independent verifier can later inspect. This paper builds upon recent theoretical work on decentralized PoL architectures and demonstrates how they can move from emulation to a physical prototype built with low-cost hardware. We implement a witnessing zone in which fixed nearby devices measure a prover's presence, exchange claims over a local mesh, and record them in a tamper-evident ledger. Building the prototype required adapting the abstract protocol to physical constraints through witness-initiated ranging, cross-witness consistency checks, and freshness binding against replay. Our controlled indoor evaluation shows that the system can produce accurate, low-latency proof objects while detecting simulated replay and malicious-ranging attacks. The result is a reusable experimental baseline for next-generation digital trust infrastructure, that exposes the remaining calibration, verifier-independence, radio-integrity, and scaling requirements for decentralized location evidence, where physical presence claims can be independently checked under real radio, networking, and timing conditions.

</details>


### [370] [When Do PEFT Adaptations Leak Structure? Measuring Black-Box Structural Bounds in Public-Base Model Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05036)
*Zhongjiang Yao,Shuangshuang Liang,Chun Yang,LiWei Chen,Gang Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VectorHijack-SR测量方法，通过配对受害者/基座模型残差来估计PEFT（参数高效微调）服务的结构泄露，包括系列、层位置和秩范围。实验表明在分类骨干网络上存在显著的结构泄露，且已知基座的高输出PEFT服务可泄露可操作的结构和版本信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务越来越多地部署带有私有参数高效适配（PEFT）的公开基础模型，当审计者或攻击者可以在本地执行公开基座模型并观察受害者输出时，会产生差异化信息泄露风险。作者旨在量化这种结构性泄露风险，并区分元数据可见性、开放世界有效性和操作可利用性。

Method: 提出VectorHijack-SR测量方法论，将配对的受害者/基座残差转化为校准的结构边界（PEFT系列、层局部性、粗略秩）。聚合查询级幅度、排序、熵、边界、长度、模板、局部性和谱统计为服务级表示。使用服务不相交分类器量化结构证据，并用交叉拟合的层次拒绝器评估受害者是否位于校准的LoRA流形之外。

Result: BERT/MNLI、RoBERTa/MNLI和DeBERTa-v3/MNLI及AG News上系列泄露显著超均匀机会；秩推断任务依赖（BERT/MNLI和DeBERTa/AG News达8/9，DeBERTa/MNLI为4/9与机会兼容）。十种子BERT开放集网格上拒绝器pooled AUROC 0.804，已知准确率0.956，但在结构相近的DoRA和LoRA+head变体上有限。五个held-out LoRA-r64服务的精确版本关联AUC达0.940。可见性-可利用性差距明显：两阶段恢复无公平预算查询节省，后验选择PEFT不如蒸馏-转换PEFT（0.356 vs 0.517），自由运行生成接近机会水平。

Conclusion: 已知基座、丰富输出的PEFT服务可以泄露可操作的结构和版本信息，但仅凭闭集置信度并不能建立通用的适配器恢复能力。这揭示了PEFT服务的结构泄露风险与操作可利用性之间存在显著差距。

Abstract: Services increasingly deploy public foundation models with private parameter-efficient adaptations, creating a differential information leakage risk when auditors or adversaries can execute the public base model locally and observe victim outputs. We present VectorHijack-SR, a measurement methodology that converts paired victim/base residuals into calibrated structural bounds over PEFT family, layer locality, and coarse rank, while separating metadata visibility from open-world validity and operational exploitability. Our estimator aggregates query-level magnitude, ranking, entropy, margin, length, template, locality, and spectral statistics into service-level representations. A service-disjoint classifier quantifies structural evidence, and a cross-fitted hierarchical rejector evaluates whether a victim lies outside the calibrated LoRA manifold. Across classification backbones, family leakage exceeds uniform chance on BERT/MNLI (8/12), RoBERTa/MNLI (21/24), and DeBERTa-v3 on MNLI (12/18) and AG News (15/18). Rank inference is task dependent: BERT/MNLI and DeBERTa/AG News reach 8/9, whereas DeBERTa/MNLI achieves 4/9 and is statistically compatible with chance after correction. On a ten-seed BERT open-set grid, the rejector achieves pooled AUROC 0.804 (95% CI [0.660, 0.927]) and known accuracy 0.956, but remains limited on structurally close DoRA and LoRA+head variants. Exact-version linkage on five held-out LoRA-r64 services reaches AUC 0.940. Experiments reveal a visibility-exploitability gap: two-stage recovery provides no fair-budget query savings, posterior-selected PEFT underperforms distill-then-convert PEFT (0.356 vs. 0.517), and free-running generation remains near chance. These results show that known-base, rich-output PEFT services can leak actionable structural and version information, while closed-set confidence alone does not establish universal adapter recovery.

</details>


### [371] [Private Direct Preference Optimization for LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05040)
*Yangfan Jiang,Fei Wei,Ergute Bao,Xiaokui Xiao,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出偏好隐私（preference privacy）概念和PrivDPO方法，用于在不损害大语言模型DPO训练效果的前提下保护偏好标签的隐私。PrivDPO仅沿着由文本决定的一维偏好轴添加校准随机性，避免了传统差分隐私方法中的过量噪声注入和偏置更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO是当前LLM对齐的标准方法，但其中人类偏好标签反映主观判断，可能泄露标注者或终端用户的敏感属性。现成的隐私保护方法与DPO结构不匹配，会导致不必要的噪声注入和训练偏差，因此亟需专门针对DPO偏好信号设计的隐私保护方案。

Method: 首先形式化偏好隐私（preference privacy）——一种标签DP风格的隐私概念，假设攻击者已知提示和响应，仅保护响应之间的相对偏好。然后设计PrivDPO，关键观察是：对于仅偏好信号不同的相邻样本，梯度差位于由文本唯一确定的一维偏好轴上，所有偏好信息都流经该轴。PrivDPO仅沿此轴通过无偏的随机重缩放DPO目标添加校准随机性，避免逐样本梯度操作。

Result: 在三个对齐基准和三个LLM家族上的实验表明，与隐私保护基线相比，PrivDPO持续实现了强隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: PrivDPO是一种与大规模LLM训练兼容的、专为DPO偏好信号设计的隐私保护方法，能够在有效保护偏好隐私的同时维持良好的模型对齐性能。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization (DPO) is now a standard method for aligning large language models (LLMs) using human preference data. Each DPO example contains a prompt and a pair of candidate model responses. While prompts and responses are often public or model-generated, the relative preference between responses reflects subjective judgments and can reveal sensitive attributes of annotators or end users. Off-the-shelf privacy-preserving approaches are not well matched to this structure, leading to unnecessary noise injection and biased updates in training.
  In this paper, we formalize preference privacy, a label-DP-style privacy notion for DPO that protects only the relative preference between candidate responses, assuming an adversary who already knows the prompt and responses. We then design PrivDPO, a DPO variant that enforces preference privacy while remaining compatible with large-scale LLM training. Our main observation is that, for neighboring examples differing only in their preference signal, the gradient difference lies on a one-dimensional preference axis determined solely by the text; all preference information flows through this axis. PrivDPO adds calibrated randomness only along this axis via an unbiased randomized rescaling of the DPO objective, avoiding per-example gradient operations. Our experiments on three alignment benchmarks and three LLM families show that PrivDPO consistently achieves strong privacy-utility trade-offs compared with privacy-preserving baselines.

</details>


### [372] [Gradient Immunity: Null-Space Resistance to Malicious Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05045)
*Yuxuan Huang,Xingyu Zeng,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向开放权重模型的安全防护机制——单向安全门（USG），通过在最终Transformer层后插入Null Space Cubic Layer和Inverse Adapter，在下游微调时抑制有害样本的梯度传播，使模型在保留大部分可训练权重的同时，将微调后的攻击成功率维持在发布前水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐后的LLM在下游微调时仍易受恶意攻击。已有防御方法主要针对微调即服务（FTaaS）范式或依赖下游用户配合安全流程，无法解决提供方控制的部分保护开放权重（PPOW）发布场景——即大多数权重可训练、仅少量安全关键组件受保护的设置。

Method: 提出Unidirectional Safety Gate（USG），由Null Space Cubic Layer和Inverse Adapter组成，插入最终Transformer层之后。Cubic Layer在微调时抑制或阻断落入校准保护区域的有害样本的梯度；Inverse Adapter用于恢复基座模型的前向行为。通过防御方持有的有害数据校准阈值，使保护泛化到邻近的分布内有害样本。

Result: 在六种模型-数据集组合上，USG在固定发布阈值下使微调后的攻击成功率接近发布前水平，同时在较简单设置上保持高安全通过率，并在BeaverTails不安全样本上展现出更清晰的安全-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 发布时在表示空间进行阻断可以提高下游恶意适配的攻击成本，无需下游用户配合，为开放权重发布提供了可行的安全防护方案。

Abstract: Released aligned large language models remain vulnerable to malicious downstream finetuning. Existing defenses are largely designed for the fine-tuning-as-a-service (FTaaS) paradigm or rely on downstream users to follow additional safety procedures, and therefore do not directly address the setting we study: a provider controlled partially protected open-weight (PPOW) release setting in which most weights remain trainable while a small safety-critical component is preserved at release. We propose a Unidirectional Safety Gate (USG), instantiated as a Null Space Cubic Layer together with an Inverse Adapter inserted after the final Transformer layer. During downstream fine-tuning, the cubic layer suppresses or blocks gradients from harmful samples whose hidden states fall in a calibrated protected region, while the Inverse Adapter restores the base model's forward behavior. In practice, we calibrate a threshold using defender-held harmful data, allowing protection to generalize to nearby in-distribution harmful samples. Across six evaluated model-dataset settings, USG keeps post-finetuning attack success rate close to the pre-release level under a fixed release threshold, while maintaining high safe-pass rates on easier settings and exhibiting a clearer safety-utility trade-off on unsafe samples from BeaverTails. These results suggest that release-time representation-space blocking can raise the cost of malicious downstream adaptation without requiring downstream cooperation. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/Gradient-Immunity.

</details>


### [373] [Agent Against Agent: An Agentic System for Automatic Prompt Injection Red Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05108)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Runpeng Geng,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PIMiner是一个用于提示注入红队测试的智能体系统，通过训练构建策略库，可在测试时将策略直接迁移到未见过的目标LLM，无需额外训练，仅需少量查询即可实现强攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入对LLM智能体构成严重安全威胁，需要高效且有效的红队测试来评估风险和收集训练数据以改进防御。现有的基于强化学习的提示注入红队方法产生的攻击模型通常对新目标LLM泛化能力差，因此需要一种能更好地跨模型迁移的红队方法。

Method: PIMiner采用智能体系统方案：训练阶段在序列化的（数据集、目标模型）对上训练，从头构建策略库；测试阶段将学到的策略库直接迁移到未见过的目标LLM，无需额外训练，每个测试样本仅需约10次查询。

Result: 在IPIArena上，对Gemini-2.5-Pro、GPT-5.1和Claude-Sonnet-4.5分别达到76.2%、61.9%和42.9%的攻击成功率（ASR）；在AgentDojo上，对这三个模型分别达到86.7%、53.3%和40.0%的ASR。

Conclusion: PIMiner展示了通过策略库构建实现跨目标模型直接迁移的红队测试能力，在保证高攻击成功率的同时所需查询数量极少，为LLM智能体提示注入风险评估提供了一种高效且泛化的新方法。

Abstract: Prompt injection poses significant security risks to LLM agents. Efficient and effective red-teaming is therefore critical, both for evaluating these risks and for collecting training data to improve defenses. Existing state-of-the-art prompt injection red-teaming methods primarily rely on reinforcement learning (RL), producing attacker models that often generalize poorly to new target LLMs. In this work, we develop PIMiner, an agentic system for prompt injection red-teaming. During training, PIMiner is trained on a sequence of (dataset, target model) pairs and builds a strategy library from scratch. At test time, the learned strategy library can be directly transferred to a previously unseen target LLM without additional training. PIMiner requires only a small number of queries to a target agent (e.g., 10) per test sample. Experimental results demonstrate that PIMiner achieves strong performance. On IPIArena, it attains a 76.2% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 61.9% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 42.9% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5. On AgentDojo, it achieves an 86.7% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 53.3% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 40.0% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5.

</details>


### [374] [Temporal Context Awareness: A Defense Framework Against Multi-turn Manipulation Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15560)
*Prashant Kulkarni,Assaf Namer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出时间上下文感知（TCA）框架，一种针对LLM多轮对话操纵攻击的新型防御机制，通过分析语义漂移、跨轮次意图一致性和对话模式演变来检测和缓解操纵行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型面临日益复杂的多轮对话操纵攻击，攻击者通过看似良性的对话轮次逐步构建上下文以绕过安全措施，而现有的单轮检测方法无法有效应对这种利用对话时间特性的攻击，构成严重的实际部署安全威胁。

Method: TCA框架包含三个核心组件：动态上下文嵌入分析（持续追踪语义漂移）、跨轮次一致性验证（检验意图一致性）、渐进式风险评分（评估对话演变的威胁程度）。此外还系统分析了多种攻击向量及其在多轮对话中的演进过程。

Result: 在模拟对抗场景中的初步评估表明，TCA框架能够识别传统检测技术常遗漏的微妙操纵模式，展示其在对话式AI系统中提供安全层的潜力。代码实现已公开供后续研究使用。

Conclusion: 研究强调了对话式AI系统迫切需要稳健的上下文感知防御机制，TCA框架为在保持LLM在合法应用中的实用性的同时保障安全性提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly vulnerable to sophisticated multi-turn manipulation attacks, where adversaries strategically build context through seemingly benign conversational turns to circumvent safety measures and elicit harmful or unauthorized responses. These attacks exploit the temporal nature of dialogue to evade single-turn detection methods, representing a critical security vulnerability with significant implications for real-world deployments.
  This paper introduces the Temporal Context Awareness (TCA) framework, a novel defense mechanism designed to address this challenge by continuously analyzing semantic drift, cross-turn intention consistency and evolving conversational patterns. The TCA framework integrates dynamic context embedding analysis, cross-turn consistency verification, and progressive risk scoring to detect and mitigate manipulation attempts effectively. Preliminary evaluations on simulated adversarial scenarios demonstrate the framework's potential to identify subtle manipulation patterns often missed by traditional detection techniques, offering a much-needed layer of security for conversational AI systems. In addition to outlining the design of TCA , we analyze diverse attack vectors and their progression across multi-turn conversation, providing valuable insights into adversarial tactics and their impact on LLM vulnerabilities. Our findings underscore the pressing need for robust, context-aware defenses in conversational AI systems and highlight TCA framework as a promising direction for securing LLMs while preserving their utility in legitimate applications. We make our implementation available to support further research in this emerging area of AI security.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [375] [Hardware-Enabled Fuzzy Inference: Architectures, Platforms, and Emerging Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04031)
*Amir Hossein Jalilvand,Parsa Hassani Shariat Panahi,M. Hassan Najafi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本综述首次以平台为中心系统梳理了硬件模糊系统领域，涵盖FPGA、ASIC/VLSI、嵌入式及TinyML三大类别，通过跨平台对比分析揭示了各平台的优劣权衡，并指出了标准化基准缺失、规则库扩展性有限、设计自动化不足等关键研究空白及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件模糊推理在实时边缘智能场景下存在延迟不可预测、功耗过高和资源利用率低等问题，亟需硬件加速方案，但现有研究分散在不同平台和技术路径中，缺乏系统性梳理和跨平台对比的综合性综述。

Method: 采用平台中心（platform-centric）的分类框架，将文献系统性地组织为三大类：基于FPGA的实现、ASIC与定制VLSI实现、嵌入式/IoT/TinyML平台。对每一类别分析其架构组织、资源映射策略、实现权衡和关键设计挑战，并进行跨平台对比分析。

Result: 跨平台对比显示没有任何单一平台在所有指标上占优：FPGA提供灵活性和快速原型验证，ASIC提供峰值性能和能效，嵌入式与TinyML系统在低功耗和成本之间取得平衡。同时识别出关键研究空白：缺乏标准化基准、规则库扩展性有限、设计自动化不足、对在线学习和新兴存储器技术支持有限。

Conclusion: 未来研究方向包括：基于忆阻交叉阵列的内存内模糊计算、与TinyML生态系统的集成、可解释硬件AI以及开源设计自动化工具，该综述可作为硬件模糊智能领域研究者和从业者的参考指南。

Abstract: Fuzzy logic systems are widely used for intelligent decision-making under uncertainty, offering interpretability and robustness across diverse applications. However, the growing demand for real-time edge intelligence has exposed the limitations of software-based fuzzy inference: unpredictable latency, excessive power consumption, and inefficient resource utilization. This has motivated extensive research into hardware acceleration, spanning platforms from custom analog circuits and digital ASICs to reconfigurable FPGAs and ultra-low-power microcontrollers. This survey presents the first comprehensive, platform-centric review of hardware fuzzy systems, systematically organizing the literature into three principal categories: FPGA-based implementations, ASIC and custom VLSI realizations, and embedded, IoT, and TinyML platforms. For each category, we analyze architectural organization, resource mapping strategies, implementation trade-offs, and key design challenges.
  Our cross-platform comparative analysis reveals that no single platform dominates across all metrics. FPGAs offer flexibility and rapid prototyping, ASICs deliver peak performance and energy efficiency, while embedded and TinyML systems balance low power and cost for edge deployment. Despite significant progress, critical research gaps persist: the absence of standardized benchmarks, limited scalability of rule bases, insufficient design automation, and limited support for online learning and emerging memory technologies. We outline future directions including in-memory fuzzy computing with memristive crossbars, integration with TinyML ecosystems, explainable hardware AI, and open-source design automation. This survey serves as a {reference for} researchers and practitioners working on hardware-enabled fuzzy intelligence.

</details>


### [376] [EDATracer: An Agentic Framework for Large-Scale EDA Artifact Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04032)
*Phat Tieu,Sayanti Jana,Matthew DeLorenzo,Jiawen Wu,Narendran Srinivasan,Srinivas Shakkottai,Jiang Hu,Jeyavijayan Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出EDATracer，一个面向芯片设计自动化（EDA）工件分析的智能体框架，通过知识图谱与语义向量索引相结合的方式，帮助大语言模型代理在源代码、日志、网表、报告等多类型工件中检索证据。基于2,787个开源芯片设计的18.9GB数据集，构建了90题的基准测试，在pass@1准确率上优于Cursor和Claude Code 6.4和7.2个百分点，同时节省2.0-3.2倍token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代芯片设计依赖EDA工具生成大量异构工件（源代码、脚本、日志、网表和报告），跨工件类型和设计阶段的分析对于调试、优化和设计流程理解至关重要，但现有LLM代理方案缺乏公开的大规模跨工件分析基准，且难以将推理扎根于工具生成的证据中。

Method: EDATracer将设计工件组织为知识图谱，并配以语义向量索引，使LLM代理能够在源代码、日志、网表和报告之间检索证据。基于2,787个可综合的开源芯片设计构建了18.9GB的数据集，并设计了涵盖事实性、统计性和推理性任务的90题基准。

Result: 在评估的多个代理中，EDATracer取得了最优的pass@1准确率，平均比Cursor高6.4个百分点、比Claude Code高7.2个百分点，同时token消耗减少2.0-3.2倍。

Conclusion: EDATracer通过知识图谱与语义检索结合的方式，有效提升了LLM代理在EDA跨工件分析中的准确性和效率，为EDA领域的大规模工件分析提供了公开基准和可行的智能体方案。

Abstract: Modern chip design relies on electronic design automation (EDA) tools that generate large, heterogeneous artifacts, including source files, scripts, logs, netlists, and reports. Analyzing these artifacts is critical for debugging, optimization, and design-flow understanding, but remains difficult because relevant evidence is often distributed across many artifact types and design stages. Although LLM agents show promise for EDA assistance, existing approaches lack public benchmarks for large-scale cross-artifact analysis and often struggle to ground reasoning in tool-generated evidence. We present EDATracer, an agentic framework for evidence-grounded EDA artifact analysis. EDATracer organizes design artifacts into a knowledge graph paired with a semantic vector index, enabling LLM agents to retrieve evidence across source files, logs, netlists, and reports. We curate an 18.9 GB dataset of 2,787 synthesizable open-source chip designs and introduce a 90-question benchmark spanning factual, statistical, and reasoning tasks. Across evaluated agents, EDATracer achieves the best pass@1 accuracy, outperforming Cursor and Claude Code by 6.4 and 7.2 percentage points on average, while using 2.0-3.2x fewer tokens.

</details>


### [377] [CheckOne: Lightweight Fault Detection and Mitigation for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04035)
*Mohammad Hasan Ahmadilivani,Sven-Markus Loorits,Jaan Raik*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出CheckOne，一种用于Vision Transformer（ViT）的轻量级故障检测与缓解方法，相比传统ABFT方法大幅降低计算成本，可将关键故障缓解高达26倍，平均性能比ABFT提升3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在安全关键应用中的广泛部署引发了对硬件故障可靠性的担忧。虽然ABFT方法被用作DNN的轻量级对称保护机制，但由于ViT计算量巨大，传统ABFT在ViT上应用尤为困难，因此需要更高效的故障保护方法。

Method: 本文首先全面评估了ViT的可靠性，强调其各层需要对称保护。在此基础上，提出了CheckOne——一种新颖且成本高效的ViT故障检测与缓解方法，通过显著降低计算开销来替代传统ABFT。

Result: 在多个ViT模型上的大量实验表明，CheckOne可将关键故障缓解高达26倍，并且平均性能比传统ABFT在ViT上的表现提升3.8倍。

Conclusion: CheckOne为实现ViT的硬件故障保护提供了一种更为经济高效的技术路径，验证了在降低计算成本的同时仍能有效提升ViT可靠性的可行性。

Abstract: The wide adoption of Vision Transformers (ViTs) in safety-critical applications raises reliability concerns related to hardware faults. Algorithm-Based Fault Tolerance (ABFT) methods have emerged as lightweight and symmetric protection mechanisms for DNNs. However, they are particularly challenging for ViTs due to their significant computational requirements. This work comprehensively evaluates the reliability of ViTs, emphasizing the need for symmetric protection in their layers. Furthermore, we present CheckOne, a novel, cost-effective method for fault detection and mitigation in ViTs that significantly reduces the computational cost compared to conventional ABFT. Through extensive experiments with multiple ViTs, CheckOne mitigates critical faults by up to $26\times$ and achieves an average 3.8x higher performance than ABFT in ViTs.

</details>


### [378] [A Symbolic Execution Framework for Symbolic Timing Analysis of Digital Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04036)
*Dennis Eigner,Arman Ferdowsi,Ulrich Schmid*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解析门延迟模型的符号执行框架，用于数字集成电路的动态时序分析（DDTA），能够自动计算电路中所有路径的符号延迟表达式，并引入符号剪枝方法来缓解组合爆炸问题，支持路径敏感的目标驱动时序推理和电路路径的解析优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模拟SPICE仿真虽然精确但速度慢，而基于仿真的动态时序分析（DDTA）虽然速度快但在精度上需要超越标准纯延迟或惯性延迟模型的更先进门延迟模型。近期的解析门延迟模型（能捕捉倾斜效应和多输入切换等）为超越仿真方法、向穷举探索方向发展的时序分析带来了新的可能性。

Method: 提出一个新颖的符号执行框架，利用解析门延迟模型，在给定输入转换顺序的条件下自动计算数字电路中所有路径的符号延迟表达式。为缓解组合爆炸问题，引入符号剪枝方法，支持路径敏感的目标驱动推理以及特定电路路径的解析优化。

Result: 该框架能够穷举探索电路中所有路径的时序行为，并通过符号剪枝有效控制计算复杂度，实现对时序性质和特定路径的解析优化。

Conclusion: 该符号执行框架代表了从仿真驱动的DDTA向穷举式、解析驱动的时序分析范式的转变，有望提供更全面、可优化的时序分析方法。

Abstract: Simulation-based dynamic timing analysis of digital integrated circuits (DDTA) offers a faster alternative to traditional analog SPICE simulations. To achieve timing predictions that are reasonably competitive in terms of accuracy, however, DDTA mandates gate delay models that go beyond the standard pure or inertial delay models used in state-of-the-art tools. Recent advances in analytic gate delay models, which now also capture effects like drafting and multi-input switching, unlock new possibilities for timing analysis, which go way beyond simulation-based approaches towards an exhaustive exploration. In this paper, we present the cornerstones of a novel symbolic execution framework, which utilizes such analytic delay models for automatically computing symbolic delay expressions for all paths in a digital circuit, for some given ordering of the input transitions. To reduce combinatorial explosion, we introduce symbolic pruning methods that also enable path-sensitive, goal-driven reasoning about timing properties and analytic optimization of specific circuit paths.

</details>


### [379] [On Design Principles for Efficient Heterogeneous DRAM-PIM-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04169)
*Corey Lammie,Hadjer Benmeziane,William Andrew Simon,Irem Boybat*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了异构DRAM-PIM-GPU系统在LLM解码阶段推理中的性能，揭示了三个关键设计原则：静态功耗（DRAM泄漏、刷新和GPU空闲功耗）会显著影响效率（动态功耗模型会高估最高3.85X）；解码性能随通道数单调非递减并在高通道数趋于饱和，且所有模型在固定容量扫描下共享一个近最优层级配置；工作负载映射策略只能提供有限的改进（最多14.0%/17.4%内核级延迟/能耗，5.6%端到端收益），不是主要瓶颈。真正的效率提升需要系统级联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构DRAM-PIM-GPU系统设计实践忽略了影响真实世界性能的关键因素（如静态功耗、通道数配置、层级配置和工作负载映射），导致效率评估失真。论文旨在通过系统化评估揭示这些被忽视因素的重要性，为下一代内存加速LLM系统设计提供指导。

Method: 作者对多种架构（OPT-7B/70B、Mamba2-2.7B/70B）和工作负载进行了系统化评估，涵盖不同batch size、输入/输出token数量配置。通过扫描通道数、固定容量下的层级配置以及不同工作负载映射策略，量化各设计维度对延迟、能耗和tokens/s/W等指标的影响，从而提炼设计原则。

Result: 1) 静态功耗在真实部署中可能主导效率计算，动态功耗模型对Mamba2-2.7B（batch=1, 128输入token, 2048输出token）高估tokens/s/W最高达3.85X；2) 解码性能随通道数单调非递减，低batch工作负载在高通道数下趋于饱和；固定容量扫描下所有模型共享近最优层级配置，但注意力模型对配置错误的惩罚显著更大；3) 工作负载映射最多提供5.6%端到端改进，非主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 显著效率提升需要系统级联合优化（系统级协同优化），而非局部设计的改进。所揭示的三个设计原则为架构师在设计下一代内存加速LLM系统时提供了清晰的设计空间指导。

Abstract: Heterogeneous DRAM-based processing-in-memory (PIM)-GPU systems promise significant efficiency gains for decode-phase large language model (LLM) inference, particularly in long-output generation, yet current design practices overlook critical factors that determine real-world performance. Through systematic evaluation of diverse architectures and workloads (OPT-7B/70B, Mamba2-2.7B/70B), we reveal three fundamental design principles: (i) static power consumption (DRAM leakage, refresh, and GPU idle power) can dominate the efficiency calculus, causing dynamic-only models to overestimate tokens/s/W by up to 3.85X for realistic deployments (Mamba2-2.7B, batch size 1, 128 input tokens, and 2,048 output tokens); (ii) decoding performance is monotonically non-decreasing with channel count across all evaluated models and workloads, generally plateauing at high channel counts for low-batch workloads; under a fixed-capacity sweep, all models instead share a common near-optimal hierarchy configuration, with substantially larger misconfiguration penalties for attention-based models; (iii) workload mapping strategies provide bounded improvements (up to 14.0%/17.4% kernel-level latency/energy reduction, up to 5.6% end-to-end gain) and are not primary bottlenecks. Significant efficiency gains require system-wide co-optimization. These principles provide design-space guidance for architects designing the next generation of memory-accelerated LLM systems.

</details>


### [380] [A Centralized Performance Monitoring Architecture for Heterogeneous Multicore SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04247)
*Mohammed Sajjad Jafri,Abdur Rahman,Emon Sarkar,Mahdi Hassen,Gopishankar Thayyil,Ashwin Krishna Mani,Rodolfo Pellizzoni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集中式性能监控架构，通过Event Monitoring Units (EVUs)捕获微架构事件并转发至Advanced Performance Monitoring Unit (APMU)，以解决现代嵌入式SoC中分布式HPC模块导致的采集、同步和关联困难问题，并在RISC-V的AXI4平台上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代嵌入式SoC中，不同组件（处理器、加速器、互连、内存控制器）各自实现独立的异构HPC模块，形成分布式监控基础设施，需要多个软件接口，导致跨组件性能数据的采集、同步和关联变得复杂。

Method: 提出集中式性能监控架构，引入Event Monitoring Units (EVUs)捕获并转发微架构事件到中央的Advanced Performance Monitoring Unit (APMU)；APMU集成可编程计数器与专用处理单元，支持灵活的事件驱动软件机制；在AXI4系统上实现并集成到RISC-V SoC平台。

Result: 通过实时资源调节、应用剖析和计数器归因三个案例研究，证明了该架构在跨组件性能监控方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 集中式性能监控架构能够高效地在多硬件组件之间收集、关联和处理架构事件，弥补了RISC-V SoC平台缺乏高级跨组件性能监控支持的不足。

Abstract: Hardware Performance Counters (HPCs) are widely used to enable event-driven software mechanisms such as profile guided optimization, performance analysis, and dynamic resource management in real-time systems. However, in modern embedded System-on-a-Chip (SoCs), different components - including processors, accelerators, interconnects, and memory controllers - typically implement separate and heterogeneous HPC modules. This distributed monitoring infrastructure requires multiple software interfaces and complicates the collection, synchronization, and correlation of performance data across the system. In this work, we propose a centralized performance monitoring architecture to efficiently collect, correlate, and process architectural events across multiple hardware components. Our design introduces Event Monitoring Units (EVUs) that capture and forward microarchitectural events to an Advanced Performance Monitoring Unit (APMU). The APMU integrates programmable counters and a specialized processing element to support flexible, event-driven software mechanisms. We implement our design for an AXI4-based system and integrate it into a RISC-V based SoC platform, which lacks advanced cross-component performance monitoring support. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through case studies on real-time resource regulation, application profiling, and counter attribution.

</details>


### [381] [Deltoris: Enabling Real-time VLA Inference in Embodied AI via Bit-level Sparsity and Speculative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04428)
*Zheng Liu,Zeyu Guo,Zihan Liu,Anbang Wu,Han Zhao,Fangxin Liu,Zhezhi He,Yinhe Han,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yiming Gan,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Deltoris是一个面向扩散型VLA模型的算法-硬件协同设计框架，利用时间相似性提出时间感知比特稀疏算法和推测推理技术，并配合定制的1D脉动比特串行PE阵列加速器，实现相比移动GPU最高34.2倍、相比先前加速器6.1倍的速度提升，且保持相当精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散型VLA模型在具身智能中表现出优异的运动质量和泛化能力，但计算量大且需要在高控制频率（50-200 Hz）下运行，对边缘设备的延迟和能耗提出了严格约束，亟需高效的推理方案。

Method: 提出算法-硬件协同设计框架：(1) 时间感知比特稀疏算法，利用连续输入的时间相似性，仅计算连续输入间的差异，消除冗余比特级操作；(2) 推测推理技术，将数据加载分摊到多个控制步骤，缓解算法引入的额外片外流量；(3) 定制加速器，采用定制化1D脉动比特串行PE阵列，消除PE工作负载不平衡。

Result: Deltoris在保持可比精度的前提下，相比移动GPU实现最高34.2倍加速，相比先前加速器实现6.1倍加速。

Conclusion: Deltoris通过算法与硬件的协同优化，有效解决了扩散型VLA模型在边缘设备上推理效率低的问题，兼顾了性能、能耗与精度。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as a key component in embodied AI. Among existing approaches, diffusion-based VLA models achieve superior motion quality and generalization. However, diffusion-based VLA models are compute-intensive and must run at high control frequency, e.g., 50-200 Hz. Thus, it imposes strict latency and energy constraints on edge devices.
  In this work, we present Deltoris, an algorithm-hardware co-design framework for efficient diffusion-based VLA inference. First, we exploit the temporal similarity of consecutive inputs and propose a \textit{temporal-aware bit-sparsity} algorithm that computes only the differences between consecutive inputs, eliminating redundant bit-level operations. To further address the extra off-chip traffic introduced by our algorithm, we propose a \textit{speculative inference} technique, which amortizes data loading across multiple control steps. Lastly, to support these techniques, we co-design a dedicated accelerator with customized 1D systolic bit-serial PE arrays that eliminate PE workload imbalance. Our evaluation shows that Deltoris achieves up to 34.2$\times$ speedup over mobile GPUs and 6.1$\times$ over prior accelerators, while maintaining comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [382] [MCHA: A Memory-Centric Hierarchical Architecture for Parallel-Sequential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04443)
*Daijing Shi,Hongxiao Zhao,Yihan Fu,Zhan Chen,Jiayi Li,Yihang Zhu,Anjunyi Fan,Yaoyu Tao,Yuchao Yang,Bonan Yan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MCHA（Memory-Centric Hierarchical Architecture），一种面向并行-顺序执行模式的可重构硬件架构，通过分层通信策略和事件驱动条件触发编程模型，显著减少主存带宽压力。在MARL等任务上相对NVIDIA A100 GPU实现153.06x至2456.96x的加速，主存访问从96%降至5.44%，在28nm工艺下面积为2.92mm²，功耗为115.36mW@200MHz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴工作负载（如MARL、大规模神经形态计算、概率图模型）呈现并行-顺序计算模式，一方面需要大规模并行以达到高吞吐，另一方面受限于集中式主存访问导致的不可预测数据访问模式，造成全局缓冲区饱和和内存瓶颈。传统架构无法高效执行这些工作负载。

Method: 提出MCHA可重构硬件架构，核心包括：(1) 分层通信策略实现分布式核间数据路由，降低全局内存带宽负担；(2) 新颖的并行-顺序编程模型，利用事件驱动条件触发器在流水线中隐藏数据传输延迟。并提供了开源、周期精确的模拟器进行验证。

Result: 在MARL工作负载上相对NVIDIA A100 GPU实现153.06x至2456.96x的加速；主存访问从96%降低到5.44%；在其他应用域（电机变量控制、Markov随机场）中保持编程灵活性；28nm工艺综合实现面积2.92mm²，200MHz下功耗115.36mW。

Conclusion: MCHA通过内存中心化的分层架构设计，有效解决了并行-顺序工作负载的全局缓冲区饱和和内存瓶颈问题，在大幅提升性能的同时实现了极高的能效比，并且开源了完整实现。

Abstract: Emerging workloads, such as Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), large-scale neuromorphic computing, and probabilistic graphical models, intrinsically exhibit parallel-sequential computing patterns. While these tasks demand massive parallelism to achieve high throughput, they are severely bottlenecked by irregular data access patterns centralized to main memory. Consequently, conventional architectures face fundamental limitations when executing these workloads, primarily manifesting as global buffer saturation and memory-bound bottlenecks. To address these challenges, we propose the Memory-Centric Hierarchical Architecture (MCHA), a reconfigurable hardware solution tailored for parallel-sequential execution. MCHA leverages a hierarchical communication strategy that facilitates distributed, inter-core data routing, thereby significantly reducing the bandwidth burden on the global memory. Complementing the hardware, MCHA introduces a novel parallel-sequential programming model that utilizes event-driven conditional triggers to effectively hide data transmission latency within the execution pipeline. We benchmark MCHA against a diverse suite of parallel-sequential tasks, including MARL, motor variable control, and Markov random fields. Validated through our open-source, cycle-accurate simulator, MCHA demonstrates performance speedups ranging from 153.06$\times$ to 2456.96$\times$ over NVIDIA A100 GPUs on MARL workloads, while maintaining robust programming flexibility across other application domains. Furthermore, the architecture successfully reduces main memory access from 96% to 5.44%. When synthesized in a 28 nm process, the MCHA implementation occupies an area footprint of 2.92mm$^2$ and consumes 115.36 mW of power at 200 MHz. MCHA is open-sourced at https://github.com/carabdis/MCHA.

</details>


### [383] [A Systolic Array Architecture for Nonlinear Activation Functions and Softmax Computation using Chebyshev Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04734)
*Benedikt Schaible,Anirudh Suresh Bharadwaj,Ulf Schlichtmann,Jiang Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于脉动阵列的激活单元架构，能够同时支持多种单变量激活函数（如tanh）和softmax多变量函数，通过共享硬件资源提高了利用率。采用切比雪夫多项式近似，与CORDIC基线相比，tanh的平均绝对误差降低71%，面积减少4.6%，功耗降低5.1%；softmax的KL散度相比CORDIC和分段线性近似分别降低44.6%和79.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络加速器相比CPU平台具有更高效率，但现有加速器通常为单变量激活函数（如tanh）和多变量softmax使用不同的硬件单元，导致资源无法共享，造成硬件资源浪费。作者旨在解决这一问题，通过统一架构实现多种激活函数的资源复用。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于脉动阵列（systolic array）的激活单元架构，统一支持多种单变量激活函数以及softmax多变量函数。使用切比雪夫多项式（Chebyshev polynomial）近似来高效计算激活函数，实现硬件资源的共享与复用。

Result: 与CORDIC基线相比：tanh的平均绝对误差降低71%，面积减少4.6%，功耗降低5.1%；softmax的KL散度相比CORDIC降低44.6%，相比分段线性近似降低79.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的脉动阵列激活单元架构通过Chebyshev多项式近似，在提高tanh和softmax计算精度的同时减少了硬件面积和功耗，证明了统一激活单元架构的可行性和优越性，为神经网络加速器的资源优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Neural Network Accelerators have gained popularity in recent years due to their greater efficiency than CPU-based platforms. Often, these accelerators utilize different hardware units for univariate activation functions, such as tanh, and the multivariate softmax, thereby missing opportunities for resource sharing between them. In this paper, we describe a novel systolic array-based activation unit architecture that supports multiple univariate activation functions as well as the softmax function. By utilizing Chebyshev polynomial approximations, our activation function unit achieves up to 71% lower mean absolute error for tanh compared to a CORDIC baseline, while using 4.6% less area and 5.1% less power. Our softmax approximation enables a 44.6% and 79.0% lower KL divergence compared to CORDIC and a piecewise-linear approximation, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [384] [Kitchen Robotic Manipulation utilizing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04042)
*Myung-Hwan Jeon,Sankalp Yamsani,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于家庭厨房餐具处理任务的模块化感知流水线，集成开放词汇目标检测、多视图分割、实例感知3D重建和2D-3D特征融合策略，实现6D位姿估计与抓取规划。系统在20场景厨房基准上最优配置（LLMDet+SAMv2+DINOv2+GeoTransformer）达到89.12%的ADI，并已在真实机器人上成功部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类日常环境中部署机器人需要既鲁棒又能适应多样动态条件的感知系统，而厨房餐具处理等家庭操作任务对感知的准确性、适应性要求很高。现有单一感知方案难以应对复杂遮挡与杂乱环境，需要构建一个模块化、可替换组件从而找到最优配置的感知流水线。

Method: 提出模块化感知流水线，包含四个核心模块：开放词汇目标检测、多视图分割、实例感知3D重建、以及2D-3D特征融合用于6D位姿估计和抓取规划。每个模块可系统性地替换多种视觉与几何基础模型，通过在自建厨房数据集上大量评估，识别出最佳配置。最优配置为LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer的组合。

Result: 在包含杂乱和遮挡条件的20场景厨房基准上，最优配置达到89.12%的ADI（平均距离度量）。真实机器人实验验证该配置无需环境特定重训练即可部署，成功完成水槽到洗碗机的餐具转移和杯子堆叠等任务，验证了流水线的适应性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该模块化感知流水线具有高度的适应性、可扩展性和实际部署价值，可作为家庭机器人系统的实用框架。代码和补充材料已公开，支持社区复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: Deploying robots in everyday human environments requires perception systems that are both robust and adaptable to diverse, dynamic conditions. In this work, we present a modular perception pipeline for household manipulation tasks, with a focus on dishware handling in kitchen environments. The pipeline integrates open-vocabulary object detection, multi-view segmentation, instance-aware 3D reconstruction, and a 2D-3D feature fusion strategy for 6D pose estimation and grasp planning. Its modular design enables systematic substitution of multiple visual and geometric foundation models, allowing us to identify the best-performing configuration through extensive evaluation on a custom kitchen dataset. The best-performing configuration (LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer) achieves an ADI of 89.12\% on the 20-scene kitchen benchmark with cluttered and occluded conditions. Furthermore, real-world demonstrations confirm that the best configuration can be deployed on physical robots without environment-specific retraining, successfully executing tasks such as sink-to-dishwasher transfer and cup stacking. It validates the adaptability and scalability of the pipeline and highlights its potential as a practical framework for household robotic systems. Our code and supplementary materials are available at https://raivlab.github.io/FM_kitchen .

</details>


### [385] [Interpretable Fuzzy Inference for UAV Target Tracking Using Bounding-Box Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04121)
*Reza Ahmari,Ahmad Mohammadi,Vahid Hemmati,Nicholas Edmond,Hossein Z. Saghazadeh,Olusola Odeyomi,Parham Kebria,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的模糊推理框架，基于YOLO检测框的低维特征（质心位置、面积、宽高比）为UAV提供连续偏航指令以引导其朝向UGV，无需几何建模，在6,169个VICON标注样本上达到0.140°的MAE，具备透明性、数据高效性和轻量化计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习和几何重建的UAV视觉引导方法需要大规模数据集、外部定位或复杂建模假设，计算负担重且缺乏可解释性，难以部署在资源受限平台上，因而需要一种透明、数据高效且计算轻量的连续偏航估计方法。

Method: 先从YOLO检测框提取三个低维特征（目标质心位置、面积、宽高比），构建Mamdani模糊系统（肩-三角-肩输入划分）作为可解释基线，随后引入一阶Takagi-Sugeno模型，每个输入含三个前件隶属项，参数由训练集分位数导出，形成紧凑的27条规则结构，无需显式几何建模。

Result: 在6,169个VICON标注样本上、5次随机训练测试划分中，Takagi-Sugeno模型的MAE为0.140°±0.003°，RMSE为0.200°±0.008°，最大绝对误差为1.254°±0.121°；±1°阈值内准确率99.676%，±3°和±5°内均为100%；图像平面水平位移与预测偏航符号方向一致性达90.254%±0.612%。

Conclusion: 该框架在透明性、数据效率、计算轻量性和实时性方面均表现优异，适合资源受限平台上的UAV视觉引导移动地面目标的应用场景。

Abstract: Vision-based guidance of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) toward unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) supports cooperative aerial--ground robotics, but reliable continuous yaw estimation from onboard vision remains challenging because of sensing uncertainty, limited computation, and the need for interpretable control. Existing deep-learning and geometric-reconstruction approaches often require large datasets, external localization, or complex modeling assumptions, reducing transparency and deployment suitability on resource-constrained platforms. We present an interpretable fuzzy-inference framework that generates continuous yaw commands from low-dimensional features extracted from YOLO boxes: target centroid location, area, and aspect ratio. No explicit geometric modeling is required. A Mamdani fuzzy system serves as an interpretable baseline using a shoulder--triangle--shoulder input partition. It is followed by a first-order Takagi--Sugeno model with three antecedent membership terms per input, whose parameters are derived from training-set quantiles, yielding a compact 27-rule structure. Evaluation uses 6{,}169 labeled samples from a VICON motion-capture environment. Across five randomized train--test splits, the Takagi--Sugeno model achieves a test-set mean absolute error of $0.140^\circ \pm 0.003^\circ$, a root mean squared error of $0.200^\circ \pm 0.008^\circ$, and a maximum absolute error of $1.254^\circ \pm 0.121^\circ$. Within-threshold accuracies are $99.676% \pm 0.270%$ for $\pm1^\circ$ and $100.000% \pm 0.000%$ for both $\pm3^\circ$ and $\pm5^\circ$. Directional consistency between image-plane horizontal displacement and predicted yaw sign reaches $90.254% \pm 0.612%$. These results show that the framework is transparent, data-efficient, computationally lightweight, and suitable for real-time vision-based UAV guidance toward mobile ground targets.

</details>


### [386] [SiMDex: Mining Similar Egocentric Videos for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04196)
*Nie Lin,Takehiko Ohkawa,Sijin Chen,Ruoshi Wen,Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Zhengming Zhu,Yiming Bao,Yunfei Li,Minjie Cai,Xiao Ma,Wei Xu,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SiMDex是一个基于相似度的人体数据挖掘框架，通过将VLA后训练中的人体数据筛选转化为推荐问题，使用三层"召回-排序-重排"流水线，从约3200万条以自我为中心的（egocentric）人体视频样本中提取任务相关子集（约149万条，不到5%），在无需修改VLA架构或训练方式的形态无关动作空间中操作，将灵巧操作成功率从47.7%提升至61.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来以自我为中心的人体视频规模在机器人操作领域爆炸式增长，但哪些数据真正对灵巧操作有益尚不明确，盲目混合数据效果有限，需要系统化的数据筛选方法。

Method: SiMDex将人体数据选择视为推荐问题，采用三层"召回-排序-重排"流水线：从约3200万条样本池中为每条机器人示范提取任务相关子集，在形态无关（morphology-agnostic）动作空间中操作，无需改动VLA架构或训练流程。

Result: 与使用等量随机采样人体数据的强基线相比，SiMDex仅使用约149万条（不到数据池的5%）挖掘样本，将整体成功率从47.7%提升至61.1%，证明选择性筛选优于无差别数据混合。

Conclusion: 选择性数据筛选远比盲目混合数据更有效，SiMDex在极小数据量下显著超越强基线，验证了相似度驱动的数据挖掘在灵巧操作VLA后训练中的价值。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an explosive trend of scaling ego-centric human videos for robot manipulation, yet it remains unclear which data actually benefits dexterous manipulation. We present SiMDex, a similarity-based data mining framework that casts human data selection for VLA post-training in dexterous manipulation as a recommendation problem. For each robot demonstration, SiMDex employs a three-layer recall-ranking-re-ranking pipeline to extract task-relevant subsets from a pool of ~32M egocentric human samples, operating in a morphology-agnostic action space that requires no changes to VLA architecture or training. Against a strong baseline trained with an equal amount of randomly sampled human data, SiMDex uses only ~1.49M mined samples (<5% of the pool) yet improves the overall success rate from 47.7% to 61.1%, showing that selective curation outperforms indiscriminate data mixing.

</details>


### [387] [Feasibility of Embedded Photoplethysmography Sensing in Short-Duration Tactile Interactions With Pocket-Sized Robots Using IMU- and Confidence-Based Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04242)
*Turjja Datta,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出在口袋大小的陪伴机器人AffectaPocket中嵌入光电容积脉搏波(PPG)传感器，实现触觉交互过程中自主心率监测，并通过基于IMU的两阶段滤波管道来消除手持使用时的运动伪影，实验表明该方法与商用腕戴式传感器在统计上等效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陪伴机器人在儿童即时焦虑缓解方面具有潜力，但其有效性依赖于连续、无侵入的生理状态监测。现有方案多依赖外部可穿戴设备，这带来可用性障碍并限制机器人自主性，因此需要将生理监测直接集成到机器人本体中。

Method: 将PPG传感器嵌入口袋大小的陪伴机器人AffectaPocket中；设计两阶段滤波管道：第一阶段利用机载惯性测量单元(IMU)拒绝高方差运动段，第二阶段采用基于置信度的平滑算法处理恢复期数据；在内实验中以26名被试进行组内设计，与常用腕戴式传感器对比验证。

Result: 滤波策略显著降低了平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)，并与地面真值测量达到统计学等效(p<0.05)。但短时交互分析表明传感器需要较长稳定期才能收敛。

Conclusion: 嵌入机器人本体的PPG传感器结合运动伪影滤波管道可实现可靠的自主体征监测，为陪伴机器人在儿童焦虑缓解场景中提供了无需外部可穿戴设备的技术路径，但需注意传感器收敛时间对短时交互的限制。

Abstract: Ubiquitous companion robots offer a promising avenue for immediate anxiety relief in children, yet their effectiveness relies on the ability to monitor physiological states continuously and unobtrusively. Current solutions often depend on external wearables, which impose usability barriers and limit the robot's autonomy. This paper investigates the integration of an embedded photoplethysmography (PPG) sensor directly into a pocket-sized companion robot, AffectaPocket, to enable self-contained heart rate monitoring during tactile interaction. We address the significant challenge of motion artifacts inherent in handheld usage by implementing a two-stage filtering pipeline that utilizes an onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) to reject high-variance segments and a confidence-based smoothing algorithm for recovery periods. We evaluated the system against a commonly used wrist worn sensor in a Within-Subjects Study with 26 participants. Our results demonstrate that the filtering strategy significantly reduced the Mean Absolute Percentage Error and achieved statistical equivalence to the ground truth measurements (p<0.05). Analysis of short-duration interactions shows that the sensor requires stability over longer periods to converge.

</details>


### [388] [SAFECAST: Robust Failure Detection for VLA Policies with Contrast-Set Training and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04246)
*Harshitha Rajaprakash,Aditeya Prajapati,Rong Xue,Abrar Anwar,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFECAST是一种利用对比集扰动改进视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时分布偏移下失败检测的方法，通过提升隐藏状态风险探针的训练与校准，在DROID真实世界和LIBERO模拟实验中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时会面临多种分布偏移（如杂乱场景、干扰物、光照变化、新物体、初始状态改变和指令改写），导致策略失败。现有的基于隐藏状态的风险探针结合功能性共形预测可以检测滚动执行失败，但其可靠性依赖于校准数据与部署条件匹配。当部署条件偏移时，校准数据与实际分布不匹配，失败检测的可靠性下降。

Method: SAFECAST引入对比集扰动（contrast set perturbations）来改进隐藏状态探针的训练和校准过程，使其更好地适应部署时分布偏移。具体而言，通过视觉和语言层面的对比集扰动增强数据，提升探针对分布偏移的鲁棒性。在训练和校准阶段同时利用对比集扰动，并在模拟到真实的校准场景下检验其效果。

Result: SAFECAST在DROID真实世界实验和LIBERO模拟实验中，跨多个VLM骨干网络，在失败检测ROC-AUC指标上相对于最先进基线获得了统计显著的提升。此外，同时使用视觉和语言对比集扰动数据增强时效果最佳；使用对比集扰动时，模拟到真实的校准比仅使用真实滚动数据训练出更好的探针。

Conclusion: 对比集扰动是提升部署时分布偏移下失败检测性能的有效策略，视觉与语言层面联合扰动效果最佳，且该方法在模拟到真实的迁移场景中同样有效。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often fail under deployment-time distribution shifts such as clutter, distractor objects, lighting changes, novel objects, altered initial states, and reworded instructions. Hidden-state-based risk probes combined with functional conformal prediction can detect rollout failures, but their reliability depends on calibration data matching deployment conditions. We introduce SAFECAST, which leverages contrast set perturbations to improve hidden-state probe training and calibration for deployment time shift. SAFECAST statistically significantly improves failure detection ROC-AUC scores over a state of the art baseline in both real-world DROID and LIBERO simulation experiments across multiple VLM backbones. We further find that SAFECAST benefits most when both visual and language contrast set perturbations are used to augment data, and that with contrast set perturbations, sim-to-real calibration leads to better probes than using real rollout data only.

</details>


### [389] [Structured LLM Reasoning for Zero-Shot Human--Robot Coordination Under Hidden Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04309)
*Dong Hae Mangalindan,Anand Gokhale,Francesco Bullo,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的结构化架构，用于零样本人机协作任务（合作建造，私有目标视图），通过Dec-POMDP框架将决策分解为五步（ToM推理、分层规划、对话理解、动作验证、反馈重规划），在人类参与者实验中相较无ToM消融模型和离线多智能体强化学习基线，交互步骤更少且信任评分更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有私有目标视图的合作建造任务中，人机零样本协调充满挑战——机器人需要推断人类目标、理解对话、进行分层规划。传统多智能体强化学习需要大量离线训练且泛化有限，而单独使用LLM缺乏物理可行性保障。作者希望通过系统化分解团队决策问题，利用LLM作为不可解计算（推理与规划）的可行代理，同时保留传统验证机制来保证物理可行性。

Method: 以Dec-POMDP为理论指导，构建结构化LLM架构，包含五个模块：(i) 基于动作条件的心理理论（ToM）推理——根据人类动作推断其私有目标；(ii) 分层规划——将高层目标分解为可执行子任务；(iii) 对话理解——解析自然语言交互；(iv) 动作验证——用传统方法验证物理可行性；(v) 反馈驱动的重规划。对比基线包括无ToM模块的消融版本以及离线多智能体强化学习策略。

Result: 在人类参与者实验中，所提方法相较两个基线（无ToM消融版和多智能体RL策略）需要更少的交互步骤，并在交互后获得更高的信任评分，验证了结构化分解策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 系统化分解团队决策问题、用LLM充当传统上不可解推理/规划计算的可行代理，并结合传统验证机制保障物理可行性，能够在零样本条件下同时提升任务协调效率与人类协作体验。这为LLM在具身人机协作系统中的应用提供了设计范式。

Abstract: We present a structured large-language-model (LLM) architecture for zero-shot human--robot coordination in a cooperative construction task with private goal views. Guided by a Dec-POMDP formulation, the architecture decomposes decision-making into (i) action-conditioned Theory-of-Mind (ToM) inference, (ii) hierarchical planning, (iii) conversation interpretation, (iv) action verification, and (v) feedback-based replanning. We compare the proposed method with an ablation without ToM inference and a multi-agent reinforcement-learning policy trained offline over many goal pairs. In human-participant experiments, the proposed method required fewer interaction steps and yielded higher post-interaction trust ratings than both baselines. These results suggest that systematically decomposing the team decision problem, using LLMs as tractable surrogates for otherwise intractable inference and planning computations, and retaining conventional verification for physical feasibility can improve both task coordination and the human experience.

</details>


### [390] [Design and Flight of an Ion-propelled Micro Hovercraft Leveraging Ground Proximity Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04343)
*C. Luke Nelson,Grant Nations,Mrinmoy Modak,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种利用电空气动力学（EAD）地面效应推进的厘米级悬浮飞行器，通过优化被动裙边几何构型，实现了16 mN/W的推力效率和约1.5克的额外有效载荷能力，比现有同类电推进机器人高一个数量级，首次在公开文献中展示离子推进微型悬浮飞行器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电空气动力学推进因其静音和固态特性，在微型飞行器领域极具吸引力，但此前效率不足，无法实现功率自主飞行。近期研究发现小尺度大气离子推进器在地面附近运行时，推力密度和效率可大幅提升，本文旨在探索利用这种地面效应实现低空飞行的厘米级悬浮飞行器设计空间。

Method: 首先对不同几何构型和配置的被动悬浮裙边进行经验性实验研究，表征其性能优势和权衡；基于实验结果制作可行的点设计方案，制造出掌心大小的悬浮飞行器，并对其飞行持久性、起降循环、被动稳定性抗扰能力和噪声水平进行系统测试。

Result: 该悬浮飞行器在系留外部电源下可持续飞行，耐受数十次起降循环，被动稳定抵抗显著机械扰动，几乎零可闻噪声。实测推力效率为16 mN/W，额外有效载荷约1.5克（车辆自重约1.6克），比同类电空气动力学推进机器人高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 这是公开文献中首次展示离子推进微型悬浮飞行器，证明了利用地面效应实现高效率和有效载荷能力突破的可行性，为全新一类机器人指明了发展方向。

Abstract: Electroaerodynamic propulsion is compelling for use in micro air vehicles due to its silent and solid-state nature, but its limited efficiency has thus far precluded a path towards power-autonomous flight. Recent work has shown that thrust density and efficiency for small-scale atmospheric ion thrusters can be vastly increased when operating close to a ground plane. Here, we explore the design space of centimeter-scale hovercraft, which can leverage this ground effect for low-altitude flight. We first perform an empirical investigation, characterizing the performance benefits and trade-offs for different geometries and configurations of passive hovercraft skirts, then use the results to fabricate a viable point design. We demonstrate a palm-sized hovercraft that, while tethered to an external power source, can fly for extended periods, withstand dozens of takeoff and landing cycles, passively stabilize to reject significant mechanical disturbances, and generate practically zero audible noise signature. The measured thrust efficiency of 16 mN/W and additional payload capacity of almost 1.5 grams above the vehicle's self mass of about 1.6 grams exceeds any similarly sized electroaerodynamically propelled robot by an order of magnitude. This is the first time an ion-propelled micro hovercraft has been shown in the open literature, and our work points the way towards an entirely new class of robot.

</details>


### [391] [Approximate Multi-Objective Search Under Rulebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04398)
*Omar Muhammetkulyyev,Oren Salzman,Tichakorn Wongpiromsarn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RA*pex算法，通过引入ε-规则支配（epsilon-rule-dominance）概念，高效计算近似规则簿最优解的紧凑集合，实现比现有方法快两个数量级的计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人规划常涉及具有复杂优先级关系的多目标优化（如安全、效率、合规性），规则簿（rulebooks）通过偏序关系形式化这些目标之间的关系，但计算完整的规则簿最优解集合计算成本极高，亟需高效的近似求解方法。

Method: 提出ε-规则支配的近似支配概念，设计RA*pex最佳优先搜索算法。算法利用维度约简加速多目标搜索，同时通过维护分离的封闭集合（closed sets）并在截断规则集和残差规则集上进行支配检查来保持规则层级结构。

Result: 形式化证明了每个规则簿最优解都被返回集合中至少一个解ε-规则支配（即所提出的近似支配概念的推广）；实验结果证明该方法比现有方法快两个数量级以上（超过100倍）。

Conclusion: RA*pex在保证近似最优性保证的前提下，大幅降低了规则簿多目标规划的计算开销，为大规模机器人规划问题提供了实用高效的求解方案。

Abstract: Robotic planning often involves multiple objectives with complex priority relationships, such as safety, efficiency, and regulatory compliance. Rulebooks formalize these relationships, allowing partial ordering of objectives that generalizes both Pareto and lexicographic dominance. Computing the full set of rulebook-optimal solutions, however, is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce the concept of epsilon-rule-dominance, a principled notion of approximate dominance under rulebooks, and propose RA*pex, a best-first search algorithm that efficiently computes a compact set of epsilon-approximate rulebook-optimal solutions. RA*pex leverages dimensionality reduction, a technique used to speed up existing multi-objective search algorithms, while respecting rule hierarchies by maintaining separate closed sets and performing dominance checks over truncated and residual rule sets. We provide a formal analysis of RA*pex, proving that every rulebook-optimal solution is epsilon-rule-dominated (a generalization of approximate dominance we introduce) by at least one solution in the returned set. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach achieves computation times over two orders of magnitude faster than existing methods.

</details>


### [392] [SCOPE: Field-of-View-Aware Path Planning in Unknown 3D Environments via Safety-Volume Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04420)
*Junbin Yuan,Muqing Cao,Yunwoo Lee,Brady Moon,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE框架，在未知体素地图中通过在线安全体积认证实现受限视场传感器的安全导航，结合乐观引导与认证执行，证明条件完备规划性能并在仿真与实物中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用安装在机体上的受限视场传感器进行安全导航时，必须确保每一步运动所占据的膨胀体积在未知环境中已被充分观测并验证为无障碍。现有方法缺乏对此"机器人膨胀体积认证"的系统性表示与规划框架。

Method: 将安全导航需求形式化为在线安全体积认证问题，构建认证图（顶点对应安全体积完全已知为自由的位姿）。SCOPE框架将乐观目标导向规划与认证执行解耦：将乐观路径上的首个未认证点转化为显式观测义务，通过以目标为中心的视点搜索解决该义务，并在有用视点尚未认证可达时递归清除中间义务。此外引入认证预览机制与感知感知的轨迹优化后端。

Result: 在三个未知3D环境的60个随机任务中，SCOPE全部到达目标且几乎不进入未认证膨胀空间；预览机制将平均任务时间降低27%；两个代表性场景的实物机器人演示验证了整个系统的可行性。

Conclusion: SCOPE实现了条件完备规划：在理想单调感知和穷举有限域图搜索条件下，只要规划原语中存在有限的认证感知动作可行序列，SCOPE即能到达目标，为受限视场安全导航提供了理论保证与工程可用的完整系统。

Abstract: Safe navigation with a body-mounted limited-field-of-view sensor requires the complete robot-inflated volume of an intended motion to be observed and verified free before execution. We formulate this requirement as online safety-volume certification in an unknown voxel map and construct a certified graph whose vertices correspond exactly to positions with fully known-free safety volumes. Based on this representation, we propose SCOPE (Safety Certification through Observation Planning and Execution), a planning framework that decouples optimistic goal-directed guidance from certified execution. SCOPE converts the first uncertified point along an optimistic route into an explicit observation obligation, resolves it through target-centric viewpoint search, and recursively clears intermediate obligations when useful viewpoints are not yet certified-reachable. A certified preview mechanism and an observation-aware trajectory optimization backend enable smooth execution. We prove conditional complete planning: under ideal monotone sensing and exhaustive finite-domain graph search, SCOPE reaches the goal whenever a finite feasible sequence of certified sensing actions exists within its planning primitives. Across 60 randomized tasks in three unknown 3D environments, SCOPE reaches every goal while maintaining near-zero entry into non-certified inflated space. Preview reduces mean mission time by 27%, and real-robot demonstrations in two representative scenarios validate the complete system.

</details>


### [393] [SSC: A Verifiable Structured Representation for Bimanual Manipulation Labelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04425)
*Yupu Lu,Shuang Wu,Sihan Chen,Ruihua Han,Yichen Zhang,Marcus Kalander,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结构化子任务链（SSC），一种基于状态转移的演示表征格式，平衡自然语言灵活性与模板过度分割问题，并支持视觉-语言辅助的验证与补全功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子任务标签用于将长时程操作演示分解为语义片段，但自然语言描述存在语言变异性导致自动验证困难，而BEHAVIOR-1K的刚性模板格式又存在过度分割问题，影响可读性和标注一致性。需要在两者之间找到平衡点。

Method: 提出结构化子任务模板（SST）作为核心表征，存储主体、谓词、宾语等核心动作组件，以及状语修饰（空间/工具短语）、独立于手臂动作的基座运动字段和后继状态场景图。在此基础上构建SSC，支持三种视觉-语言辅助功能：将SST渲染为自然语言、依据四条状态转移规则检查链的组装、通过查询解析级联补全未指定的字段。在BEHAVIOR-1K上实例化流程，选13个SOTA视觉-语言模型作为候选验证器。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K上进行评估（50个任务、每任务3个episode、共2,357个标注动作单元），执行逻辑验证与内容补全，评估了13个先进视觉-语言模型的验证能力，并报告了标注异常。

Conclusion: SSC通过结构化状态转移表征在自然语言的灵活性与模板格式的严格性之间取得平衡，并借助视觉-语言模型实现有效的逻辑验证与字段补全，为长时程操作演示的子任务标注提供了更一致、可验证的框架。

Abstract: Subtask labels decompose a long-horizon manipulation demonstration into shorter semantic segments for policy training and evaluation. Natural language descriptions are easy to read, but their linguistic variability makes automatic verification difficult. Rigid template formats, such as BEHAVIOR-1K's skill_annotation, are linguistically over-segmented, hindering both readability and annotation consistency. We propose the Structured Subtask Chain (SSC), a state-transition representation that bridges these extremes. A demonstration is a sequence of Structured Subtask Template (SST) entries. Each SST stores core action components (subject, predicate, object), flexible conditions (adverbial modifiers such as spatial or instrumental phrases), a base-motion field separate from arm actions, and an after-state scene graph. Built on this format, SSC supports three vision-language assisted functions: rendering SSTs as natural language, checking the assembled chain against four state-transition rules, and completing underspecified fields through a query resolution cascade. We instantiate the pipeline on BEHAVIOR-1K (50 tasks, 3 episodes per task, 2,357 annotated action cells) for logic verification and content completion, evaluating 13 selected state-of-the-art VL models as candidate verifiers and reporting labelling anomalies.

</details>


### [394] [GUARD: Grounding Uncertainty and Ablation-Based Risk Detection for Diffusion-Based VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04510)
*Suhas Hegde,Jitendra Yasaswi Bharadwaj Katta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GUARD是一种测试时故障检测方法，通过测量扩散式视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略对多模态证据的依赖程度来检测其预测是否被弱地锚定在视觉和语言条件上，无需修改预训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式VLA策略即使其预测与视觉、语言证据的耦合很弱，也能生成看似合理的动作，这会造成安全隐患。已有方法缺乏在测试时检测这种弱锚定（弱grounding）的手段。

Method: GUARD通过以下步骤实现：(1) 估计最终视觉-语言模型KV缓存中按token索引条目的影响；(2) 通过消融显著KV条目构建反事实缓存；(3) 比较反事实去噪响应与原始条件化；(4) 由此推导出诊断流，包括敏感性、注意力熵、模态偏差和锚定效率；(5) 在线校准这些信号并通过轻量级时序分类器处理。

Result: 在五种策略基准设置中，使用Pi0、SmolVLA和Alpamayo-1.5在LIBERO、SimplerEnv、MetaWorld和PhysicalAI-AV上进行评估。GUARD在五个未见任务设置中的四个取得最佳ROC-AUC，在剩余一个设置中排名第二，未见任务平均ROC-AUC比最强竞争运行时监控器提升5.73个百分点，同时与最佳已见任务平均差距仅0.19个百分点。

Conclusion: 直接探测动作头对多模态证据的依赖提供了一种跨策略、任务、实体和领域的可迁移故障信号，表明GUARD方法具有良好的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies can generate plausible actions even when their predictions are weakly grounded in the visual and language evidence defining the task. We introduce GUARD, a test-time failure detection method that measures this grounding without modifying the pretrained policy. GUARD estimates the influence of token-indexed entries in the final vision-language model key-value (KV) cache, constructs counterfactual caches by ablating salient KV entries, and compares their denoising responses with the original conditioning. Based on the comparison, we derive GUARD diagnostic stream including sensitivity, attention entropy, modality bias, and grounding efficiency, which are calibrated online and processed by a lightweight temporal classifier. We evaluate GUARD under task-held-out splits across five policy-benchmark settings, using Pi0, SmolVLA, and Alpamayo-1.5 on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, MetaWorld, and PhysicalAI-AV. GUARD achieves the best ROC-AUC on four of five unseen-task settings and ranks second on the remaining setting, improving the average unseen-task ROC-AUC by 5.73 percentage points over the strongest competing runtime monitor while remaining within 0.19 points of the best seen-task average. These results show that directly probing action-head dependence on multimodal evidence provides a transferable failure signal across policies, tasks, embodiments, and domains.

</details>


### [395] [Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04527)
*Yuze Fan,Yue Cao,Pengjie Gao,Haojia Gao,Guangqiu Guo,Ziyue Zhang,Junbo Tan,Bokui Chen,Zhuo Zou,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RTCF，一种无需训练的测试时校正框架，通过渐进记忆对齐（PMA）因果对齐视觉执行历史与完整成功轨迹，并以系数裁剪的低频残差在运动通道上校正冻结策略的动作块，在不更新参数、不重复推理、不额外消耗GPU的情况下将LIBERO基准的聚合成功率从86.4%提升至88.4%，LIBERO-Long从61.6%提升至68.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作任务中，冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然能生成时间扩展的动作块，但累积执行误差和跨任务阶段的视觉混叠会导致长期操作脆弱。现有帧检索方法可能返回与任务进度不对齐的动作，而直接重放或时域融合又会覆盖策略提案的反应式结构。

Method: RTCF将“检索哪些经验”与“传递动作的哪部分”分离。渐进记忆对齐（PMA）通过增量更新的单调前沿，将不断增长的视觉执行历史与完整成功轨迹进行因果对齐，无需阶段标签即可联合识别相关记忆和当前对齐位置。从对齐的动作块中，仅迁移运动通道上经过系数裁剪的低频残差，而高频成分和夹爪决策仍继承自冻结策略。

Result: 在四个LIBERO数据集及每个条件下2000个episodes的实验中，RTCF将聚合成功率从86.4%提升至88.4%，其中LIBERO-Long从61.6%提升至68.6%。总延迟仅10.99ms/动作块，校正可在客户端CPU上于单次策略调用后完成，无需更新参数、重复VLA推理或额外GPU资源。

Conclusion: RTCF证明了通过解耦“检索内容”与“迁移内容”并在频域中选择性传递低频运动残差，可以在极低的模型侧开销下有效改进冻结VLA策略的长程操作性能，同时保留策略原本的高频响应和夹爪决策结构。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate temporally extended action chunks, but long-horizon manipulation remains vulnerable to accumulated execution error and visual aliasing across task stages. Successful rollouts provide useful corrective evidence, yet current frame retrieval can return progress-misaligned actions,while direct replay or time-domain fusion can overwrite the reactive structure of the policy proposal. We introduce Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency (RTCF), a training-free test-time correction framework that improves frozen VLA performance with low model-side overhead.RTCF separates which experience to retrieve from which part of its action to transfer. Progressive Memory Alignment (PMA) causally aligns the growing visual execution history with complete successful trajectories through incrementally updated monotonic frontiers, jointly identifying a relevant memory and the current aligned memory position without stage labels. From the aligned action chunk,RTCF transfers a coefficient-wise-clipped low-frequency residual on motion channels. Higher-frequency components and gripper decisions remain inherited from the frozen policy. Across four LIBERO suites and 2,000 episodes per condition, RTCF raises aggregate success from 86.4% to 88.4% and improves LIBERO-Long from 61.6% to 68.6%.These gains require no parameter updates, repeated VLA inference, or additional GPU resources: correction can be performed on the client CPU after a single policy invocation, and the median latencies sum to only 10.99 ms per action chunk

</details>


### [396] [Static Timing Orchestration for Tree-Structured Robot Control Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04600)
*Wang Xi,Feiran Wei,Mo Deng,Weiheng Lin,Pangkit Fong,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FineMote，一个面向树状结构设备模型的机器人控制固件生成框架，通过编译期静态调度机制确定执行顺序，在保证期限和前置约束的前提下最小化运行时开销，实验证明其改善了时序行为和运行时响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人系统日益复杂，从结构化描述文件生成控制固件成为降低开发复杂度、提高可维护性的有效范式。然而机器人描述格式的树状层级结构引入了结构化数据依赖，影响感知到决策的延迟，进而制约控制性能。因此需要一种针对树状设备模型的调度机制来优化执行顺序并降低延迟。

Method: FineMote框架将异构的低层控制逻辑对象化，暴露统一的调度单元和执行入口。基于生成的对象层级，调度机制利用编译期信息静态确定执行顺序，从而最小化运行时开销。作者还证明了该机制满足期限和前置约束，并推导了树内决策延迟的上界。

Result: 在真实机器人控制平台上实现并评估了FineMote框架，实验结果表明其在时序行为和运行时响应性方面均有改善，验证了所提设计的实际有效性。

Conclusion: FineMote通过编译期静态调度有效地解决了树状机器人设备模型中的数据依赖和延迟问题，在不增加运行时开销的情况下满足了期限和前置约束，为复杂机器人系统的控制固件生成提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: As robotic systems become increasingly complex, generating control firmware from structural description files has emerged as a promising paradigm for reducing development complexity and improving maintainability. Existing robot description formats naturally represent robotic systems as hierarchical tree structures, where devices are recursively composed into functional subsystems and eventually into the complete robot. However, such tree-structured organization also introduces structured data dependencies that affect perception-to-decision latency and, consequently, control performance.
  In this paper, we propose FineMote, a control firmware generation framework with a scheduling mechanism tailored for tree-structured device models. The framework objectifies heterogeneous low-level control logic and exposes unified scheduling units and execution entry points. Based on the resulting object hierarchy, the scheduling mechanism exploits compile-time information to statically determine execution order with minimal runtime overhead. We prove that the proposed mechanism satisfies deadline and precedence constraints, and further derive an upper bound on intra-tree decision latency. We implement the proposed framework and evaluate it on real robotic control platforms. The experimental results show improved timing behavior and runtime responsiveness, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>


### [397] [GASP: GPU-Accelerated Safe Planner for Real-Time Collision-Aware Motion Generation with Latent Trajectory Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04612)
*Colin Merk,Stefanos Charalambous,Peter Dürr,Farshad Khadivar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GASP，一种GPU加速的安全规划器，用于已知环境中实时、碰撞感知的关节空间运动生成。它结合clamped B样条轨迹参数化与卷积残差神经网络，通过条件变分自编码器采样多条候选轨迹，并在GPU上并行解码验证，实现近毫秒级推理，在机器人乒乓球强化学习重设规划中显著减少训练碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已知环境下进行实时、碰撞感知的关节空间运动生成是机器人领域的关键挑战。传统GPU轨迹优化方法推理时间较长，难以满足实时性要求；而需要一种既能保证碰撞感知安全性，又能实现近毫秒级推理的高效规划方法。

Method: GASP采用两阶段策略：(1) 使用clamped B样条参数化轨迹，通过卷积残差神经网络预测内部自由控制点，并解析插入边界控制点以满足初始和最终导数约束；(2) 利用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）采样多条候选轨迹，在GPU上并行解码和验证，形成批量规划器，实现碰撞感知的耦合关节空间运动规划。

Result: GASP作为在线运动生成模块，达到解析级成功率，具备高碰撞感知可行性，并在推理时间上相较GPU轨迹优化显著降低。在机器人乒乓球强化学习重设规划中，GASP匹配基线回报率的同时，将训练期碰撞减少约一半。

Conclusion: GASP通过GPU并行架构与学习方法的结合，实现了近毫秒级、碰撞感知的实时关节空间运动规划，同时满足安全性与性能要求，并在强化学习训练场景中展现了实际可用性。
